Account settings & billing: Account settings

Can’t sign in to your Otter account

If you can’t sign in to your Otter account, choose the topic that most resembles the issue you are experiencing. Follow the instructions for help getting back into your account. #### You forgot your password For issues signing in with your password, follow these steps to reset the password and gain access to your account. 1. Go to https://otter.ai/forgot-password. 2. Enter your email address and click Send reset link. (image: forgot_password.png) 3. Check your email for a message with a reset password link and click Reset Now. (image: forgot_password_email.png) 4. Follow the onscreen steps to reset your password.

If you don’t receive an email in your inbox, check your junk or spam folder, or add reply@otter.ai and no-reply@otter.ai to your contacts to make sure that Otter emails are successfully delivered to your inbox. #### You forgot the email address you used to sign in 1. Try other email addresses you might have used. 2. Search through your email inbox for ‘Otter.ai’, to see if any emails come up. Use that email to sign in or complete a password reset.

If you don’t remember the email you used, you may have created an account through Google, Microsoft, or Apple. Try using these authentication methods if you remember creating an account using one of those methods: (image: alternative_sign_in_options.png) Note: If your account was not already associated with the methods above, completing this step  will create a new Otter account. #### Otter is not loading or you get an error message 1. Make sure that:

There isn’t a typo in your username or password. 2. You’ve entered your full email address. For example, “username@gmail.com” instead of “username@gmail.” 3. Your keyboard is set to the right language. 4. Make sure zeros “0” and/or the letter “O” were not entered in place of the other. 5. Refresh your browser and try signing in again. 6. Clear your cache, restart your browser, and try signing in again.

Learn how to clear cookies and site data in Chrome, Safari, and Firefox. #### You’re having trouble with two-factor authentication (2FA) Contact Otter Support and CC your admin if you are currently having issues due to two-factor authentication (2FA). The following are some reasons you may have trouble authenticating: - You no longer have access to your authenticator app due to a new phone, deleted app, or restored phone. - You are not able to receive text messages, either due to carrier issues, sim issues, or you are outside the U.S. and Canada region. - You lost your backup codes. - The code is not working.

Read our article on Troubleshooting Two-Factor Authentication (2FA) for more info. Note that two-factor authentication (2FA) using text messages is only available for phone numbers located in the United States (U.S.) and Canada region. #### You can’t reset your password because you can’t access your email If you no longer have access to the email associated with your Otter account, contact Otter Support, and provide the following information to verify your account: - Last 4 digits of the payment card on file - Card expiration date - Amount of the last transaction - Last transaction date - The email address you would like your Otter account to be updated to

With that information, we can verify the account and update your email address for you. Note: We need all 4 pieces of information in order to verify your account. If you were previously part of an employer’s Workspace and used your work email, please contact your former manager or your former employer’s helpdesk, and have them contact Otter Support so that we can remove you from the Workspace and update your email. The admin of the Workspace can remove you from the Workspace as well. #### You get a message that your account is deactivated There are several reasons your account may have been deactivated. Your account may have been deactivated by your admin, your password could have been entered incorrectly too many times, your account may have violated the Terms of Service, or various other reasons. Contact Otter Support for more information and to request reactivation. #### None of the steps above If you cannot find the answer to your question in the Help Center, or cannot log into your Otter account and you have already tried the steps to reset your password, please contact our Otter Support Team using this form. Please be sure to include the email address associated with your Otter account, as without this information we will not be able to locate your account and help resolve your issue in a timely manner. Additionally, your request may not be prioritized correctly if your email is not associated with an Otter account.

Change or reset your password

Did you forget your password or need to change it? Read on for steps to sign back into Otter and change your password. #### Reset your password 1. Go to https://otter.ai/forgot-password. 2. Enter your email address and click Send reset link. 3. Check your inbox for an Otter confirmation email. 4. Click the button in the email. 5. Follow the steps to choose your new password.

Change a password

  1. Sign in to Otter from a web browser.
  2. Click your profile photo or initial in the top left, and then click Account settings.
  3. On the General tab next to Password, click on the field. Enter your current password, new password, then click Change password.

Troubleshooting

Why didn’t I get a password reset email?

Check your spam or junk folder. If emails from Otter aren’t getting delivered to your inbox, ask your company’s IT team to allow emails from no-reply@otter.ai and support@otter.ai. ##### I lost access to the email address on my account For security purposes, you must be able to access the email inbox associated with your Otter account to reset your password. If that’s not possible, ask an owner or admin to update the email address linked to your account. Please note that administrators can’t reset or change passwords. ##### I don’t have a password If you didn’t set a password when you signed up for Otter (e.g., if you sign in with Apple or Google), you can request a password reset to add one to your account.

Change your email address

The email address you use to create an Otter account is where we’ll send password reset emails and other important messages, so you’ll want to keep it up to date. Keep reading to learn how to change your email address at any time. #### Update your Otter account’s email address. Updating your account email is only supported on a mobile or desktop browser and not through the Otter mobile app at this time. Follow the steps below to update your email. 1. Sign in to Otter.ai on a mobile or desktop browser. 2. Click on your profile in the upper left corner. Click Account Settings.  3. On the General tab, click Edit in the profile section.  (image: account settings general.png) 4. In the profile pop-up, click on the Email field. (image: edit your email.png) 5. Type in your new email. Click the blue checkmark when you’re finished. (image: confirm email change.png) 6. In the pop-up, type in your password and click Validate to proceed. (image: validate your email.png) 7. A verification email will be sent to the new email. Click Okay.  (image: email verification sent.png) 8. In the email, click Confirm email address or paste the link into a web browser.  (image: confirm email address.png) 9. The confirmation link will take you to a verification success page and redirect you to your account. 10. You can verify your email change in Account Settings > General. (image: email has been updated.png)

Missing confirmation email

If you didn’t receive the confirmation email, try the following: - Check your spam or junk folder, and any filters in your inbox. - Open your email settings and check for typos in the email address. If the address is incorrect, you can change it by following the steps above.

FAQs

Can I change or update my email on the mobile Otter app?

  • No.
  • Changing or updating your email on the mobile Otter app is not yet supported at this time. We hope to bring this functionality to the mobile app in future updates.
  • You will need to sign in to Otter on a mobile or desktop browser to access Account Settings > General > Profile.
  • Follow the steps above to update your email address.

Change your profile picture

You can choose a photo to set as your Otter profile picture. This image shows up when someone sees your name in their speaker list or when you’ve been tagged within a conversation. #### Using a web browser 1. Sign in to Otter from a web browser. 2. Click your profile photo or initial in the top left, and then click Account settings. 3. Click your current profile picture and follow the on-screen prompts to choose or take a photo. 4. Click Save.

Using the mobile app

  1. Open the Otter mobile app.
  2. Tap (image: menu-black-24dp.svg) and tap (image: settings-black-24dp.svg) Settings.
  3. Tap your current profile picture.
  4. Follow the on-screen prompts to choose or take a photo.
  5. Tap Done.

Contact Otter.ai Support

If you can’t find the answer in the Help Center, you can contact our Otter Support team for assistance with your account, subscription, billing, workspace, or technical issues. This article explains how to submit a support request, what information to include, and what to expect after contacting our support team to help resolve your issue as quickly as possible. #### Submitting a support request on desktop The easiest way to contact our Otter Support team is by submitting a support request. To help us prioritize your request and provide the fastest assistance, we recommend submitting your ticket while signed in to your Otter account and following the steps below. 1. While signed in, click your profile in the upper-left corner, then select** ****Contact Support. 2. On the support request form, use the email address associated with your Otter account whenever possible. This helps us quickly locate your account and provide faster support. When completing the form, include as much detail as possible about your issue or question. The more information you provide, the faster our Support team can investigate and help resolve your request. 3. When you’ve completed the form, click Submit** to send your request to our Otter Support team.

Submitting a support request on mobile

You can submit a support request on the iOS or Android Otter app. 1. In the Otter mobile app, tap Account in the bottom-right corner, then select Help Center. 2. Use the email associated with your Otter account to help us locate your account faster. Include as much detail as possible in your request. 3. When you’ve completed the form, click Submit to send your request to our Otter Support team.

I can’t log in to my Otter account You can still reach out to us directly if you can’t sign in to your Otter account

If you can’t sign in to your Otter account and have already tried resetting your password, contact our Otter Support team by clicking the link below. Include as much detail as possible about the issue, and use an email address you can access so we can respond. In the form, enter the email address associated with the Otter account you’re unable to access. This helps us quickly locate your account and resolve your request more efficiently. #### How can I resolve my issue without contacting Otter Support? Check out our Help Center and try out Otter AI Chat to resolve your issue Before contacting the Otter Support team, we recommend searching the Help Center for answers. Our Help Center includes step-by-step guides, troubleshooting articles, FAQs, and best practices for many common questions and issues. You can also use our Otter AI Chat within the Otter app to ask questions. It can help you quickly find information or point you to the appropriate Help Center article. Learn more about AI Chat. #### Request prioritization Ticket requests are prioritized based on account Support is prioritized for Business and Enterprise plans. For more information, refer to our Pricing page. If you’re interested in the Enterprise plan, please contact our Sales team by submitting the Sales form. #### FAQs #### Does Otter offer phone support? No. Otter does not currently offer phone support at this time. To contact Otter Support, submit a support request through the Help Center. Our Support team will respond by email to help troubleshoot your issue or answer your questions. #### Can I solve my issue without contacting support? Before contacting our Otter Support team, we recommend searching our Help Center. Many common questions can be resolved without submitting a support request. Here are some of our most popular articles: - How long does it take Otter to process a recording or an imported file? - Why won’t my audio or video file upload - How can I improve transcription accuracy? - How do I cancel or downgrade my Otter Pro subscription? downgrade an Otter Pro subscription? - How do I delete my Otter account?

If you’re a member of an Otter Business or Enterprise workspace If you’re a workspace member and not an administrator, your workspace admin may be able to help resolve your issue without contacting Otter Support. Common requests include: - Removing yourself from the Workspace - Billing and subscription questions - Changing user roles and permissions

How long does it take to receive a response from Otter Support?

Response times vary based on your Otter plan, the type of request, and current support volume. Support requests are prioritized according to your plan. Refer to our Pricing page for more information about support levels. #### How can I check the status of my support request? After submitting a support request, you’ll receive a confirmation email. You can reply directly to that email to provide additional information or continue communicating with our Otter Support team. #### I submitted a support request, but haven’t received a reply. Check your spam or junk folder for emails from Otter Support, and add support@otter.ai to your contacts or safe sender list. If you still haven’t received a confirmation email, verify that you submitted your request using a valid email address and that you’re able to receive emails at that address. Submit another request below if needed. #### Can I rely on the Help Center chatbot’s answers? We have both the Otter AI chat in the product and the Help Center chatbot on our help center site to answer questions. They are both designed to provide quick answers based on our documentation, but they may occasionally misunderstand a question or provide inaccurate information. For the most accurate and up-to-date guidance, refer to the linked Help Center articles or contact our Otter Support team. We continuously improve the chatbot using updated Help Center content. #### Does Otter have a Help Center for Enterprise features and administrators? Yes. Otter provides a dedicated Enterprise Help Center with guides for administrators, IT teams, and workspace owners. You’ll find documentation on Enterprise features such as SAML Single Sign-On (SSO), Directory Sync (SCIM), security and compliance settings, workspace management, Otter Notetaker administration, integrations, and other Enterprise-only capabilities. Visit the Enterprise Help Center to browse all Enterprise guides and documentation.

Delete account option is missing

There could be several reasons why the Delete account option may not appear in your account settings. To help prevent accidental data loss, Otter may hide this option based on your account type, subscription, or workspace membership. Review the scenarios below to identify your situation and learn what steps are required before you can delete your account. #### You are a member or admin of a workspace The Delete account option is not available for users who belong to a workspace with two or more members. Review this section to learn the requirements for deleting your account If you are part of a workspace that has 2 or more members, the Delete account option is hidden. For members or admins of a workspace, contact the workspace owner to have your account deleted. ➡️ Have the workspace owner follow our instructions on deleting a member. ##### Find the owner of your workspace The workspace owner’s contact info can be found in the workspace indicated by the role Owner or Managed by.  #### You are the owner of a workspace Workspace owners must remove, deactivate, or delete all other workspace members before they can delete their own account As the owner of a workspace, you cannot delete your account if your workspace has 2 or more members (including yourself). Check how many users you have in Manage workspace. To delete your account, follow these steps: 1. Remove or delete all users from your workspace. 2. Once you are the only user remaining in your workspace, follow our Delete your account steps.

Delete your Otter account

As part of putting you in control of your data and privacy, Otter gives you the ability to permanently delete your account. When your account is deleted, the account details, conversations, and data associated with your account are permanently deleted from Otter’s servers. #### ➡️ Before deleting 1. Turn off Otter Notetaker auto-join. 2. Disconnect your calendar. 3. Export your conversations to a text or audio file to save any conversations you currently need or expect to need. 4. Review your subscription and cancel. When deleting, all paid subscriptions are forfeited.

Delete your account

  1. Go to Account Settings → General, scroll to the bottom,** and click Delete account**.
  2. Enter your password and click Verify and continue.

Note: Depending on how you created your account, account verification may be through a different platform (e.g., Google, Microsoft, Apple sign-in). 3. Carefully review the pop-up and click Delete account.

  1. Your Otter account has been permanently deleted.

Delete your account on mobile

There may be scenarios in which you cannot delete your account through the mobile app. If you run into any errors, we recommend deleting your account on a web browser. 1. Tap Account → Account Settings. 2. Scroll down and tap Delete account. 3. Verify your credentials. Depending on how you originally created your account, verify through the associated method. Tap Verify and continue. 4. Carefully read through the pop-up. Tap Delete account permanently to proceed with immediately deleting your account. 5. Your Otter account has been permanently deleted.

Why is the “Delete account” option is missing

There are several reasons why the Delete account option may not appear in your account settings. To help prevent accidental data loss, Otter may hide this option based on your account type, subscription, or workspace membership. Review the scenarios below to identify your situation and learn what steps are required before you can delete your account. ##### You are a member or admin of a workspace The Delete account option is not available for users who belong to a workspace with two or more members. Review this section to learn the requirements for deleting your account If you are part of a workspace that has 2 or more members, the Delete account option is hidden. For members or admins of a workspace, contact the workspace owner to have your account deleted. ➡️ Have the workspace owner follow our instructions on deleting a member. ###### Find the owner of your workspace The workspace owner’s contact info can be found in the workspace indicated by the role Owner or Managed by.  ##### You are the owner of a workspace Workspace owners must remove, deactivate, or delete all other workspace members before they can delete their own account As the owner of a workspace, you cannot delete your account if your workspace has 2 or more members (including yourself). Check how many users you have in Manage workspace. To delete your account, follow these steps: 1. Remove or delete all users from your workspace. 2. Once you are the only user remaining in your workspace, follow our Delete your account steps.

FAQs

I don’t see the “delete account” option. Why is it missing?

If you don’t see the ‘delete account’ option, you may be in a workspace with 2 or more members. Learn more about why the delete account option may be missing. #### Can I recover or restore my deleted account? No. A deleted account is permanent and irreversible and can not be recovered or restored. #### What happens to my conversations when my account is deleted? When your account is deleted, all of your conversations are permanently deleted as well. Deleted conversations are not recoverable. #### What happens to the conversations that I’ve shared with others? Any conversations that you’ve shared with others are permanently deleted. They can no longer be accessed by others. #### What happens to my subscriptions? Subscriptions are canceled and forfeited if you choose to delete your account before the end of the billing cycle. Review our Terms of Service for more information. #### If I delete the mobile app, will it also delete my Otter account? No. Deleting the mobile app does not delete your Otter account. You must follow the steps in this article to delete your Otter account. Deleting the mobile app does not cancel your Otter subscription if you purchased through the Apple App Store or Google Play Store. Make sure you cancel your subscription through the associated platform. #### Is my email info deleted? Even after account deletion, we will retain certain limited account information, such as email, solely for accounting and administrative purposes. #### How do I leave a workspace so I can delete my account? If you’re a workspace member, contact your workspace admin and ask them to delete your account or to remove your access. Once you’re no longer part of the workspace, the Delete account option may become available. #### Do deactivated users need to be removed or deleted? Yes. Deactivated users in your workspace must be removed or deleted before you can delete your account if you are the owner of a workspace. Learn more about removing or deleting your workspace members. #### How to fix the error “Please contact support to handle deletion of your account”? If you encounter the “Please contact support to handle deletion of your account” message on mobile while trying to delete your account, review why the delete account option is missing. If you continue to experience issues, please contact our Otter support team.

Manage vocabulary

You can teach Otter jargon, names, and any other vocabulary words you choose to increase the accuracy of Otter, becoming the AI assistant you’ve always wanted. Add custom vocabulary to boost the accuracy for proper names, jargon, acronyms, and unique name spellings. The Basic free plan includes up to 5 words or phrases. The Pro plan gives you a personal vocabulary of up to 100 names and 100 other vocabularies. Upgrading to the Otter Business plan gives you a team vocabulary of 800 names and 800 other vocabularies (in addition to a personal vocabulary of 200 names and 200 other vocabularies per user). #### Add a term to your personal vocabulary 1. Sign in to Otter. 2. Click your name in the top left and click Account settings > General. 3. Click Manage Vocabulary. (image: Manage_vocabulary.png) 4. To add a proper name, click Names of people under Personal Vocabulary. To add a general term, click Other vocabulary.

Add a term to your team vocabulary

  1. Sign in to Otter.
  2. Click your name in the top left and click Account settings > General.
  3. Click Manage Vocabulary.
  4. To add a proper name, click Names of people under Team Vocabulary. To add a general term, click Other vocabulary. (image: Adding_vocabulary.gif)

Terms added to the team vocabulary are applied to every team member’s conversations within your team or organization.

Unsubscribe from Otter emails and turn off push notifications

Follow the steps below to unsubscribe from Otter emails or turn off push notifications. You have full control over your notifications. Unsubscribe through the web browser or through the email link. You can manage the following notification types: - Live meeting - Meeting summary - Imported conversation - Conversation shared - Weekly digest - Event reminder - Comments - Highlights - Product Tips - Offers & Discounts

Web

Follow these steps to unsubscribe from email notifications or to turn off push notifications on the web browser. 1. Expand your profile in the upper corner and click Account Settings. Click on the Notificationstab. (image: account settings _ notifications.png)

Manage all notification settings at once for Email and Push by unchecking All Notifications. (image: all notifications.png) Or manually uncheck notifications for each setting individually. (image: select which notifications.png) #### Mobile You can manage email and push notifications on the Otter iOS and Android mobile apps. 1. Open the Otter mobile app. 2. Tap on Account and select Account Settings. (image: account settings.png) 3. Tap Notifications. (image: notifications.png)

Toggle off all notifications at once, or tap to expand each setting section to toggle off Push or Email settings separately. (image: manage notifications.png) #### Email Unsubscribe options are located at the end of the email in the footer section. While each email layout may be slightly different, you can always find the unsubscribe options at the end of the email. (image: unsubscribe from emails.png) Click Unsubscribe to unsubscribe from only the specific notification type (e.g., Weekly digest, Event reminder, Product tips, etc.) related to the email. No login is required. (image: unsubscribe successful.png) Or click Email settings to sign in to your account to manage all notifications on the Account Settings > Notifications page.

Update your iOS Apple private relay (Hide My Email) email to a personal one

Sign in with Apple or Continue with Apple is one of the options we offer for iOS users to sign up with. If you created an Otter account using your Apple ID, you may have selected Hide My Email during the signup process. This creates a randomly generated email (typically ending in @privaterelay.appleid.com) assigned to your Otter account instead of the email attached to your Apple account. #### Why can this create problems? Using your Apple account is a convenient way to create an Otter account and sign in; however, there may be situations where you want to update your Otter account email to a personal one. Creating a 2nd account by accident - Users may accidentally sign up for two Otter accounts using both their personal account (otter-demo@otter.ai) and Apple private relay email (abc123@privaterelay.appleid.com). - This can create confusion when trying to find an account, subscription, or conversation. - A paid subscription may not be associated with the Otter account you are wanting to use due to the 2nd account.

Contacting Otter Support - When reaching out to us, we ask that you provide the email associated with your account. - Our team may need you to reply from the email address associated with the account, depending on the request. - Regardless if our emails are forwarded to your Apple email account (e.g., otter-demo@otter.ai), we still require a response from the Apple private relay email (e.g., abc123@privaterelay.appleid.com) as that is the email associated with the Otter account. - If you are unable to reply from the private relay email associated with the account, our team may not be able to fulfill your request.

Emails are not delivered - Emails from Otter be not be delivered successfully due to the @privaterelay.appleid.com domain.

Update your Apple private relay email to a person email

Follow the steps below to update your Apple private relay email to a personal email. If you are experiencing any issues or have questions, please contact our Otter Support Team. - Step 1: Find the private relay email associated with your Otter account - Step 2: Attach a password to your account - Step 3: Update your account email

Step 1: Find the private relay email associated with your Otter account
  1. On your iOS device, tap on Settings. Tap on your Apple Profile.
  2. Tap Sign in with Apple. ****
  3. Under APPS AND WEBSITES, look for Otter and tap on the application.
  4. Copy down the email next to Hide My Email. ****
Step 2: Attach a password to your account

Attaching a password to your account will ensure you are able to update the email, as we currently do not support changing an email through the Apple sign-in verification. 1. On a browser, navigate to our Forgot Password page: https://otter.ai/forgot-password. 2. Type in the Apple private relay email. Click Send reset link.  3. Navigate to Otter Password Reset email. Click Reset Now to add a password to your account. 

Note: If you do not see the email, check your spam folder. 

Step 3: Update your account email

Now that you have updated your password, you’ll be able to update your account email. 1. Sign in to Otter.ai on a browser. 

Note: Updating your account email is only supported on a mobile or desktop browser and not through the Otter mobile app at this time. 2. Navigate to Account Settings > General > Profile. Click Edit. 3. Click into the Email field to add your new email. 4. Click the blue check mark when you are finished typing the new email. Type in the password. 5. Type in your password. Click Validate. **** 6. Follow the steps in the email sent to confirm the email change. Click Okay. 7. Click Done to finish.

FAQs

Can I use my Apple account password to validate the email change?

  • No.
  • You cannot use your Apple account password to validate your email change.
  • You must attach a password to your Otter account.

Can I change or update my email on the mobile Otter app?

  • No.
  • Changing or updating your email on the mobile Otter app is not yet supported at this time. We hope to bring this functionality to the mobile app in future updates.
  • You will need to sign in to Otter on a mobile or desktop browser to access Account Settings > General > Profile.
  • Follow Step 3 above to change your email.

Account settings & billing: Managing billing, payment, & plan

Add a UK VAT ID to your Otter account

If your billing address is in the United Kingdom (UK), you can add a valid UK Value-Added Tax identification number (VAT ID) to your Otter account. VAT IDs will be applied to future eligible purchases and invoices. #### Before you begin - You must have a valid UK VAT registration number. - Add your VAT ID before your next renewal or purchase to ensure it is applied to future invoices. - Adding or updating a VAT ID does not automatically change invoices that have already been issued.

Add your VAT ID

  1. Go to Integrations (Account Settings) → Billing, then click Edit next to Billing Contact.
  2. Select United Kingdom (UK) as your billing country, enter your ZIP/postal code and VAT ID, then click Confirm.

Note: Enter your VAT ID with the GB prefix (e.g., GB123456789). 3. Your VAT ID will automatically appear on future eligible invoices.

Add a VAT ID during checkout

If you’re purchasing Otter for the first time, you can add your UK VAT ID during checkout. Select UK (United Kingdom) as your billing country to display the VAT ID field. #### Correcting previous purchases after adding a VAT ID Adding a VAT ID to your billing account only applies to future purchases and invoices. If you need a correction for a previous purchase after adding your VAT ID, send proof of your VAT registration to ar@otter.ai or fill out this form. Once the Otter.ai team has reviewed the supporting documentation, the team will make the necessary corrections. #### FAQs #### Can I add a VAT ID after purchasing? Yes. You can add or update your UK VAT ID at any time in Integrations (Account Settings) → Billing → Billing Contact. However, it will only apply to future eligible purchases and invoices. Previously issued invoices won’t be updated. You can correct previous purchases after adding a VAT ID. #### Can I use a VAT ID from another country? No. Otter currently supports VAT IDs issued in the United Kingdom (UK) only. #### Why don’t I see the VAT ID field? The VAT ID field is only displayed when United Kingdom (UK) is selected as the billing country. #### When will my VAT ID appear on invoices? Once your VAT ID has been added, it will automatically appear on future eligible invoices. It won’t be added to invoices that have already been issued. You can correct previous purchases after adding a VAT ID. #### Can I add my VAT ID during checkout? Yes. If you’re purchasing Otter for the first time, select United Kingdom (UK) as your billing country during checkout to enter your VAT ID. #### Why am I getting an error when adding my VAT ID? Enter your VAT ID with the GB prefix. The following formats are accepted when updating your billing information: - ✅ GB123456789 - ✅ GB123 4567 89 - ❌ 123456789 (missing GB prefix)

If on the checkout page, enter your VAT ID without spaces: - ✅ GB123456789 - ❌ GB123 4567 89 - ❌ 123456789

Cancel an Otter subscription

This guide provides a comprehensive overview and general steps for canceling an Otter subscription. For more specific and detailed instructions, click the option below that best matches your plan. - Cancel your Business subscription - Cancel your Pro subscription - Cancel your Business trial

Only the owner or admin(s) can cancel an Otter subscription. They may be canceled at any time to set the subscription to expire at the end of the billing period. Once the paid subscription expires, the workspace can continue to use Otter on the Basic plan. - Subscriptions automatically renew unless you cancel them. - If you cancel, you can keep using the paid features until the subscription expires at the end of the current billing period. - Cancel your subscription at least 24 hours before the next billing date to avoid an unwanted charge. - Applicable limits of the Basic plan apply after the paid subscription expires. - Subscriptions are non-refundable as explained in the Otter Terms of Service.

Steps to cancel

  1. Sign in to Otter.ai as an admin.
  2. Click on your profile in the left navigation menu. Select (Integrations) Account Settings → Billing → Cancel plan.

Tip: Don’t want to fully cancel your subscription, but need a break? Try using the Pause Plan option instead. 3. Select your main reason for unsubscribing,** and then click Continue. 4. Carefully review the information in the pop-up. The message may vary depending on your plan or workspace. Click Cancel subscription **to confirm.

✅ The subscription has been canceled and will expire at the end of the billing period. You and any member in your workspace can continue to use the paid features until the end of the billing cycle. #### Canceling a subscription on the mobile app 1. Tap on Account → Account Settings. 2. Scroll down and tap Manage subscription. **** 3. Click Cancel subscription. **** 4. Click Continue to confirm. **** 5. Once your account has been canceled, you will still have access to your full subscription until the billing period ends.

FAQs

Does canceling my subscription delete my account?

No. Canceling your subscription is not the same as deleting your account. Learn more about deleting your account. #### Can I pause my subscription? Yes. You can pause your subscription in Account Settings → Plan. Click Pause Plan. #### If I cancel my subscription, is it immediate? No. Your subscription will expire at the end of your billing cycle. Review when your billing cycle ends in Account Settings → Plan. You and any member in your workspace can continue to use the paid features until the end of the billing cycle. You can reactivate your plan anytime before it expires, or repurchase it after it expires, on the billing page. #### I only need to use Otter for a short duration. Can I pay for a specific duration and not be charged for renewal? Subscriptions automatically renew per our Terms of Service. Make sure to cancel anytime before the end of the subscription billing cycle to avoid being charged for renewal. Canceling will set your subscription to expire at the end of the billing period. You can continue to use the paid features until the subscription expires. #### Can I get a refund for my subscription? Otter does not offer refunds for purchased subscriptions per our Terms of Service. For subscriptions purchased through the Apple App Store or Google Play Store, please manage your subscription directly on their platform for any requests regarding billing, refunds, or cancellations, and refer to their respective refund policies. #### I forgot to cancel my subscription. Can I get a refund on my unused portion? Otter does not offer refunds for subscriptions purchased per our Terms of Service. For subscriptions purchased through the Apple App Store or Google Play Store, please manage your subscription directly on their platform for any requests regarding billing, refunds, or cancellations, and refer to their respective refund policies. #### What happens after the paid subscription has expired? The Otter workspace will remain active. Once a paid subscription has expired, the workspace can continue to use Otter on the Basic plan. Refer to our pricing page to learn more about the features available on the Basic plan. Team members will continue to have access to the 25 most recent conversations. Applicable limits of the Basic plan apply after the paid subscription expires. #### How can I purchase my own individual subscription after my workspace subscription has expired? If you are a member of a workspace, contact your admin to have them remove you from it. You can then purchase a subscription for your own account/workspace in Account Settings → Plan. Learn more about purchasing an Otter subscription.

Cancel your Otter Pro subscription

Cancel your Otter Pro subscription at any time from your workspace billing settings. When you cancel, your paid subscription remains active until the end of the current billing period. After the subscription expires, your workspace will automatically move to the Otter Basic plan and retain access to your Otter account and conversations. - Subscriptions automatically renew unless you cancel them. - If you cancel, you can keep using the paid features until the subscription expires at the end of the current billing period. - Cancel your subscription at least 24 hours before the next billing date to avoid an unwanted charge. - Subscriptions are non-refundable as explained in the Otter Terms of Service.

Steps to cancel your Otter Pro subscription

Subscriptions purchased through Otter.ai must be managed on a web browser. For subscriptions purchased through the Apple App Store or Google Play Store, please manage your subscription directly on their platform for any requests regarding billing, refunds, or cancellations, and refer to their respective refund policies. Select the option below that best matches your original subscription purchase method. #### Subscriptions purchased through Otter.ai (web) 1. Sign in to Otter.ai as an admin. 2. Go to Integrations (Account Settings) → Billing, and click Cancel Plan.

Tip: Don’t want to fully cancel your subscription, but need a break? Try using the Pause plan option instead. 3. Select your main reason for cancellation from the options listed or provide more information, and click Continue. 4. Carefully review the information in the pop-up. The message may vary depending on the plan. Click Cancel subscription to confirm. 5. The subscription has been canceled and will expire at the end of the billing period. You and any member in your workspace can continue to use the paid features until the end of the billing cycle.

Reactivate your plan

If you change your mind before the billing cycle ends, reactivate your plan at any time in Account Settings → Billing → Reactivate plan. #### Cancel your Otter subscription on mobile For subscriptions purchased through the Apple App Store or Google Play Store, please manage your subscription directly on their platform for any requests regarding billing, refunds, or cancellations, and refer to their respective refund policies. 1. Tap Account and select Account Settings. 2. Scroll down and tap Manage subscription. 3. Click Cancel subscription. 4. Click Continue. 5. Depending on where you purchased your subscription, you may need to manage and complete the cancellation through that platform. Only subscriptions purchased directly through Otter.ai can be canceled within the Otter app.

After clicking Continue, you’ll be redirected to your Apple subscription to complete the process.

Note: Google may update its subscription management steps, instructions, or UI over time. The subscription has been canceled and will expire at the end of the billing period. You and any member in your workspace can continue to use the paid features until the end of the billing cycle.

If you cancel your Otter Pro subscription, you can continue using paid features until the end of your current billing period. Error in creating team subscription

There may be many reasons why an error appears on the checkout page when trying to purchase a subscription. (image: error in creating subscription.png) #### Make sure you are logged into an admin account Only admins can purchase and manage subscriptions for a Workspace. Members cannot purchase a subscription for a Workspace. Make sure you are logged into an admin account before purchasing a subscription. Users in Workspace > Manage Workspace > Members with the role ‘Member’ cannot purchase subscriptions. Reach out to your admin to have them purchase or have the admin update your role. (image: member.png) #### General troubleshooting tips We recommend clearing cache and cookies and restarting your browser: Learn how to clear cookies and site data in Chrome, Safari, and Firefox. Sign back into Otter with an admin account and try purchasing again. If you continue to experience issues, contact our Otter Support Team.

Move your Apple App Store (iOS) or Google Play Store (Android) subscription to Otter via Web

We recommend purchasing your Otter subscription via Web to take advantage of the following: - Savings vs. subscribing via Apple or Google. - Our Otter Support Team can help manage your subscription if any issues arise. - Manage and view your subscription directly within Otter, and easily upgrade to Otter Business (only available via Web) when ready.

Select the situation below that best fits your current subscription status and follow the outlined steps to purchase an Otter Pro Annual subscription via Web. To purchase a subscription via Web, you will need to first cancel your subscription that was purchased through the Apple App Store. Once your subscription has expired, you can purchase a new Otter Pro subscription via Web. Below outlines the steps for canceling your subscription on an iPhone/iPad: #### Step 1: Cancel your Otter Apple App Store subscription 1. Open the Settings app on your iPhone/iPad. 2. Tap your name. 3. Tap Subscriptions. 4. Tap the Otter.ai subscription. 5. Tap Cancel Subscription. You might need to scroll down to find the Cancel Subscription button. If there is no Cancel button or you see an expiration message in red text, the subscription is already canceled. 6. Tap Confirm to proceed with cancellation. 7. Your subscription will expire at the end of the billing cycle.

Once your subscription has expired, you can proceed to Step 2. We recommend waiting until the subscription expires to avoid being double charged. #### Step 2: Purchase your Otter subscription via Web 1. Log into your Otter.ai account. 2. Click your profile in the upper right corner to expand the menu. Click Upgrade plan. 3. Click Buy Now in the Pro column. 4. Fill out your billing and payment information. Click Continue to complete the purchase. 5. You are now on the Otter Pro Annual subscription purchased via Web.

To purchase a subscription via Web, you will need to first cancel your subscription that was purchased through the Google Play store. Once your subscription has expired, you can purchase a new Otter Pro subscription via Web. Below outlines the steps for canceling your subscription through Google Play store: #### Step 1: Cancel your Otter Google Play store subscription 1. On your Android device or browser, go to your subscriptions in Google Play. 2. Select the subscription you want to cancel. 3. Tap Cancel subscription. 4. Follow the instructions.

Once your subscription has expired, you can proceed to Step 2. We recommend waiting until the subscription expires to avoid being double charged. #### Step 2: Purchase your Otter subscription via Web 1. Log into your Otter.ai account. 2. Click your profile in the upper right corner to expand the menu. Click Upgrade plan. 3. Click Buy Now in the Pro column. 4. Fill out your billing and payment information. Click Continue to complete the purchase. 5. You are now on the Otter Pro Annual subscription purchased via Web.

If you have any further questions regarding moving your iOS / Android subscription to Web, don’t hesitate to contact our Otter Support Team. We’re happy to answer any questions.

My Apple/iOS Otter Pro paid subscription is not showing

This article will provide more information on troubleshooting your paid subscription, purchased through the Apple app store, not showing in your account. Review the troubleshooting steps below to find your paid account. #### iOS subscription is not reflected in my account ##### Signed up using Apple Private Relay The most common reason why your subscription may not be showing up is due to a second account through Apple Private Relay. Apple provides a way to hide your email address during sign-up. Using this method creates an email address that is separate from your iCloud/Apple account: - iCloud/Apple email: otter-demo@icloud.com - Private Relay email: @privaterelay.appleid.com

You may have purchased a subscription through one of those accounts, or through a second email using one of our login methods: Google Sign-in, Microsoft Sign-in, or Email + Password combination. To find your paid account, please provide our Otter Support Team with the original transaction id: 1. Log in to the Otter mobile app. 2. Tap on the menu icon (3 lines) in the upper left corner. 3. Tap the blue Upgrade button. **** 4. Tap Restore purchases. **** 5. A blue pop-up will appear containing the original_transaction_id. ****

Contact us with the original_transaction_id and our Otter.ai Support Team will respond as soon as possible. Note: We are not able to find your account through the receipt number. Apple restricts all access to its billing system. We will need the original transaction id to find your account.

My Google Play store Otter Pro subscription is not showing in my account

This article will provide more information on troubleshooting your paid subscription, purchased through the Google Play store, not showing in your account. Review the troubleshooting steps below to find your paid account. #### Google Play store subscription is not reflected in my account We can find your paid account through the order number. Subscriptions purchased through the Google Play store will be associated with an order number. You can find the order number on your receipt. The receipt will have been sent by Google and may contain the subject line ‘Your Google Play Order Receipt’. Try searching your email inbox for the receipt or through your Google Play store account. Contact us with the Order number (including the GPA) and our Otter.ai Support Team will respond as soon as possible.

My paid subscription is not showing

The most common reason why your subscription may not be reflected in your account is due to a second account. Make sure you are logged into the correct account to access your paid subscription. View our trouble signing into your Otter account article if you are having trouble accessing your account. If you have gone through troubleshooting steps and still are not able to see your paid account, please select the topic associated with your subscription purchase method and provide the related information to our Otter Support Team. - Otter subscription originally purchased on the web browser - Otter subscription originally purchased through Apple app store - Otter subscription originally purchased through Google Play store

Subscriptions purchased on a Web browser

Invoice

Check the Otter receipt/invoice, as the email associated with your account is shown there. The receipt/invoice will have been sent by Stripe and may contain the subject line ‘Your receipt from Otter.ai’. Try searching your email inbox for the receipt/invoice. To find the email address, open the receipt/invoice. The email will be located under Bill to. Complete a password reset here if needed. If you are unable to find the receipt/invoice, experience issues logging into the email, or no longer have access to that email, reach out to us with the four pieces of billing information. ##### Payment method used to purchase the subscription Provide the following information, so that we can find the account associated with your paid subscription: - Last 4 digits of the payment card on file - Card expiration date - Amount of the last transaction - Last transaction date

Contact us with the 4 pieces of information and our Otter Support Team will respond as soon as possible.

Sales Tax FAQ

Does Otter.ai charge sales tax?

To comply with local laws and regulations, Otter.ai is required to charge sales tax on Otter.ai purchases when applicable. Sales tax is calculated from the customer provided billing address. It is critical that the customer provide accurate address information as tax rates will differ based on the jurisdiction. If sales tax is applicable, the customer is responsible for paying all applicable taxes charged per Section 4 of Otter.ai’s Terms of Service. If the customer believes sales tax is being charged in error, please see the section below. #### When did Otter.ai start charging sales tax? For customers who have purchased Otter.ai on the Apple App Store and Google Play Store, sales tax is already included in the price at the time of purchase. For customers who purchase on the Otter.ai website or through the assistance of Otter.ai’s sales team, sales taxes application to purchases began on Sep/Oct 2024. Sales tax will be charged when required by local laws and regulations. #### Where does Otter.ai charge sales tax? For customers who purchase on the Otter.ai website or through the assistance of Otter.ai’s sales team, Otter.ai started charging sales taxes on Sep/Oct 2024 in the countries and states listed below. Please note that this list may change when necessary to comply with local laws and regulations. - Country: State/Local Jurisdiction: Effective Date - United States: Arizona: May 2026 - United States: Alaska (select cities): Sep 2024 - United States: Colorado (select cities): Sep 2024 - United States: Connecticut: Sep 2024 - United States: District of Columbia: Oct 2024 - United States: Hawaii: Sep 2024 - United States: Iowa: Sep 2024 - United States: Illinois (Chicago): Sep 2024 - United States: Kentucky: Sep 2024 - United States: Louisiana: Jan 2025 - United States: Massachusetts: Oct 2024 - United States: Maryland: Oct 2024 - United States: New Mexico: Sep 2024 - United States: New York: Oct 2024 - United States: Ohio: Oct 2024 - United States: Pennsylvania: Oct 2024 - United States: Rhode Island: Sep 2024 - United States: South Carolina: Oct 2024 - United States: Tennessee: Oct 2024 - United States: Texas: Oct 2024 - United States: Utah: Oct 2024 - United States: Vermont: Sep 2024 - United States: Washington: Oct 2024 - United States: West Virginia: Sep 2024 - Canada: Alberta, Northwest Territories, Nunavut, Yukon (GST): Jan 2026 - Canada: New Brunswick, Newfoundland and Labrador, Nova Scotia, Ontario, Prince Edward Island (HST): Jan 2026 - Canada: British Columbia (GST + PST): Jan 2026 - Canada: Quebec (GST + QST): Jan 2026 - Canada: Saskatchewan (GST + PST): Jan 2026 - Canada: Manitoba (GST + PST): Jan 2026

Can I exclude sales tax on my first purchase?

Sales tax exemptions will be applied after the initial purchase, which will include sales tax if applicable. Once the first purchase has been made, please send ar@otter.ai the email address associated with the Otter account along with a valid sales tax exemption certificate. Once received and reviewed, the Accounting team will apply a sales tax exemption status on the account for future purchases and issue any credits/refunds for sales tax when appropriate. #### What do I do if I believe the sales tax was charged in error? As a reminder, Otter.ai will charge sales tax whenever required by local law and regulations. Incorrect Billing address: If the customer believes the incorrect rate is being charged, this can be due to an incorrect billing address provided at the time of purchase. If purchased through the Apple App Store or Google Play Store, customers should update their address in their respective account. If assistance is required, please reach out to their respective support teams to assist in correcting the address on the account. If purchased through the Otter.ai website or with help from the Otter.ai sales team, the owner/billing contact on the account can update the zip code by logging into the Otter account, navigating to Account Settings → Billing, and clicking on Billing Contact to update. Learn more about updating your billing ZIP code. Once completed, please email ar@otter.ai to let our Accounting team know the billing zip code has been updated so they can complete any credits or refunds, if applicable. Exemption Certificate and Remitted Sales Tax: If the customer has an exemption certificate, VAT ID, or has already remitted sales tax for their Otter.ai purchase, the customer should send the exemption certificate and proof of remitted sales tax to ar@otter.ai or fill out this form. Once the Otter.ai team has reviewed the supporting documentation, the team will make the necessary corrections. Note that exemption certificates, if offered by the taxing jurisdiction, are available on that tax jurisdiction’s official website. Otter.ai may not assist in filling out a customer’s tax certificate. If assistance is needed, please reach out to a tax professional. #### Can I add a VAT ID? If your billing address is in the United Kingdom (UK), you can add a valid UK Value-Added Tax identification number (VAT ID) to your Otter account. VAT IDs will be applied to future eligible purchases and invoices. Learn more about adding a VAT ID to your Otter account. #### When should I reach out for the status on a sales tax exemption request? Once the request is submitted, our Accounting team will review your request and make any necessary corrections. Notifications of credits/refunds will automatically be sent to the billing contact listed on your Otter account. If you do not receive any notifications within 10 business days, please reach out directly to ar@otter.ai to follow up on the status of your request. #### Where can I find more information regarding sales tax for my state or country? As tax forms and tax law change periodically, Otter.ai recommends the customer reach out to a tax professional or do their own independent research on the tax jurisdiction’s website. Otter.ai does not provide any tax advice to customers. The company will only charge sales tax when required and at rates specified by local law and regulations.

Add a new payment method

Add a new credit card to use for future payments. 1. Follow the steps above to navigate to the Billing page. 2. Click Add next to Payment Method. 3. Enter your card details in the pop-up.

Note: Optionally, uncheck Make primary payment if you do not want this card to be charged as the default payment. 4. Click Add to confirm. 5. The card has been added and will be used for all future payments.

Repeat steps 1-4 to add multiple payment methods. #### Update your credit card information Update your credit card information anytime for new details or to replace expired credit cards. 1. Follow the steps above to navigate to the Billing page. 2. Click on the 3-dot menu next to the credit card you want to update. Click Edit. 3. Enter your updated card details in the pop-up.

Note: Optionally, uncheck Make primary payment if you do not want this card to be charged as the default payment. 4. Click Save to confirm. 5. The card has been added and will be used for all future payments.

Delete a payment method

Delete a credit card on file. 1. Follow the steps above to navigate to the Billing page. 2. Click on the 3-dot menu next to the credit card you want to delete. Select Delete.

Note: To delete the existing default card on file, set another card as the default first. **** 3. Carefully review the pop-up. Click Delete to proceed. 4. The card has been deleted from your account.

If you have only one card on file (set as the default), you will not be able to delete it. Add another payment method and set that card as the default first. You can then follow the steps above to delete the card on file. #### Make a card the default payment method If you have multiple cards on file, you can set a default payment method. The default payment method will be used for all future subscriptions, licenses, and purchases. 1. Follow the steps above to navigate to the Billing page. 2. Click on the 3-dot menu next to the credit card you want to set as the default payment method. Select Make default. 3. The card is set as the default payment method and will be used for all future charges.

  • For subscriptions purchased through the Apple App Store or Google Play Store, refer to their platform for more information on available payment methods.
