Account settings & billing: Security settings

Set up two-factor authentication (2FA)

For an added layer of security, turn on two-factor authentication (2FA) for your account. If your password is compromised or stolen, you’ll have peace of mind knowing that two-factor authentication keeps others out of your account, even if they have your password. #### How it works With 2FA enabled, when you sign in to your account, you’ll need to provide two pieces of information—your password and a one-time temporary 6-digit verification code. By entering the code, you’re verifying that you trust your device. Because your password alone is no longer enough to access your account, 2FA dramatically improves the security of your account and all the important conversations you store with Otter. (image: codes.gif) Once signed in, you won’t be asked for a verification code on that device again unless you sign out completely or need to change your password for security reasons. We have two options for you to receive the 6-digit verification code: Authenticator or Phone Number (SMS) Authenticator  For all plans, you will be able to use any authenticator service/app that is able to receive a 6-digit one-time password (OTP). Here are some authenticator options you can use, however, any OTP authenticator service will work: - Google Authenticator (recommended) - Microsoft Authenticator - Duo Security - Authy

Phone Number (SMS) This option is only available for our Business & Enterprise plans. If you are on a Business or Enterprise plan, you will have the option to use your trusted phone number to receive an SMS text with your 6-digit verification code instead of using an authenticator app. #### How to turn on two-factor authentication (2FA) Follow these steps to turn on 2FA for your account: 1. Click on your name in the upper right corner to expand the menu. 2. Click on **Account Settings. (image: 1__account_settings.png)**** 3. Navigate to the Security tab on the right. 4. Click Set up **to begin the process. (image: 2__2fa.png) 5. Click Continue for the Authentication app method. (image: 3__choose_the_app.png) 6. You will then need to scan the QR code or add the secret key in your authenticator app. Add in the 6-digit verification code and click Continue. (image: 4__authenticate.png) 7. Your account is now set up with 2FA. (image: 5__success.png) 8. (Recommended) On the Securitytab, we recommend generating recovery codes at the bottom to ensure you will be able to access your account even if you lose your phone. Save the codes in a safe location. (image: 6__recovery_codes.png)

How to turn on two-factor authentication (2FA) for your Team account

For Business & Enterprise, Admins can turn on 2FA for your entire team to ensure your users’ accounts are secured. As the admin, you will need to first set up 2FA for your own account, before enabling 2FA for the rest of the team.

 (image: 9__setup.png) Follow the How to turn on two-factor authentication for your account instructions. Once you have enabled it for your own account, follow the steps below. 1. Sign in to Otter using your admin account. 2. Click on your name in the upper right corner to expand the menu. 3. Click My Team. **(image: 7__my_team.png) 4. Navigate to Settings & Security** and toggle on Two-factor authentication. (image: 8__team_2fa.png) 5. A pop-up will appear. Click Enable to complete the process. (image: 9__enable_for_whole_team.png) 6. All team members will be signed out if they have not set up 2FA yet. They will be prompted to add a phone number or set up through the authenticator app method once they sign in again. (image: 10__user_add_phone.png)

Changing authentication method

Each team member will be able to select the authentication method that works best for them. You can use an authenticator app or your phone number for SMS. 1. Go to Account Settings > Security. 2. Click Change authentication method. (image: 11__change_method.png) 3. You will need to enter your password to verify. (image: 12__verify.png)

Note: If you originally created your Otter account using Google, Microsoft, or Apple sign-in, you will need to verify through that method.

  1. You will then have the option to change your authentication method. (image: 12__change_method.png)
  2. Click Continue to follow the steps for setup.

Turn off two-factor authentication (2FA)

To turn off two-factor authentication for your own account, follow these steps. 1. Navigate to Account Settings > Security.  2. Click Turn off. **(image: 13__turn_off.png) ** 3. Verify that you are the owner. 4. 2FA is now turned off.

To turn off two-factor authentication for your Team account, follow these steps. 1. As the admin, navigate to My Teams > Settings & Security. 2. Toggle off Two-factor authentication. (image: 14__turn_off_team_2fa.png) 3. Click **Disable. (image: 15__disable.png)**** ** 4. Team members who set up it previously will still have 2FA enabled. They can individually turn it off or on for their own account.

If you’re having trouble accessing your account with two-factor authentication, contact our Otter Support Team for assistance. You can also review our ‘Troubleshooting two-factor authentication’ article for more information.

Troubleshooting two-factor authentication (2FA)

If you are having trouble setting up two-factor authentication (2FA) or signing into your account with 2FA, check out the troubleshooting steps below. #### I am encountering an error setting up two-factor authentication (2FA) Please check out our ‘Set up two-factor authentication (2FA)’ article for installation steps. - Please ensure your authentication app supports 6-digit one-time password codes. We recommend using Google Authenticator for the authenticator app. - You will need to grab the 6-digit temporary code either through the authenticator app or through SMS in order to finish the setup process. - If you requested multiple codes, only the newest one will work. Codes expire after three minutes. After a code expires, you can’t use it to sign in. Wait a few moments and then resend a new code and try again. - If an error message pops up, please customer support, with images or a screen recording, along with your steps.

You didn’t receive a text message (SMS)

If you’re not receiving codes by text message (SMS), this could be for a few reasons. - Make sure your service plan and mobile device support text message delivery. Delivery speed and availability may vary by location and service provider. - Make sure your phone has a strong internet connection and that you’ve turned on cellular data on your phone. If this setting is off, you might not be able to receive text messages. - If text message delivery is slow in your location or you have limited cellular service, try moving to a different location or connecting to a Wi-Fi network, and try resending a new code.

I’ve lost or can’t access my phone/authenticator app

  • If you can’t receive codes by text message because you don’t have your phone or can’t access it, try using another device or computer that can receive text messages sent to your phone number. Learn how to send and receive text messages from your Mac or PC.
  • If you can’t access your authenticator app, please use the recovery codes that you saved during setup to log back into your account. Once you’re in the account, you can go toAccount Settings > Security, to turn off and set up 2FA again. (image: 19__use_recovery_code.png)
  • If you have SMS enabled for your 2FA, but have left the US or Canada region, please customer support. 

I am not able to log in

  • Check out our ‘Can’t sign in to your Otter account’ article for more information.
  • If your 6-digit code is not working when logging in, please wait a few minutes to resend the code and try again.
  • If you’re on a Business or Enterprise plan, your admin may have enforced 2FA. On the first sign-in, you will need to attach a phone number to set up 2FA before continuing to log in.

Note: For Business & Enterprise plans, where your admin has enabled 2FA, you will only have the option to add a phone number on the first sign-in after 2FA has been enabled. Once you are signed in, you can update the authentication method.  If you’re still experiencing problems, contact your admin or customer support. 

Announcements: Known issues

Audio imports not supported for Otter iOS App 3.8.0 and earlier

Please note that the Otter iOS apps with version 3.8.0 and earlier will no longer be able to import audio files. Update your app to the latest version to continue using audio imports. Update iOS app now

Conversation being transcribed incorrectly

If your conversation is being transcribed incorrectly, try exporting the conversation audio and re-importing the audio back into Otter. This will reprocess the conversation. 1. Export the conversation audio. 2. Re-import the audio into Otter.

Known apps, browser extensions, and plugins that affect Otter

If you are experiencing an issue using Otter in your browser, there may be an app, 3rd party browser extension, VPN, or plugin affecting Otter. The list below are known apps, extensions, VPNs, or plugins that may affect Otter functionality. #### Known apps, extensions, plugins, and VPNs We recommend disabling or removing apps, extensions, VPNs, and plugins that are affecting Otter. It is up to the user to verify, test, and ensure that installed browser extensions and plugins do not affect Otter. - Privacy Badger - MaxAI.me - TikTok Pixel Helper - Perimeter 81 VPN - McAfee VPN - TeamCity Notifier - Bitdefender Anti-Tracker - uBlock Origin - Jump Desktop

Otter transcript missing audio when using a Chrome browser

Chrome added an experimental feature, Echo Cancellation, to remove any sound played out of the speakers from the microphone signal on the same computer. When the experimental feature is enabled, the microphone will only pick up your voice when recording using Chrome on the same computer as the audio playback. Playback recording will also be affected, such as recording audio from YouTube or audio played on your computer speaker. All Chrome versions are affected. An example of where you may experience this issue: - Recording an online meeting using Chrome on the same computer. Otter will not record speakers in the meeting. Only your voice will be recorded. - Recording a YouTube video using Chrome on the same computer. The YouTube audio will not be recorded. - Recording audio playback such as QuickTime using Chrome on the same computer. The audio from the video will not be recorded.

Note: This does not affect any Otter recording integration or features, such as Otter Notetaker, Zoom Live Notes, or Zoom Sync. Only direct recordings on Otter using Chrome are affected. For all Zoom, Google Meet, or Microsoft Teams meetings, we recommend using Notetaker. #### Solution Disable the experimental feature in Chrome. 1. Open Chrome. 2. In the address browser, type chrome://flags and hit ENTER. 3. In the search bar, type Echo Cancellation. 4. The Chrome-wide-echo-cancellation feature appears under the Available tab.

(image: echo_cancellation.png) 5. Click the drop-down and click Disabled. (image: disable_echo_cancellation.png) 6. Click Relaunch to restart your browser. 7. Verify that the experimental feature is disabled by repeating steps 2-3 and confirm that it has been disabled.(image: disabled.png)

Getting started: Otter 101

Conversation, import, and app limits on the Basic free plan

You can use the Basic free plan of Otter as long as you’d like, but there are a few usage limits to keep in mind. - Transcription limit - Up to 300 minutes per month - Transcription duration - Up to 30 minutes per transcription - File imports - Import up to three audio or video files - Conversation history - 25 most recent (older ones archived)

Transcription monthly limit

You can transcribe up to 300 minutes per month using Otter. If you exceed the transcription limit, you will not be able to record or import recordings until your next monthly cycle. #### Transcription duration limit On the Basic free plan, each conversation and import has a limit of 30-minutes. If you exceed that limit, your recording or import will not stop, but you will only be able to access up to 30-minutes of the transcription. If you upgrade to a paid plan, the full transcription will become available once you click Unlock full transcript at the bottom of the transcript. #### (image: Unlock_full_transcript.png) #### File import limit On the Basic free plan, you can import up to three audio or video files for transcription per account (not per month). If you reach the import limit, upgrade to our Business plan to import as many files as you’d like up to 6,000 minutes per month. #### Conversation history On the Basic free plan, you will see the 25 most recent conversations. Older conversations will be archived (not deleted). To access all previous conversations, upgrade to a paid plan. The paid plans offer unlimited conversation history.

Getting the most out of Otter.ai: Best Practices for in-person recordings

Otter.ai makes it easy to capture, transcribe, and share conversations. In this guide, we’ll provide some best practices for recording clear audio in person, so you can make the most of everything Otter has to offer. #### 🎙️ Recommended Microphone By default, Otter will pull from your device’s built-in microphone. The audio quality and what it captures will depend on your device’s audio input. (e.g. Recording on your iPhone will pull directly from your built-in phone’s microphone, while recording on the browser will pull from audio from your built-in computer’s microphone). You have two options to record using Otter for in-person meetings or conversations. - Record through Otter on any computer using any browser (Chrome recommended) - Record on a mobile device (phones or tablets) using our Otter iOS or Android app

If you are recording a specific event, scenario, or multiple speakers, we recommend the following types of microphones - Microphone Type: Description: Example Scenario - Uni-directional: Records audio coming from the direction the microphone is facing.: You are recording a lecture by a professor. Using this type of microphone, you will want to point the microphone towards them to capture their audio. - Bi-directional: Records audio coming from the front and back of the microphone.: You are interviewing a speaker. Using this type of microphone, you’ll want to position it right between you and the speaker to capture both of your audio. - Omni-directional: Records audio coming from all directions, regardless of placement.: You are at an event booth and want to capture the conversations you’re having with multiple customers. An omnidirectional microphone will be able to capture audio from all angles. We recommend positioning the microphone in the center of all speakers.

There are many types of microphones you can use. The table above highlights just a few; however, there are many more different types of microphones. We recommend researching microphones based on where the speakers may be. (image: different-types-of-polar-pattern.jpg) (image source: Polar Patterns in Microphones) #### ⏺️ Before recording a conversation 1. Ensure your device, browser, and mobile app are up-to-date

Check that your browser is up-to-date. 2. Check if your device (laptop, computer, phone, etc.) has any software updates. 3. Check the relevant Apple App Store or Google Play Store for any Otter mobile app updates. 4. Ensure you have a good internet connection Otter requires a stable internet connection to provide real-time transcription. We recommend a minimum uplink bandwidth of 512kbps. We recommend using a direct Ethernet cable if possible, but WiFi or cellular data is supported. 5. Microphone placement Place the microphone in the center of all speakers or near the speaker you are wanting to capture. Ensure the microphone is near the speakers. 6. Check your device audio output settings Navigate to your microphone settings and select the microphone name as the input.

Mac - Navigate to System Preferences > Sound > Input.(image: image.png) 7. Windows** - Navigate to Start > Settings > System > Sound > Input. (image: image (1) 1.png) 8. Do a test recording before the event **Log in to Otter.ai and click Record in the top right. (image: image (2) 1.png) Your audio should be recording now and you’ll be able to see the live transcript in real-time produced on the screen (indicated by the live transcribing and audio waveform). (image: in person recording.png)

Network interruptions

If you encounter any interruptions due to a sudden network outage or drop in bandwidth, we recommend keeping the recording going. Otter will continue to record and cache to your computer’s browser or mobile device. Once the network connection is established again, Otter will finish the upload. - Do not close the browser or mobile app during the network outage. - Continue recording and stopping the recording as normal. - Live transcript will not be available, but will be processed and transcribed once the network is connected again.

(image: network offline.png) #### FAQs #### Can I use Bluetooth headphones? - Yes. - However, please note since recording in Otter directly pulls from the microphone input, bluetooth headphones can be great for recording your own voice, but not other speakers around you. - If you’re experiencing an issue with capturing other speaker audio, please try disconnecting your headphones and record through the device’s built-in microphone (e.g. iPhone’s microphone, laptop’s microphone, tablet’s microphone, etc.)

Can I use an external Bluetooth microphone?

  • Yes.
  • However, we recommend using a microphone directly plugged into the device to prevent any sudden disconnection and interruption of the recording.

Why is the audio playback quality not the same as my microphone input?

  • Audio recorded to Otter’s server will be compressed.
  • During playback of audio, the quality may sound different from what it would be if you were recording directly as a local file on your device.

Why is the live transcription not showing?

  • Your upload internet speeds may be too low. Please check do a speed test check to you have a minimum uplink bandwidth of 512kbps.
  • You may have a VPN, extension, plugin, or application that is interfering with the recording. Please review our Known apps, browser extensions, and plugins that affect Otter article for more information.

I am using an external microphone, but it’s still not capturing any audio. What should I do?

  • Check if you have any background, noise suppressant, or noise reduction settings for your audio inputs.
  • There may be various audio programs installed on your device that contain these settings. Please try disabling them to see if the audio improves. Check out our My audio isn’t being recorded article for more info.

Invited users not receiving email to join Workspace

Users may not be receiving the email for various reasons. As an admin, check that the user’s email shows as invited in Workspace > Manage Workspace > Members.  Click Resend to attempt sending the invite email again. If the user is still not receiving the email, have them follow the workaround steps below. (image: invited member.png) #### Sign up directly (workaround) Users who have already been invited to a Workspace can sign up directly at Otter.ai and create an account. Once the account creation is completed, they’ll see the pop-up to join the Workspace from the existing invite. Have the invited user follow the steps below: 1. Sign up at Otter.ai. Use any of the 3rd party sign-ins (e.g. Google or Microsoft) or click Create Account to manually type in the email address to get started.

Note: Ensure there are no typos if manually typing in the email. If using a 3rd party sign-in option, ensure the email linked to that account matches the one invited. The account created must reflect the email that was invited. (image: create an account.png) 2. Complete the signup flow and verify the Otter account. 3. Once logged in, a pop-up will surface with the invitation to join the Workspace. Click Join now to immediately join the Workspace. (image: join now.png)

Troubleshooting email

Check the junk folder
  • Have the invited user check their junk folder.
  • If the email is there, add Otter as a safe sender to ensure emails are delivered successfully to the inbox.
Whitelist Otter.ai emails
  • We recommend having the user or your IT team whitelist Otter.ai to ensure emails are delivered successfully to the inbox.
  • Whitelist reply@otter.ai and no-reply@otter.ai.
  • Once whitelisted, an admin can resend the invitation from the Workspace > Manage Workspace > Members page.

Meeting Summary Overview

Otter provides an easy way to save time and ensure everyone is aligned after a meeting through an automated meeting summary. Based on our Automated Summary feature, Otter will send you and your calendar guests a summary of the meeting, providing a quick overview of topics, action items, highlights, and meeting slides. #### Meeting Summary Overview When you record a meeting for a calendar event synced to Otter, you and all calendar guests will receive a Meeting Summary email within 2 hours after the meeting ends. For Otter to successfully send the email, the following guidelines must be met: - The calendar event is synced to Otter on the Home page - Enable auto-share to the calendar event guest for the meeting - Record the calendar event through web, mobile, or Otter Notetaker (recommended)

The email summary can vary depending on the meeting and may contain any of the following, - General meeting info (title, dates, time, duration) - Automated Summary - Action items created during the meeting - Automated Slides presented during the meeting

Automated Summary

One of the exciting features of the Meeting Summary email is the summary section, which has been generated by Otter’s proprietary AI. This section allows all calendar guests to easily scan through the main highlights and topics of the meeting. The summary will reflect the Automated Summary on the Summary tab** within the conversation. Click View in Otter or See full summary** to access the transcript. #### Action items The Meeting Summary email provides a quick overview of any action items created during the meeting. Collaborators and calendar guests can easily review tasks, follow-ups, or actionable items that were assigned to the team in this section of the email. Click See # action items to view all action items in the conversation. #### Highlights View quick highlights generated from the outline in the conversation. Click See all insights to view the full conversation outline. #### Automated Slide Capture Images added to your conversations during the live recording through the Automated Slide Capturefunction using Notetaker will appear in your Meeting Summary email. Click See # images or on the image to view all screenshots taken during the presentation. #### Enable meeting summary emails Review the settings and info below to ensure you and your calendar guests receive the meeting summary email. ##### Notifications By default, the meeting summary is enabled for all accounts. You can change the notification settings in Account Settings > Notifications > Meeting Summary. By enabling notifications, you will receive meeting summaries for your conversations and those shared with you. ##### Share meeting summaries with calendar guests Ensure sharing is enabled for calendar event guests so that they will receive the meeting summaries emails. - Manage Default audience for shared notes auto-share settings in Account Settings > Meetings. - Manually set sharing permissions per event on the Otter homepage.

System requirements & third-party hardware

Curious about what the technical requirements are to use Otter? We’ve put together some helpful information about our supported browsers and operating systems (OS). #### Mobile apps - Operating System: Requirements - iOS: iOS 15 or above - Android: Android 5.0 (Lollipop) or above

Web browsers

Sign in to your Otter account at otter.ai using one of our supported browsers. - Browser: Requirements - Google Chrome: Version 67 or above - Mozilla Firefox: Version 59 or above - Safari: Version 15 or above - Microsoft Edge: Version 88 or above

External microphones

You can connect a microphone to your computer or mobile device’s audio input port, a USB port, using Bluetooth, or to an audio interface connected to your computer. You can also use your computer’s built-in microphone to record audio. We recommend these USB, mobile, and wearable microphones. ##### Change your browser and mobile settings If you are using an external microphone on your computer, you will need to allow permission in your browser to use your microphone when prompted. Learn about permissions in Chrome, Safari, and Firefox. When using a mobile device, grant access to Otter to access the microphone. - In iOS, go to Settings >** Privacy** >** Microphone - In Android, go to Settings** >** Apps** > Otter > Permissions > Microphone > Allow

Using a headset during a virtual meeting or class

In general, we do not recommend using a headset during virtual meetings or classes when using Otter on a web browser to record. Although headsets have an external microphone, the audio from the meeting or class plays within the headset, preventing Otter from recording and transcribing it. Instead, we recommend using Otter Notetaker for virtual meetings and classes, which allows you to use a headset. #### Third-party noise cancellation software Any noise cancellation features on your computer, mobile device, or headset may prevent Otter from picking up audio from your speakers. For the purposes of recording audio from your speakers, temporarily disable any noise cancellation features.

What is Otter?

(image: Collage.png) Taking notes during meetings or transcribing conversations can be time-consuming and distracting. Otter.ai streamlines this process by converting your voice interactions into searchable, shareable smart notes. Whether you’re in a meeting, conducting an interview, brainstorming on the go, or transcribing existing recordings, Otter.ai enhances your productivity. Additionally, you can capture photos—such as whiteboard content or speaker slides—during a recording, and Otter.ai will seamlessly integrate them into your transcripts. This comprehensive approach ensures you never miss a detail. #### Your conversations Record and transcribe conversations using Otter on your mobile phone or web browser. Then share your conversations with teammates or classmates. Learn more about recording and transcribing conversations. ##### 📤  Share conversations You can easily share conversations in Otter with anyone. When you share from Otter, you can control whether people can edit, comment on, or only view the conversation. ##### ✏️  Review and edit conversations Review and playback your conversations to catch up on anything you may have missed and collect action items. Also, edit conversations to refine the text and make it easier for you to organize and listen to the conversations. ##### 💾  Import and export conversations You can import (upload) audio (mp3, aac, wav, m4a, wma) and video (mp4, avi, mov, wmv, mpg) files with Otter for transcription. You can also export the text and audio of a conversation in a variety of formats to suit your different needs. ##### 📂 Organize your conversations To organize your conversations in Otter, create folders to make conversations easier to find and share with others. Create folders for teams, projects, office locations, or anything else that’s relevant to your organization. #### Discover the power of apps Simplify and automate your workflows by connecting your favorite apps 🤖  such as (just to name a few): - Google Calendar & Contacts for Otter - Microsoft Calendar & Contacts for Otter - Dropbox for Otter - Zoom for Otter (our most popular integration)

Connect Slack to Otter.ai

What is the Otter integration for Slack?

The Otter integration for Slack automatically shares updates with teammates in real time, ensuring everyone is in the loop. For more information on Otter and Slack, see our privacy policy. With the Otter app, you can: 1. Receive Otter notifications. Get notified when you’re mentioned or assigned an action item in an Otter conversation. 2. See rich previews of Otter conversations. When Otter links are posted in Slack, they will be instantly enriched with meeting information and the automatic summary. 3. Share meeting summaries in Slack channels. Send summaries to Slack channels after the meeting to keep everyone on the same page.

Who can use the Otter integration for Slack?

The Otter app is available for all Otter plans. Please see the Otter privacy policy. ##### Get the Otter app now (image: Add to Slack) #### How do I install the Otter integration for Slack? 1. Go to https://otter.ai/setting/connected_apps. 2. Next to Slack, click on “Add”. 3. Connect your Otter account with your Slack account. 4. If you are the first person in your Slack workspace to connect Otter, you’ll see one more step to add the Otter app to your Slack workspace.  Click on “Add to Slack”. 5. Follow the steps to install the Otter app to your Slack workspace, and you’re done!

Features of the Otter Slack Integration

  1. See rich previews of Otter conversation with link unfurls When you paste a link to an Otter conversation in a Slack channel, the Otter Slack app will unfurl that link to show more information, including meeting information such as the title and duration, and the automatic summary for that conversation.

Note: In order for the link to unfurl, you must have access to the conversation. 2. Get notifications in Slack If you connect your Slack account to Otter, you will receive Otter notifications in Slack, for these following activities:

When someone requests access to a conversation. You can grant access directly in Slack with collaborator or viewer permissions. 3. When you are** assigned an action item in Otter. 4. When you’re mentioned in a comment in Otter. **

  1. Post to Slack After connecting Slack to your Otter account, you will see a new “Post to Slack” button in the top right corner of every conversation you own.
  2. Here, you can post the meeting summary to a specific Slack channel.
  3. To post to private Slack channels, please first add the Otter app to that channel.
  4. If this Otter conversation is part of a recurring meeting, you will also see an option to set up all future instances to be posted to Slack.
  5. Connect recurring meetings to Slack channels Steps

Go to https://otter.ai/setting/connected_apps. 6. Add your Slack account, if you haven’t already. 7. Connect your calendar. 8. Below the Slack section, schedule automatic posts to Slack. 9. For any recurring meeting, choose which Slack channel(s) you want to post the pre-meeting notification and after-meeting summary.

Disconnect your calendar (Google, Microsoft, Apple Calendar)

Disconnect your Google or Microsoft calendar anytime in Otter. This article will provide instructions on how to disconnect your calendar. 1. Sign in to Otter.ai. 2. Click on Integrations on the left navigation menu. Scroll to the calendar section. 3. Click Disconnect on the right side next to the calendar you want to disconnect. (image: disconnect calendar.png)

Your calendar will be immediately disconnected. Previously synced future events will be removed, and no further new events will sync to Otter. Reconnect your calendar anytime. (image: add calendar.png) #### Disconnect your calendar on the Otter mobile app (iOS & Android) Disconnect your Google, Microsoft, or Apple calendar (iOS only) anytime on the Otter mobile app. 1. Open the Otter mobile app. 2. Tap Account and select Account Settings. (image: mobile account settings.png) 3. Scroll down to the SETUP section. Tap on Connect calendars.  4. Tap the X icon next to the calendar you want to disconnect. 5. Tap Remove connection.

Your calendar will be immediately disconnected. Previously synced future events will be removed, and no further new events will sync to Otter. Reconnect your calendar anytime. ##### Remove Apple Calendar (This Device) 1. Follow steps 1-3 above to open the Connect calendars page in Account > Account Settings > Setup. 2. Tap Syncing # calendars under This Device. 3. Tap Stop syncing this device’s Calendar.

FAQs

Notetaker is still joining my meetings after I disconnected my calendar. How do I stop that?

  • If you disconnected your calendar, Notetaker will no longer join future events.
  • If you are still experiencing Notetaker join your meetings after disconnecting your calendar, verify that no future events are showing on your calendar on the Otter homepage calendar.
  • Additionally, review your Notetaker auto-join settings in Account Settings > Meetings. Learn more about managing your Notetaker settings.

Does disconnecting my calendar remove past events?

  • Past events will still be available, allowing you to view past recorded conversations associated with the calendar event.
  • Future and new events will not be synced to Otter.
  • You can view your past events on the homepage calendar tab. Click through the various dates to view past events.  For example, the Calendar was disconnected on August 22nd. Calendar event conversations before August 22nd will still show, but future events will no longer be available or synced to Otter, as indicated by the grayed-out dates.

Does disconnecting my calendar immediately stop syncing events?

  • Yes.
  • Disconnecting your calendar will immediately remove previously synced future events and stop syncing new events to Otter.
  • You will need to reconnect your calendar to sync events to Otter.

If I disconnect my calendar, can I still use Otter Notetaker?

  • Yes.
  • You can manually add Notetaker through a meeting URL. Learn more about manually adding Notetaker to a meeting.

Does Otter offer an open API?

Yes, Otter offers a public API for Enterprise customers. To get started, contact your Otter account manager to enable access.

Dropbox for Otter

Otter’s integration with Dropbox provides automatic transcription for any video or audio file you import into Dropbox. Files added to the Otter app folder in Dropbox will be automatically sent to Otter for processing, including transcription, automated summaries, action items, outlines, speaker identification, and more. Take interviews, video footage, or a recording of a meeting, and have it transcribed automatically. - Connect your Dropbox account - Manage your Dropbox settings - Sync files from Dropbox to Otter - Import a file directly from Dropbox - Manually export a transcript to Dropbox - FAQs

Connect your Dropbox account

  1. Sign in to Otter.
  2. Click Integrations on the left navigation menu. Click Add next to Dropbox. (image: integrations dropbox add.png)
  3. Sign in to Dropbox. Click Allow on the pop-up to complete the connection.  

Note: The email address associated with your Dropbox account does not need to match the email address of your Otter account. (image: allow connection.png) 4. Your Dropbox account is now connected. You can begin syncing files from Dropbox to Otter.  (image: dropbox is connected.png)

Manage your Dropbox settings

Any time a file is uploaded to Dropbox, it’ll sync to Otter to be transcribed. Once Otter has finished processing the file, you can have it set to export the transcription back to Dropbox automatically. Manage the sync settings for the exported file format. 1. On the Integration page under Dropbox, click Modify export settings. 

Note: Uncheck the setting if you do not want to export the transcription back to Dropbox.

(image: modify export settings.png) 2. Click the drop-down format menu to select the format type: txt, docx, pdf, srt. Adjust any other output settings. Click Save when finished. (image: select format and click save.png)

All synced files from Dropbox will be exported with the same settings. (image: completed sync.png) #### Sync files from Dropbox to Otter Sync audio and video files from Dropbox to Otter to be transcribed. Learn more about the file formats that Otter supports. 1. Sign in to Dropbox. 2. Navigate to the Apps / Otter folder. (image: dropbox apps otter folder.png) 3. Upload audio or video file(s) into the Dropbox folder.

Note: Learn more about uploading files to Dropbox. (image: upload file to otter folder in dropbox.png) 4. Once uploaded to Dropbox, your file(s) will sync to Otter and begin processing for transcription, summaries, outlines, speaker tagging, and more. You can find the conversation on the Home page or in My Conversations. (image: access on home or in my conversations.png) 5. If you have the auto-export setting enabled, the transcription will be synced back to Dropbox. (image: completed sync.png)

Import a file directly from Dropbox

The Apps / Otter folder on Dropbox automates the import process; however, for files located outside that folder, you can import those files directly from Dropbox. 1. On the Otter homepage, click Import and select Dropbox. (image: import dropbox.png) 2. Select the file(s) to be imported into Otter. Click Choose. (image: select dropbox file.png) 3. The file(s) will begin to process. Once completed, the transcript can be found on the Homepage and in My Conversations.(image: imported file is completed.png)

Manually export a transcript to Dropbox

Export transcript from Otter directly to Dropbox. 1. Navigate to the conversation you want to export. Click the 3-dot menu in the upper-right corner. Select Export to Dropbox. (image: export to dropbox.png) 2. Select the format and adjust settings. Click Export.(image: export to dropbox export.png) 3. The transcript will be sent to Dropbox. (image: transcript exported to dropbox.png) 4. The exported transcript can be found in the Apps / Otter folder on the Dropbox side. (image: file is exported to dropbox.png)

FAQs

My files are not syncing from Dropbox to Otter. How do I fix or troubleshoot this?

  • Make sure the file format and size are supported. Learn more about the file formats that Otter supports for importing.
  • Make sure you have not exceeded your monthly minutes quota.
  • If you’re still experiencing issues, we recommend disconnecting and reconnecting your account on both the Otter and Dropbox sides.
  1. Save and remove all files from the Apps / Otter folder on Dropbox. This will make sure duplicates are not accidentally synced over during this process and count against your monthly minutes.
  2. Disconnect your Dropbox account in Account Settings > Integrations > Dropbox > Disconnect on Otter’s side. (image: disconnect dropbox.png)
  3. Disconnect Otter on Dropbox’s side in Settings > Apps. Expand the Otter section. Click Disconnect.  (image: disconnect otter from dropbox.png)
  4. Check Also remove Otter Voice Notes folder from my Dropbox. Click Disconnect.

Warning: Make sure you save all files from Apps / Otter before you remove the folder and disconnect. (image: remove otter voice notes folder.png) 5. Follow the Connect your Dropbox account steps to reconnect. 6. Once reconnected, try syncing one file from Dropbox to Otter.

Does importing count against my monthly minutes?

  • Yes.
  • Importing from Dropbox, both automatically and manually, will count against your monthly minutes.

What file formats are supported for importing?

  • Learn more about the file formats that Otter supports for importing.

Is there a file size limit?

  • Yes.
  • You can import files as long as they’re less than 5GB in size and don’t exceed your monthly limit. If you’re experiencing problems importing a large file, learn how to resolve errors that occur while importing.

How long does it take to process and transcribe the synced file?

  • Learn more about transcription processing times.

Can I manually bulk export conversations from Otter to Dropbox?

  • Manually bulk exporting conversations from Otter to Dropbox is not supported at this time.
  • You can only manually export to Dropbox from the conversation page.

(image: export to dropbox overview.png)

Grant Microsoft Entra Admin Consent for Otter (Sign-in, Calendar, and Integrations)

Microsoft administrators can grant organization-wide permissions for Otter using a Microsoft Entra admin consent link. This allows users to sign in with Microsoft and connect their account without triggering individual approval requests. After permissions are granted, users in the tenant can connect their Microsoft accounts to Otter without encountering permission prompts. #### ✅ Quickest setup An administrator can grant all required permissions for Otter by signing in with a Microsoft Entra Global Administrator or Application Administrator account. Learn more about Microsoft’s admin consent endpoint. Click the link below and sign in as an administrator to approve the permissions for your organization. Once permissions are granted: - Users can sign in to Otter using Continue with Microsoft - Users can connect their Microsoft calendar - Administrators do not need to approve individual user requests

Permissions requested

Otter requests the following Microsoft permissions: - Microsoft Graph: Permission: Purpose - User.Read , openid, profile: Sign in and read user profile: Allows Otter to identify the signed-in user - offline_access: Maintain access to data you have given it access to: Enables persistent authentication - Calendars.Read: Read user calendars: Allows Otter to sync calendar events - Contacts.Read: Read user contacts: Allows Otter to access calendar contacts

Enterprise Integrations Permissions Enterprise features may require additional permissions

If you are an enterprise customer using additional integrations, use the link below to grant the required permissions based on the features enabled in your workspace. #### 👉 Video Permissions Includes permissions for the feature add videos to your conversations: 🔐 Grant Microsoft admin permissions for Otter #### 👉 Microsoft Outlook Extension Permissions Includes permissions for the feature Microsoft Teams Recording Permissions: 🔐 Grant Microsoft admin permissions for Otter #### Customize requested permissions (optional) Add and remove permission scopes The admin permission link can be customized to request only specific permissions depending on how your organization plans to use Otter. This is controlled by the scope parameter in the admin permissions URL. Each permission requested by Otter is included as a space-separated value in this parameter. Some organizations prefer to grant only specific permissions depending on how they plan to use Otter. For example: - If users only need to connect their Microsoft calendar, administrators can approve only the calendar-related permissions. - If users will use additional integrations, administrators can approve the full set of permissions.

These permissions can be customized by adjusting the scopes included in the admin consent link.

--- Customize permissions after [**&scope=**] --- <https://login.microsoftonline.com/organizations/v2.0/adminconsent?client_id=7caa06af-66c7-4db4-95c6-aedae793935a&redirect_uri=https%3A%2F%2Fotter.ai%2Fsignin&scope=openid%20profile%20offline_access%20https%3A%2F%2Fgraph.microsoft.com%2FUser.Read%20https%3A%2F%2Fgraph.microsoft.com%2FCalendars.Read%20https%3A%2F%2Fgraph.microsoft.com%2FContacts.Read>

FAQs & Info

Can I just click ‘Grant admin consent’ in Entra?

No. Clicking Grant admin consent in Microsoft Entra will not grant all of the permissions required for Otter. To ensure all required permissions are granted, administrators should use the Otter admin permission link. #### Do we have full control over who is able to connect their Microsoft account to Otter? Yes. Administrators have full control over which users can access Otter and connect their Microsoft accounts. After granting admin permissions, you can configure the Otter enterprise application in Microsoft Entra to require user or group assignment. This allows you to restrict access to only specific users or groups within your organization. - Users who are assigned to the application will be able to sign in and connect their accounts - Users who are not assigned will not be able to access Otter or complete the connection

This ensures that access and integrations are fully controlled through your existing Microsoft Entra user and group management. #### What if I want to grant only sign-in permissions but block other permissions? If you prefer to restrict user consent and manage permissions centrally in Microsoft Entra, you can grant only the required sign-in permissions using this link. #### Can I remove permissions anytime? Yes. Administrators can revoke permissions at any time in Microsoft Entra. Go to Enterprise Applications → Otter → Permissions to revoke permissions. #### How do I troubleshoot “Approval required” when users connect their Microsoft account? If users see an “Approval required” message when attempting to sign in with Microsoft or connect their calendar, it typically means your Microsoft Entra tenant requires administrator approval before applications can access organizational data. - User consent for applications is restricted - Only verified applications can be approved - Applications requesting certain permissions require admin approval

👉 An administrator can grant the required permissions for the entire organization using the Otter admin consent link. Once permissions are granted, users will no longer see the “Approval required” prompt. Users can sign in and connect their Microsoft accounts without additional approval. #### Do I need to change any of my existing Microsoft Entra policies or access settings? No. You do not need to modify your existing Microsoft Entra policies, settings, or approval workflows to use the Otter admin link. The admin link simply allows a Microsoft Entra administrator to approve the required Otter permissions for the organization. This approval works alongside your existing tenant configuration and does not override your current security policies. After permissions are granted, administrators can continue to manage access using their existing Entra controls, including: - User or group assignments - Conditional Access policies - Application access settings

If you want to limit who can use Otter within your organization, you can do so by managing group assignments or user access in Microsoft Entra for the Otter enterprise application. In most cases, administrators can grant permissions using the link and leave all other Entra settings unchanged. #### Do I need an Otter account as a Microsoft Entra admin? No. If you are a Microsoft Entra administrator and are only responsible for granting permissions for Otter, you do not need an Otter account. You can use the admin consent link to grant the required permissions for your organization directly from Microsoft Entra. Once permissions are approved, users in your organization will be able to sign in to Otter and connect their Microsoft accounts without requiring additional admin approval. #### Users not added to the Otter application (AADSTS50105) If a user is not assigned to the Otter application under Users and groups in Microsoft Entra, they will not be able to sign in or connect their Microsoft calendar. In this case, users will see an error (such as AADSTS50105) indicating that access to the application is restricted and they should contact their administrator. To resolve this, an administrator must assign the user or group to the Otter application: Microsoft Entra Admin Center → Enterprise Applications → Otter → Users and groups This setting allows administrators to maintain full control over which users in the organization are able to access Otter and connect their Microsoft accounts.

Otter Desktop App (Mac & Windows)

Otter is available on both the web browser and desktop app (Mac & Windows), giving you flexibility in how you work. While the Otter Desktop app has all the features of the web app, it offers additional features such as meeting reminders, botless recording for meetings, automatic recording, wearing headphones during a meeting, automatically ending a recording, and an easy pause-or-end recording indicator. Choose the recording option that best fits your workflow. #### Install the Otter desktop app #### Mac Installation Install Otter Desktop on macOS 12.3 or higher 1. Download the Otter macOS Desktop app. 2. Double-click the DMG file to open it. 3. Drag the Otter.app to the Applications folder. 4. Once the Otter has been fully moved to your Applications folder, you can open the Otter macOS Desktop app. We recommend pinning to the dock for easy access. 5. Sign in to or create your Otter account. 6. When you first open the Otter desktop app, you will need to enable access for permissions. Follow the prompts on the screen to enable access. Restart the app after enabling permission settings. For more detailed information, view our permissions section. 

Microphone - Allows Otter to transcribe the audio input from your microphone. 7. Screen & audio recording - Allows Otter to capture what’s on screen and what meeting participants say (this setting allows you to wear headphones during a meeting while transcribing meeting audio). 8. You are now logged in to Otter and can use all of the features available on the desktop app.

Enable microphone permissions on Mac
  1. Navigate to System Settings → Microphone.
  2. Toggle ON Otter. ****
  3. Select Later (if you need to enable the other settings) or Quit & Reopen.
  4. Open Otter.
Enable screen & system audio permissions on Mac
  1. Navigate to System Settings → Screen & System Audio Recording.
  2. Toggle ON Otter.
  3. Select Later (if you need to enable the other settings) or Quit & Reopen.
  4. Open Otter.

Windows Installation Install Otter Desktop on Windows

  1. Download the Otter Windows Desktop app.
  2. Run otter_latest.exe to begin the installation process.
  3. Once the installation has completed, open the Otter Desktop app (if not already open). We recommend pinning the application to the dock for easy access. Sign in to Otter.
  4. After signing in, you may need to enable access for permissions. Review your microphone permissions settings if you are experiencing any issues recording.
  5. You are now logged in to Otter and can use all of the features available on the desktop app.
Recording permissions

Navigate to system settings. Permission locations may vary depending on your Mac or Windows device. Otter will need the following permissions depending on your device: - Microphone - Allows Otter to transcribe the audio input from your microphone. This includes headphones, external microphones, Bluetooth headsets, etc. Whichever microphone device your audio input is set to, Otter can record from it. - Screen & audio recording - Allows Otter to capture what’s on screen and what meeting participants say. This will allow you to wear headphones during the recording, and Otter will still be able to record what’s on your screen, such as a YouTube video playing in the background, or speakers during an online meeting (e.g., Slack, Google Meet, Zoom, Microsoft Teams, etc.).

🖥️ Botless Recording for your meetings

In the Otter desktop app, whenever you start a new recording, you can record audio from a meeting without needing Notetaker to join. You can wear headphones and record the audio from your device (e.g., the sound that would come out of your speaker, such as people speaking during an online meeting or a YouTube video playing in the background). The Otter desktop app supports** botless recording** for supported meetings without Notetaker joining as a meeting participant. This provides a more seamless meeting experience while still capturing transcripts, summaries, and other Otter features. ✅ Notetaker does not need to join the meeting. ✅ Wear headphones during a meeting. ✅ Otter can automatically detect, start, and stop a recording for a meeting. Click Record in the upper-right corner of the Otter desktop app to start a recording. #### 🖥️ Recording indicator A small recording indicator will appear overlaid above your applications When you record a conversation in the desktop app, you can continue using your computer as usual. When you navigate away from the Otter desktop app, a small recording indicator will appear overlaid above your applications. Learn more about managing the Otter recording indicator. You can drag and move the Otter controls anywhere on the screen. Easily manage your live recording using the provided controls. - Finish Recording - End the Otter live recording and return to the conversation in the Otter desktop app. - Pause - Pause the recording. - Resume Recording - If paused, you can resume the recording at any time. - Open Otter - Open the live Otter transcription during the recording.

🖥️ Notification reminders Real-time notifications for meeting reminders, recording prompts, and errors

The Otter desktop app provides real-time notifications for meeting reminders and recording prompts, and alerts you to any errors. The notification will pop up in the upper-right corner and automatically disappear. Learn more about managing your notification settings. #### 🖥️ Huddle and meeting notifications Get notified to record when a live meeting is detected Otter can automatically send you a notification to record when a live meeting is detected. Learn more about managing your meeting settings notifications. - Slack huddles - Zoom meeting - Browser meetings (e.g., Google Meet) - Microsoft Teams meeting - Dialers - Messaging apps (e.g., WhatsApp)

When you see the notification, click Start recording. Otter will immediately start transcribing the meeting. Otter can automatically detect when a meeting ends and stop the recording. If you wish to continue recording after a meeting has finished, click Keep recording when you see the notification. When using Notetaker, if Otter detects that it was not able to join the meeting, it’ll prompt you to manually record the meeting. Click Record manually to start. #### 🖥️ Error notifications Get alerted for any issues that may arise during a recording The Otter desktop app can alert you to issues that may arise during a recording. - Slow network connection: Check your network connection. Keep the Otter desktop app open. Otter will automatically start uploading and transcribing the file once a connection is detected. - No network connection: Check your network connection. Keep the Otter desktop app open. Otter will automatically start uploading and transcribing the file once a connection is detected. - Otter couldn’t join your meeting: Check our Notetaker troubleshooting guide. Ensure that the host has allowed Notetaker to join the meeting and that Otter has permissions to record. - No conversation detected in the past 10 minutes: Otter will stop recording in 15 seconds. Click keep recording to continue.

FAQs

Can I deploy the Otter desktop app using MDM?

Yes. Otter supports deployment through Mobile Device Management (MDM) solutions, allowing IT administrators to install and manage the Otter desktop app across their organization at scale. #### Can I record a Slack huddle with Otter? Yes. Otter can record Slack huddles, including while you’re wearing headphones. Otter can automatically detect a Slack huddle and prompt you to start recording. You can also start a manual recording at any time. #### Are my account settings reflected across web, desktop, and mobile? Yes. Changes you make to your account settings are automatically synced across the web, desktop, and mobile versions of Otter when you’re signed in to the same account. #### How do I check for new updates? You can check for and install updates directly from the desktop app. 1. Click the Otter menu in the upper-left corner of your screen. 2. Select Check for Updates. ****

Click the Otter drop-down menu in the upper left corner of your device. If there is an update, you can click Install Latest Version.  **** You’ll be alerted if you are on the latest version. If you can’t update from within the app, download the latest version from our Downloads page and replace the existing application in your Applications folder after completely quitting Otter. #### Can I record meetings without an AI bot? Yes. With the Otter desktop app, you can use botless recording to capture supported meetings without an AI bot joining as a meeting participant, while still generating transcripts and other Otter AI features. #### Does the Otter desktop app support botless recording? Yes. The Otter desktop app supports botless recording for supported meeting platforms, allowing you to capture meetings without an AI bot joining. #### What is botless recording? Botless recording allows you to record and transcribe supported meetings without an AI bot joining as a meeting participant. You can use the Otter desktop app to record without a bot or Notetaker joining your meeting.

Integrations: CRM Platforms Integrations

Connect HubSpot with Otter.ai

Set up the HubSpot Integration

Connect HubSpot with Otter.ai to automatically sync meeting conversations, AI insights, and customer context with your HubSpot CRM. The Otter.ai HubSpot integration helps teams keep Contacts and Deals meeting notes up to date so sales and customer teams always have the latest conversation insights in one place. #### Integration overview Once connected, Otter will automatically sync conversations to HubSpot if: - The conversations are recorded from a calendar event* - The calendar event has an external guest (i.e., guests outside your Otter workspace domain) - The contact exists in HubSpot

*Conversations can be recorded with Notetaker (recommended) or by manually starting a recording and selecting the calendar event. Seamlessly manage and see all Otter conversations directly in HubSpot. Some of the highlighted features include: - Searching and accessing your Otter conversations directly in HubSpot. - Searchable meeting title that is consistent across Otter and HubSpot. - One-click link to view the full Otter conversation. - Automatically associate Otter conversations with relevant HubSpot contacts and opportunities.

⚙️ Connect HubSpot to Otter

Connect your HubSpot account to start syncing your conversations. Completing these steps will only sync your account and Otter conversations to HubSpot. 1. Click Integrations, then click Add next to HubSpot. 2. Sign in to your HubSpot account, select your account, and click Choose Account to continue. 3. Review the pop-up and click Connect app to proceed. 

Note: The HubSpot UI may change over time. Refer to the pop-up prompts and permission requirements within HubSpot for the most up-to-date information. 4. HubSpot is now connected to your Otter account.

What is synced over?

Easily review important details generated by Otter on the Contacts or Deals page. - Meeting conversation title - Date and time of the meeting conversation - Participants of the meeting - One-click link to the conversation to use Otter Chat, preview, playback, and edit the transcript - Customizable insights through meeting templates

Search for Otter conversations Easily find Otter conversations in HubSpot

Using HubSpot global search, you can easily find the Otter conversation linked to the meeting, contact, and opportunity. Type your meeting keywords into the search bar, then click the desired result to view the Otter insights and conversation URL. #### Deals & contacts View Otter insights and link in HubSpot The Otter conversation is available on both the Contacts and Deals pages. #### Required permissions & shared data Learn more about the required permissions to connect Otter requires certain permissions to connect to HubSpot, and the following data will be synced between the two platforms. Learn more about the permissions and shared data in the HubSpot marketplace. You can disconnect your account at any time in Account Settings → Integrations → HubSpoton Otter’s side, or remove the integration through your HubSpot account. #### Uninstalling HubSpot You can uninstall HubSpot anytime You can uninstall Otter.ai from your HubSpot account at any time. Uninstalling or disconnecting will not affect your existing Otter account or data—only new Otter meeting data will stop syncing to HubSpot. ##### Disconnect on Otter’s side Follow the steps below to disconnect your HubSpot account from Otter. 1. Sign in to your Otter account. Click on Integrations. Click Disconnect next to HubSpot. 2. Review the pop-up and click Yes, disconnect to confirm. 3. HubSpot is disconnected from your Otter account.

Uninstall on HubSpot’s side

Refer to HubSpot’s documentation on uninstalling an app. #### FAQs #### What happens to my data when I uninstall or disconnect HubSpot from Otter? If you uninstall the HubSpot integration or disconnect your HubSpot account from Otter.ai, all previously synced meeting data will remain in both your HubSpot and Otter accounts. Existing notes, insights, Contacts, Companies, and related CRM records will not be deleted. New Otter meeting data will no longer sync to HubSpot. You will need to reconnect your HubSpot account to resume syncing meeting data. #### Why didn’t my meeting conversation sync to HubSpot? For a meeting conversation to sync from Otter.ai to HubSpot, the meeting must be associated with a calendar event that includes at least one external guest. Otter.ai uses meeting participants’ email addresses to determine whether a meeting qualifies for syncing. If at least one participant has an email address outside your company’s domain, the meeting is considered eligible to sync to HubSpot. Only eligible calendar-based meetings will sync to HubSpot automatically. Auto-joined meetings (e.g., “Meetings where I am the host”) — Meetings will be automatically recorded and evaluated to determine whether they are eligible. Manually joined meetings (e.g., “Meetings I manually select”) — Users must select the associated calendar event when starting a recording to ensure the meeting is evaluated correctly. #### How do I sync manually recorded meetings to HubSpot? To sync a manually recorded meeting to HubSpot, you must select the associated calendar event when starting the recording in Otter.ai. This ensures the meeting can be evaluated correctly for syncing eligibility. 1. Click Record in Otter.ai. 2. Select the associated calendar event before starting the recording.

Otter.ai uses meeting participants’ email addresses to determine whether a meeting qualifies for syncing. If at least one participant has an email address outside your company’s domain, the meeting is considered eligible to sync to HubSpot. Only eligible calendar-based meetings will automatically sync to HubSpot. #### Why don’t I see the option to connect my HubSpot account? If you do not see the option to connect your HubSpot account in Otter.ai, it is usually due to one of the following reasons: 1. Your workspace reached the maximum number of HubSpot connections allowed for your plan. The limit on the number of HubSpot accounts that can be connected within a workspace varies by the Otter plan. Once the limit is reached, additional members will not see the option to connect their accounts. For more information about HubSpot connection limits and plan availability, view our pricing page. 2. Your workspace is on Enterprise, and the admin has already connected HubSpot for the entire workspace. When HubSpot is connected at the workspace level, individual members cannot connect their own HubSpot accounts. Contact your workspace admin if you need changes made to the integration. This only applies to Enterprise workspaces.

Connect Salesforce with Otter.ai

Set up the Salesforce Integration

Integrate Salesforce with Otter.ai to automatically sync conversation data with your CRM. Connect meeting insights from Otter.ai to the right Opportunities, Contacts, and Leads in Salesforce, so your team always has up-to-date context. #### Integration Overview Once connected, Otter will automatically sync conversations to Salesforce if: - The conversations recorded from a calendar event* - The calendar event has an external guest (i.e., guests outside your Otter workspace) - The contact exists in Salesforce

*Conversations can be recorded with Otter Notetaker (recommended) or by manually starting a recording and selecting the calendar event. #### ⚙️ Connect Salesforce to Otter Connect your Salesforce account to start syncing your conversations. Completing these steps will only sync your account and Otter conversations to Salesforce. 1. Click Integrations, then click Add next to Salesforce. 2. Sign in to your Salesforce account. Review the pop-up and click Allow to proceed.

Note: Salesforce UI may change over time. Refer to the pop-up prompts for the most up-to-date information. 3. Salesforce is now connected to your Otter account.

Only conversations you record that include at least one external calendar guest whose email domain differs from your workspace domain will be synced to Salesforce through this connection. #### What is synced to Salesforce? Learn more about what is synced to Salesforce The following Otter conversation elements will be synced to Salesforce to help expedite your post-meeting workflow: - Otter conversation name is populated in both Otter and Salesforce for easy lookup - Owner of the conversation - Date of the meeting - One-click link back to the Otter conversation - Participant(s) of the meeting conversation - Insights of the conversation: Custom Meeting Type Templates

Salesforce Overview View Otter insights and conversation link in Salesforce

Otter will sync Insights and meeting notes to the appropriate Opportunity, Contact, and Lead in Salesforce. Review synced Otter conversations: 1. In Salesforce, open a Contact, Opportunity, or Lead record. 2. Under the Activity tab, locate your meeting Task. The Task name matches the Otter conversation name for easy lookup.

Note: A new Task is created when Otter syncs the Insights to Salesforce. 3. Click the Task to view the synced meeting details and Insights.

Uninstall Salesforce You can uninstall Salesforce anytime

You can uninstall Otter.ai from your Salesforce account at any time. Uninstalling or disconnecting will not affect your existing Otter account or data—only new Otter meeting data will stop syncing to Salesforce. Follow the steps below to disconnect your Salesforce account from Otter. 1. Sign in to your Otter account. Click on Integrations. Click Disconnect next to Salesforce. 2. Review the pop-up and click Yes, disconnect to confirm. 3. Salesforce is disconnected from your Otter account.

Integrations: Zapier <> Otter Integration

Connect Otter to Zapier

Connecting Otter.ai to Zapier unlocks a new level of automation and efficiency. With seamless integration, you can easily connect Otter to your other favorite tools, automating repetitive tasks and streamlining workflows. Use Zapier to import audio files to Otter, or use Zapier to export Otter’s transcripts, summaries, action items, and more to other apps. Check out all the premade recipes to quickly get started on Zapier for free. #### Zapier setup Follow the steps below to connect your Otter account to Zapier. 1. Go to Zapier directly, or in Otter, go to Account Settings → Integrations, then click Connect next to Zapier. 2. Create a new Zap. Under Account, click Sign in. If you have already created an API key, skip tostep6.

Note: Make sure Otter.ai is selected as the App before signing in. 3. Go to the Account Settings → Developer. 

Note: The Developer tab may not be available on all plans. If you don’t see it, access it directly at otter.ai/setting/developer. 4. Click Create key.**** 5. An API key will be generated. Click Copy and save your API key in a secure location.

Note: Your API key is only displayed once and can’t be viewed again. If you lose it, you’ll need to delete the existing API key and generate a new one. 6. Paste your API key and click Connect. 7. Your Otter account is now connected to Zapier. Continue building your Zap by selecting an event and configuring your workflow.

Delete and regenerate your API key Regenerate your API key if you’re running into any issues

If you are experiencing issues or can’t remember the code, you can delete and regenerate the API key. 1. Go to Account Settings → Developer. Click the three-dot menu next to the API key you want to delete, then select Delete. 2. Carefully review the pop-up details and click Delete Key to confirm. 3. Click Create key to generate a new API key.  ****

What can I create with Zapier?

Connect Otter with thousands of apps to automate your workflows. Otter currently supports the following Zapier events: - New Recording — Trigger a workflow whenever a new recording is created in Otter. - Import New Recordings into Otter — Automatically import recordings from another app into Otter.

Browse Otter’s premade templates in Zapier to quickly build workflows without starting from scratch. #### FAQs #### What can I automate with Zapier? Zapier lets you connect Otter with thousands of apps to automate workflows. For example, you can: - Import recordings into Otter from other apps. - Trigger workflows when a new recording is created. - Connect Otter with productivity, storage, and collaboration tools.

Do I need an API key to connect Otter to Zapier?

Yes. You’ll need to generate an API key in Account Settings → Developer before connecting your Otter account to Zapier. #### How do I reconnect my Otter account in Zapier? If your connection expires or you revoke your API key, reconnect your Otter account by signing in again with a valid API key. Regenerate your API key if you no longer have access to your existing one. #### Which Zapier triggers and actions does Otter support? Otter currently supports the following Zapier events: - New Recording - Import New Recordings into Otter

As new integrations become available, additional events may be added. #### Why isn’t my Zap working? If your Zap isn’t running as expected: - Confirm your API key is still valid. - Make sure your Otter account is connected. - Verify the selected trigger or action is configured correctly. - Check the Zap history in Zapier for any reported errors.

If you require additional assistance connecting Otter to Zapier, check out Zapier’s Help Center or contact our Otter Support team.

Import recordings automatically to Otter using Zapier

You can import recordings automatically from other third-party platforms into Otter to be transcribed and summarized using our integration with Zapier. This guide will go over a general setup of creating a Zap between Otter and the third-party platform; however, there are a variety of workflows and setups you can create. This guide outlines some of the available options, but it is not exhaustive. Learn more about Otter’s integration with Zapier. 1. Sign in to Zapier. 2. Create a new Zap. 3. Select the trigger from the third-party platform you want to pull the recording from.

Note: This guide uses Google Drive as an example for the third-party platform; however, the steps for other platforms should be similar. Please refer to the third-party platform’s documentation for any questions you have regarding their Zapier configurations. 4. Connect and set up the third-party platform. For the trigger, select the option most related to when a new file is detected or created. This may vary per platform. 5. Configure your third-party platform to where the file will be pulled from. 6. Test the trigger. The file should pull from your selected platform. 7. For the Action, search and select Otter.ai. 8. Next, create the API key in Otter. Sign in to Otter. Click Integrations in the left navigation menu. Click API Key next to Zapier to generate the key. 9. Save and Copy the API key. 

Note: Make sure you save the API key and note it down, as it will not be shown again after this pop-up. You will have to regenerate the API key if you lose it and set up your Zaps again. 10. In Zapier, click Sign In and paste the API key in the pop-up. Click Yes, Continue to Otter.ai to complete the connection. 11. Select Import New Recordings Into Otter for the Action event field. 12. Configure the settings from the third-party platform. Ensure the settings are configured correctly to retrieve the file. We recommend using the title of the file for the name. 13. Test the Zap and click Publish when ready.

The Zap is published and will trigger every time a new file is detected on the third-party platform. (e.g., every time a new file is added to Google Drive, the Zap will import that file to Otter) #### FAQs #### Why is my file not importing into Otter? - Check that the file size does not exceed 5GB. - Check that the file type is a supported format. - On the third-party platform, ensure that the file has proper permission settings for access. - If the file’s location reflects a private link or special permission to view or access, it may not be retrievable through Zapier into Otter. We recommend grabbing the link to the file and testing it in a private/incognito browser window to see if it requires special permission to view or access. - Adjust your permission settings or configurations on the third-party platform or through Zapier.

What files can I import into Otter?

  • We support a wide range of file types, including audio and video, for seamless importing into Otter.
  • Learn more about the file types we support in our Importing an audio or video file article.

Where do I find the imported file on Otter’s side?

  • Imported files will show on the Otter homepage and in My Conversations.

How long does it take to import the file to Otter?

  • Importing a file to Otter can vary and can depend on your third-party platform’s service and Zapier’s trigger of the Zap.
  • Once a file has been successfully imported, Otter will begin processing, transcribing, and summarizing the conversation. This process can take up to the file’s duration.

Is there a file size limit for importing?

  • You can import files as long as they’re less than 5GB in size, don’t exceed your monthly limit, or exceed your recording limit per plan. If you’re experiencing problems importing a large file, learn how to resolve errors that occur while importing.
