This guide outlines some of the available options, but it is not exhaustive. Learn more about Otter’s integration with Zapier. 1. Sign in to Zapier. 2. Create a new Zap. 3. Select the trigger. 4. Search and add Otter.ai. 5. Select the Trigger event (e.g., new recording in Otter). 6. Next, create the API key in Otter. Sign in to Otter. Click Integrations in the left navigation menu. Click API Key next to Zapier to generate the key. 7. Save and Copy the API key. 

Note: Make sure you save the API key and note it down, as it will not be shown again after this pop-up. You will have to regenerate the API key if you lose it and set up your Zaps again. 8. In Zapier, click Sign In and paste the API key in the pop-up. Click Yes, Continue to Otter.ai to complete the connection. 9. Once connected, click Continue on the setup card. Click Test trigger. The most recent recordings from your Otter account should show. Click Continue with selected record to proceed if everything looks correct. 10. Set up Trello. 

Note: The following steps outline how to create a new board when a new Otter recording is created; however, adjust the configurations according to your own workflow and needs. 11. On the configure page, there are a variety of Otter elements you can select to populate into Trello. Fill out all the required and optional fields. Click Continue to proceed. 12. Test the Zap and click Publish when ready.

The Zap is published and will trigger every time a new recording is made in Otter. (e.g., every time there is a new Otter recording, a new board is populated in Trello with the Title and Summary of the Otter conversation)

Integrations: Zoom Integration

Record a Zoom meeting

Switching between apps makes it harder to stay focused. Notes, action items, and recordings end up scattered across tools, causing unnecessary context switching. Otter brings everything together in one place with multiple ways to record Zoom meetings, so you can stay focused and keep moving forward. #### Otter Notetaker (recommended) Otter Notetaker automatically joins your meeting as a participant to record and transcribe in real time,capture slides, provide powerful AI-generated summaries, and enable participants to use Otter AI Chat to gain deeper insight during the meeting. Notetaker can join meetings you host, and those you’re invited to via calendar events synced to Otter. You can also manually add Notetaker to ad-hoc meetings. Review the following articles to get started with setting up Notetaker. - Set up Notetaker to join your Zoom meetings - Connect your Calendar and Contacts to Otter - Manage Notetaker Conversation Share Settings - Manage your Notetaker settings

Other ways to record a Zoom meeting

Otter Desktop App (Mac & Windows)

The Otter Desktop app (Mac and Windows) lets you record Zoom meetings directly, without relying on a Notetaker bot. It captures meeting audio locally, supports using headphones during the meeting, and provides easy controls to pause or stop recording. With built-in meeting reminders and full access to your transcripts, the desktop app provides a flexible way to record Zoom meetings. - Install the Otter Desktop App

Otter Live Notes

Record all meetings that you are the host of, scheduled or ad-hoc. Through Zoom’s live streaming function, Otter Live Notes will record and transcribe the meeting in real time. Meeting participants can view the transcript through the automatically generated link during the meeting. - Set up Otter Live Notes

Otter Zoom Cloud Sync Recordings

If you’re simply looking to transcribe your Zoom cloud recordings to Otter, we offer a simple way to sync them to your Otter account. Once your Zoom meeting has ended and the cloud recording has finished processing, Otter automatically transcribes the audio, and the transcript is saved to your Otter account under My Conversations. - Set up Otter Zoom Sync

Set up Otter for Zoom Webinars

Otter for Zoom Webinars enables automatic transcription and note-taking for Zoom webinar sessions, creating a searchable transcript for collaboration and post-event reference. Stay focused on delivering your webinar while Otter captures key moments, insights, and follow-ups for you. #### Step 1: Schedule the webinar in Otter Make sure you have set up Otter Live Notes** before continuing. This is required for Otter to record Zoom Webinars. 1. Once Live Notes has been set up, click Settings next to Zoom on the Integration page. 2. Click on the Webinar tab. 3. Fill out the webinar information. Click Generate codes**.

Fill out the title of the webinar conversation 4. Set the start time of the webinar 5. Optionally share to a Channel 6. Copy down the webinar codes.

Step 2: Schedule the webinar in Zoom

  1. Log in to the Zoom web portal.
  2. Schedule a webinar.
  3. Once the webinar has been scheduled, navigate to the Live Streaming page. Click Configure custom streaming.
  4. Copy the streaming details from Otter to Zoom. Click Save when finished.
  5. The webinar is ready to be recorded by Otter.

Step 3: Start the recording during the webinar

Follow these steps when the webinar begins to start recording in Otter. 1. Start the Zoom webinar. 2. Click More, select Livestream, then choose Live on Custom Live Streaming Service. 3. Otter will begin setting up the custom live stream and redirect you to the Otter conversation page.

Note: Recording will start automatically at the scheduled start time. 4. Once the webinar begins, Otter will start recording and transcribing in real-time.

FAQs

How do I stop recording?

  • In the Zoom webinar, click Livestream and choose Stop live stream. Otter will stop recording.

Why don’t I see the Webinars tab in Otter?

  • Please try using this direct link to access the webinars tab: https://otter.ai/zoom-connection-settings/live-notes/webinar
  • Contact our Otter Support Team if you do not see the Webinars tab.
  • This feature does require the Otter Business planor higher.

I forgot the webinar codes. Where can I find the streaming key info again?

  • Hover and click the key icon on the Otter webinar page to view the webinar codes again.

Do I need to set up Otter Live Notes?

  • Yes. Make sure you have set up Otter Live Notes** before continuing**. This is required for Otter to record Zoom Webinars.

Set up Otter Live Notes

Otter Live Notes integration provides real-time transcription and note-taking during your meetings, capturing conversations as they happen so you can stay focused and engaged. Live Notes generates a live transcript with highlights and action items, making it easy to follow along, collaborate in the moment, and review key points immediately after the meeting. Otter Live Notes requires setup at both the Zoom admin level and the individual user (meeting host) level. If you are not a Zoom admin, share this article with your Zoom administrator so they can complete the required admin configuration. Once the admin setup is complete, each meeting host must finish a one-time personal setup. - Zoom Admin: Completes the admin setup once for the Zoom workspace - Meeting Hosts: Complete personal setup once per Otter user account

Zoom Admin Setup (Required) Completed once by a Zoom administrator

Follow the 2 steps below to complete the admin setup for your Zoom workspace. ##### Step 1: Pre-approve Otter in the Zoom Marketplace 1. Sign in to the Zoom App Marketplace with a Zoom administrator account. Search for Otter.ai in the Zoom Marketplace, then click Approve. 2. In the pop-up, select All users on your account, check I’d like to authorize this app on behalf of some or all of these approved users, then click Confirm. 3. Select All users, review the requested app permissions and data access, then click Allow to grant organization-wide access.

Note: The Zoom authorization page and permission prompts may change over time as Zoom updates its interface or Otter introduces new features. Before approving the app, you can review the most up-to-date permissions and data access in the App Info section of the Otter app in the Zoom App Marketplace.

Step 2: Enable live streaming settings for your Zoom workspace (Admin)
  1. Go to the Zoom web portal. Under Admin,** **navigate to Account Management → Account Settings → Meeting. The following settings should be reflected. 

Allow livestreaming of meetings: Enabled 2. Custom Live Streaming Service: Checked(optional) Type Managed by Otter.ai into the field

Meeting Host Setup Zoom (Required) Each user (meeting host) will need to set up their Personal settings in Zoom

Follow the 2 steps below to complete the meeting host setup. Each Otter user will need to complete these steps. ##### Step 1: Enable live streaming settings for your Zoom (Personal) 1. Log in to the Zoom web portal. 2. Under Personal, navigate to Settings > Meeting. Click on In Meeting (Advanced) and find Allow livestreaming of meetings. The following settings should be reflected.

Allow livestreaming of meetings: Enabled 3. Custom Live Streaming Service: Checked(optional) Type Managed by Otter.ai into the field

Step 2: Connect your Otter account to Zoom

With all of the Zoom steps completed, you can now connect your Otter account and use Live Notes to capture all your meeting conversations. 1. Sign in to your Otter account. 2. Click Integrations. Next to Zoom, click Add. Follow the steps to connect your Zoom account. 3. Once connected, toggle on More Zoom integrations.** Select Otter Live Notes**.

Starting a Zoom meeting

Now that you have Live Notes enabled, whenever you start a Zoom meeting you host, Otter will immediately start recording and transcribing it in real time. Click OK once the live streaming begins. (image: 14) meeting is being streamed.png) You’ll see the Custom Live Streaming Service with a link to the Otter conversation. (image: custom live streaming.png) #### FAQs #### Are participants notified that they are being recorded through Live Notes? - Yes. - By default, Zoom will display a notice to meeting participants that the meeting is being live-streamed. The participant will be prompted to accept or leave the meeting. Learn more about Zoom’s recording prompts.

Can I record a Zoom Webinar?

  • Yes. Once you have Otter Live Notes set up, follow our Set up Otter for Zoom Webinar guide to record your conversations.

Set up Otter Zoom Sync

The Otter Zoom Sync integration automatically syncs and transcribes your Zoom cloud recordings, producing a post-meeting transcript for collaboration, editing, and summary reference.  Stay engaged in the meeting and let Otter take notes for you. Otter Zoom Sync requires setup at both the Zoom admin level and the individual user (meeting host) level. If you are not a Zoom admin, share this article with your Zoom administrator so they can complete the required admin configuration. Once the admin setup is complete, each meeting host must finish a one-time personal setup to enable syncing for their own meetings. - Zoom Admin: Completes the admin setup once for the Zoom workspace - Meeting Hosts: Complete personal setup once per Otter user account

Zoom Admin Setup (Required) Completed once by a Zoom administrator

Follow the 2 steps below to complete the admin setup for your Zoom workspace. ##### Step 1: Pre-approve Otter in the Zoom Marketplace 1. Sign in to the Zoom App Marketplace with a Zoom administrator account. Search for Otter.ai in the Zoom Marketplace, then click Approve. 2. In the pop-up, select All users on your account, check I’d like to authorize this app on behalf of some or all of these approved users, then click Confirm. 3. Select All users, review the requested app permissions and data access, then click Allow to grant organization-wide access.

Note: The Zoom authorization page and permission prompts may change over time as Zoom updates its interface or Otter introduces new features. Before approving the app, you can review the most up-to-date permissions and data access in the App Info section of the Otter app in the Zoom App Marketplace.

Step 2: Enable Cloud Recording settings for your Zoom workspace (Admin)
  1. Go to the Zoom web portal. Under Admin,** **navigate to Account Management → Account Settings → Recording & Transcript. The following settings should be reflected. 

Cloud recording: Enabled 2. Record audio-only files: Checked 3. Cloud recording downloads: Enabled 4. Only the host can download cloud recordings: Unchecked 5. Allow cloud recording sharing: Enabled 6. Require users to authenticate before viewing cloud recordings: Disabled 7. Require passcode to access shared cloud recordings: Disabled 8. Set recording as on-demand by default: Disabled 9. Prevent hosts from accessing their cloud recordings: Disabled 10. IP Address Access Control: Disabled

Meeting Host Setup Zoom (Required) Each user (meeting host) will need to set up their Personal settings in Zoom.

Follow the 2 steps below to complete the meeting host setup. Each Otter user will need to complete these steps. ##### Step 1: Enable Cloud Recording settings in Zoom (Personal) 1. Log in to the Zoom web portal. 2. Under Personal, navigate to Settings > Recording. The following settings should be reflected. 

Cloud recording: Enabled 3. Record audio-only files: Enabled 4. Automatic recording: Enabled (recommended) 5. Record in the cloud: Enabled (recommended) 6. Allow cloud recording sharing: Enabled 7. Require users to authenticate before viewing cloud recordings: Disabled 8. Require passcode to access shared cloud recordings: Disabled 9. IP Address Access Control: Disabled 10. Set recording as on-demand by default: Disabled

Step 2: Connect your Otter account to Zoom

With all of the Zoom steps completed, you can now connect your Otter account and use Zoom Sync to capture all your meeting conversations. 1. Sign in to your Otter account. 2. Click Integrations. Next to Zoom, click Add. Follow the steps to connect your Zoom account. 3. Once connected, toggle on More Zoom integrations. Select Cloud Sync Recordings.

Cloud Sync Overview

Now that you have Zoom Sync enabled, ensure you are recording to the cloud when you start a Zoom meeting. Otter will sync that recording post-meeting and transcribe it. You’ll find the conversation in Otter once it has finished processing. #### FAQs #### Are there settings I need to change in Zoom? - Yes. Both the admin and all meeting hosts (users) need to enable the correct settings. Admin steps - Meeting hosts (users) steps

Why isn’t my recording syncing to Otter?

  • Check whether the recording appears in your Zoom Cloud Recordings. If it does not show up there, we will not be able to sync the recording to Otter.
  • Ensure both Zoom Admin Setup and Meeting Host Setup Zoom steps are completed.
  • If you continue to experience issues, contact our Otter Support Team.

How do I resolve an error?

  • Please try disconnecting and reconnecting Zoom in Integrations > Zoom.
  • Ensure both admin and meeting hosts enable the correct settings. Admin steps
  • Meeting hosts (users) steps

Using Otter Live Notes

Otter Live Notes provides real-time transcription for Zoom meetings you host, making conversations more accessible and easier to follow. Once enabled, Otter automatically captures spoken content during your meetings so you, your participants, and collaborators can stay engaged, review key points, and reference the transcript as the meeting happens. #### Managing the automatic live transcription As the meeting host, you can start, stop, or restart the live transcription at any time. #### Start the Otter live transcription When you start a meeting you host, a recording indicator appears in the upper-right corner with a link to the Otter conversation. Zoom will also notify all participants that the meeting is being recorded. #### Stop the Otter live transcription If there are parts of the meeting you don’t want recorded, the host can stop the live transcription at any time. There are multiple ways to stop the transcript. - Expand the Otter.ai (click here to open live transcript) menu and click Stop. - Click Livestream and select Stop Live Stream.

Restart the live transcription

If you stop live transcription, you can restart it at any time during the meeting. Transcription will continue in the same conversation URL for the remainder of the meeting. No new conversation will be created. Click Livestream and select Live on Custom Live Streaming Service. #### Access the live transcript All meeting participants can access the live transcript through the link. Expand the Otter.ai (click here to open live transcript) menu and click the link. #### Mobile Otter Live Notes also works on the Zoom mobile app. Starting a Zoom meeting on iOS or Android will prompt the live transcript. All meeting participants will be notified by Zoom that the meeting is being livestreamed for Otter Live Notes. **** Hosts and participants can open the Otter Live Notes transcription by tapping Live and then tapping the provided URL. **** #### Known Limitations - For participants using Zoom on Chromebooks, there is no red LIVE icon. As a workaround, the host can copy the link to Otter Live Notes and paste it into Zoom’s chat, so all participants can access it there. - Otter Live Notes does not work in breakout rooms. As a workaround, you can utilize [[NOTETAKER]] to send to a breakout room, or one person in each breakout room can do a side-by-side recording.

FAQs

Who can stop the Otter Live Notes live stream recording?

  • Only the Zoom host and co-hosts can stop the Otter Live Notes live stream. This is due to Zoom’s permission model, which restricts live streaming controls to hosts and co-hosts only. Otter follows Zoom’s access and permission rules and cannot override these settings.

Can I stop the live stream from Otter’s side?

  • No. The live stream must be stopped by the Zoom host or co-host directly in Zoom. This is a limitation of Zoom’s permissions, and Otter cannot stop or control the live stream independently.

Meeting settings & recordings: Calendar meeting events

Add Otter meeting notes to Google calendar events

Add Otter meeting notes directly to your Google Calendar events using the Otter Chrome Extension. Easily access your Otter conversation directly from the calendar event and manage sharing permissions. Calendar guests can access the meeting notes to stay in sync through the pre-populated URL. 1. Install the Otter Chrome Extension. 2. Create a Google Calendar event. 3. Click Add Otter meeting notes.

Otter will create a meeting conversation with the URL pre-populated in the calendar event description. During the live recording and post-meeting, calendar guests can access the conversation through the meeting notes link. #### Manage your sharing permission settings You have the option to share the Otter meeting notes. 1. Click the Share to drop-down. 2. Select the audience to share with.

Calendar event guests: Shares to all event guests in the Google calendar invite. 3. Filter to find and select an existing Otter Channel. 4. Create a new Channel directly in the pop-up.

Create a new Channel

  1. Type your Channel name in the Create new channel field.
  2. Click Create.
  3. Set your audience permission level for Collaborator or Viewer.

The Channel will be immediately created. The Otter meeting notes will be shared in that Channel once the meeting recording starts. #### Set your conversation permission levels 1. Click on the permission level drop-down. 2. Select your option.

This will set the permission level for the audience you are sharing with. Learn more about managing your default sharing permissions. #### Automatically have Otter Notetaker join your meeting When adding a valid Google Meet or Zoom meeting link, Notetaker can automatically join your meeting to record and transcribe the conversation. Learn more about Notetaker: - Otter Notetaker Overview - Set up Otter Notetaker to join your Zoom meeting - Manage your Otter Notetaker settings

FAQs

Does “Add Otter meeting notes” work with recurring meetings?

  • Yes.
  • Otter will sync over recurring meeting events. Learn more about managing your recurring meeting events in Otter.
  • The meeting notes pre-populated URL will be the same for every event in the recurring meeting series and will include /mt in the URL to reflect a meeting conversation (e.g., otter.ai/mt/abc123).
  • During the live recording, click on that link to open the Otter conversation page for the meeting.

How do I add the meeting notes without sharing with anyone?

  • Manage your default audience and sharing permissions in Account Settings > Meetings. Learn more about managing your meeting settings in Otter.
  • Click the X icon next to the sharing options to remove any conversation sharing when adding Otter meeting notes.

Choose which meetings Otter Notetaker records

Otter [[NOTETAKER]] can automatically join all calendar events to record and transcribe meetings. If you prefer to manually choose which meetings Otter records, you can disable auto-join and select individual calendar events for recording. #### Turn off auto-join 1. Sign in to Otter.ai. 2. Go to Integrations > Meetings, then click the Default auto-join settings drop-down menu. 3. Select Meetings I manually select. 4. Only meetings you toggle on auto-join for will be recorded. Make sure to review your calendar events.

Note: If you previously changed the auto-join setting for specific calendar events, those selections will remain unchanged. Only calendar events that have not been modified will follow the updated default meeting settings.

Manage your calendar events

  1. Go to the homepage calendar.
  2. Click the Meetings or Calendar tab to review your auto-join settings for each event. Turn auto-join on or off as needed for individual calendar events.

Note: To ensure Otter Notetaker automatically joins a meeting, update the auto-join setting at least 30 minutes before the event start time. Changes made less than 30 minutes in advance may prevent Notetaker from joining successfully or on time.

Notetaker will record meetings that are set to auto-join. #### FAQs #### How far in advance should I change the auto-join setting? - To ensure Otter Notetaker joins a meeting automatically, update the auto-join setting at least 30 minutes before the scheduled start time. Changes made less than 30 minutes before the event may prevent Notetaker from joining on time or successfully.

Does this apply to recurring events?

  • Yes. Enabling auto-join for a recurring event once will enable it for the rest of the series.

Does changing the default setting affect existing meetings?

  • Calendar events that were previously adjusted manually will retain their existing auto-join setting. Updating the default auto-join setting only applies to meetings that have not been individually modified. If you previously changed settings for specific events, review those calendar entries to confirm their current configuration.

Connect and sync your Zoom calendar meetings

Connecting your Zoom calendar to Otter makes it easy to transcribe and capture key details from your meetings. Once connected, Otter Notetaker can automatically join your future Zoom calls, allowing you to experience smooth note-taking, Otter AI-powered features, and synced collaboration post-meeting. #### Connect your Zoom calendar 1. Click on Integrations in the left navigation menu. Click Add next to Zoom. 2. Sign in to your Zoom account. 3. Review the app permissions. Click Allow once you have finished reviewing. 4. Your Zoom account is now connected to Otter.

Set up Notetaker to join your Zoom meetings

With Zoom connected, you can set up Notetaker to join your meetings automatically. Follow the steps below to ensure Notetaker can seamlessly join your meetings. 1. Sign in to your Zoom account. 2. Under Personal on the left navigation menu, click Settings > Recording Tab. 3. Toggle ON** Record to computer files. 4. Checkmark External meeting participants. 5. Checkmark Auto approve their permission request.**

On Otter’s side, manage your Notetaker settings for auto-join, auto-share, and chat messages in Account Settings > Meetings. These settings will apply to all meetings that Notetaker joins. Learn more about managing your Notetaker settings. #### Manage your synced Zoom meetings On the homepage calendar, your Zoom meetings will sync to Otter. You can manage individual settings for each calendar event. Learn more about managing your calendar events. (image: zoom calendar synced overview.jpg) #### Manage recurring Zoom meetings You can manage Otter settings for recurring Zoom meetings. Click on any recurring Zoom meeting and review your settings. Any changes you make to the calendar event will apply to all future recurring Zoom meetings. Learn more about managing recurring meetings. (image: recurring zoom meeting.jpg) #### FAQs #### My Zoom meeting is not syncing. What should I do? - If you recently created the meeting on Zoom’s side and it’s not showing up in Otter, click the sync button on the homepage calendar to initiate an immediate sync. (image: resync.jpg) - Check out our Troubleshooting calendar issues article for more help.

Do I need to toggle More Zoom integrations to sync my Zoom calendar?

  • No.
  • You can leave More Zoom integrations toggled OFF. This setting is not required for syncing your Zoom meetings to Otter.
  • The More Zoom integrations setting is only required for the integrations of Otter Live Notes and Otter Zoom Cloud Sync.

My Notetaker is not joining my Zoom meetings. What should I do?

  • There are specific settings that need to be enabled on Zoom’s side after connecting your account to allow Notetaker to join your meetings.
  • Make sure you have set up Notetaker to join your Zoom meetings.

Connect your Calendar and Contacts to Otter

Connect your calendar and contacts to Otter to automatically record and summarize your meetings. Otter supports Google Calendar, Microsoft Outlook, and iOS Calendar, so you never miss an online meeting. Once connected, Otter Notetaker automatically joins scheduled events, records conversations, and generates notes you can easily share with your contacts. #### Connect your calendar account on desktop or web Connect your Google Calendar or Microsoft Outlook calendar at any time on the Integrations page in Otter. #### Google Calendar Connect your Google Calendar to Otter to automatically record and summarize your meetings. 1. Click Add next to Google and follow the steps to sign in to your account. 2. Review the permissions and click Continue to connect your Google account to Otter.     

Note: You can remove permissions or disconnect your calendar from Otter at any time. 3. Your Google Calendar is now connected to Otter. To connect more Google accounts, repeat steps 1-2.

Your calendar events will begin to sync. #### Microsoft Calendar Connect your Microsoft Outlook Calendar to Otter to automatically record and summarize your meetings. 1. Click Add next to Microsoft and follow the steps to sign in to your account. 2. Review the permissions and click Accept to connect your Google account to Otter. 

Note: You can remove permission and disconnect your Microsoft Outlook account anytime. 3. Your Microsoft Calendar is now connected to Otter. To connect more Microsoft accounts, repeat steps 1-2.

Your calendar events will begin to sync. Follow the steps to connect your calendar. 5. Your calendar(s) are now connected to Otter. To connect more accounts, repeat steps 1-4.

Your calendar events will begin to sync. ##### iOS Calendar options “This device” iOS users can connect their Apple Calendar to Otter. Tap the plus button (+) next to This device, follow the steps, and select the calendars you want to sync to Otter. ##### Android calendar options Mobile Android users will only have Google and Microsoft as options for calendar connection. #### Disconnect your calendar You can disconnect your calendar at any time. #### Connected calendar features Now that your calendar is connected, take advantage of the many features available: - Have Otter Notetaker automatically join and transcribe all of your calendar meetings - Automatically share your Otter conversations with your calendar contacts - Receive automated Meeting Summary emails after each meeting

FAQs

How to fix calendar permission issues?

If you’re experiencing calendar permission issues, such as being unable to accept permissions or needing admin approval, contact your Microsoft admin. We recommend having your admin grant the required calendar permissions to ensure a seamless connection to Otter. #### Can I connect my calendar from my on-premise server to Otter? No. Otter does not support connecting to calendars from on-premises servers, including Microsoft Exchange on-premises or other locally hosted calendar systems. #### Can I connect my on-premise Microsoft Exchange calendar to Otter? No. We do not support connecting on-premise Microsoft Exchange calendars to Otter. We only support connecting calendars attached to the Microsoft 365 Outlook online calendar. To check whether your Microsoft account can connect to Otter, sign in to Microsoft Calendar. If you can log in, you can sync your Microsoft account with Otter. If you log in and your Microsoft Exchange events are not showing up, you will need to move them to the Microsoft 365 Outlook Calendar to sync with Otter.

Manage recurring meetings

When you connect your calendar, Otter will sync and recognize your recurring meetings. Easily manage the setting once for a recurring meeting to set it for all events in that series. #### Recurring meeting overview On the Otter homepage calendar, any synced event with the cycle icon (image: refresh-3-flattened.png) indicates a recurring event. Learn more about managing your calendar events. #### (image: recurring vs single meeting event.png) #### Manage recurring meeting settings When you select or toggle a setting for a recurring meeting, it will apply to all meeting events in that series. Click on the recurring meeting to expand the events settings pop-up. You can manage various settings for a recurring event. #### (image: recurring meeting settings.png) Learn more about the settings below. - Set sharing permissions for the calendar event - Choose a Channel the conversation from that event automatically shares to - Manage the conversation URL link settings - Auto-join settings for Notetaker

FAQs

My calendar event is not syncing as a recurring meeting. How do I change that?

  • Otter will sync the recurring meeting information from your connected calendar.
  • Review how you set up the event initially at the calendar source level (Google Calendar, Microsoft Outlook, iCal, etc.).
  • Example: Google calendar event set to repeat Weekly on Tuesday (image: google calendar recurring.png)

Can I schedule a recurring meeting on Otter’s side?

  • No.
  • You must create the recurring meeting at the calendar source level (Google Calendar, Microsoft Outlook, iCal, etc.) and sync that meeting to Otter.

Manage your calendar meeting events

Once you have your calendars connected, Otter will start syncing and populating the Home page with all of your calendar events. Any event that has a valid meeting URL for Zoom, Google Meet, or Microsoft Teams, will show a toggle on the right side for Otter Notetaker. #### Manage which meetings Notetaker will join If an event is toggled on, Otter Notetaker will automatically join your meeting as a participant: (image: meeting_calendar_events.png) You can individually toggle off the meetings that you don’t want to be recorded by Notetaker: (image: otterpilot_will_not_join.png) If you change your mind, you can still add Notetaker during the meeting. On yourHomepage calendar, the event will show a Notetaker microphone icon once the meeting has started. Click the Notetaker icon to add Notetaker to your meeting. It will take a moment for Notetaker to join your meeting. (image: add_otterpilot_copy.png)

Otter is still automatically sharing conversations after I turned off the settings

Review the following options below to make sure auto-share settings are set to how you want them. #### Review your global auto-share settings for your account Review your global auto-share settings for your account. You can access the share settings by navigating to Account Settings > Meetings through the menu or through homepage calendar gear setting. (image: account settings.jpg) Review your share settings. For Default audience for shared notes settings, set it to Don’t share to turn off auto-sharing for future calendar meeting conversations. (image: share settings.jpg) #### Review your individual calendar event settings Turning off the global settings for your Notetaker settings will affect all calendar event meetings that have not been previously manually set. For individual calendar events where you previously adjusted the auto-share setting, you will need to manually go into each one and turn off auto-share. On the Otter homepage calendar, check your individual calendar events. Click on an event to bring up the settings pop-up and review Share with calendar guests, Share with channels and people, and Share with link settings. (image: review individual calendar settings.jpg)

Meeting settings & recordings: Otter Notetaker

Auto-share conversations with users of the same domain

Otter provides the option to auto-share conversations only with users who share the same Workspace domain. This setting is only available for Workspaces with non-generic domains. #### Manage the auto-share setting 1. Find all of your auto-share settings on the Account Settings > Meetings page or through the settings gear icon on the Home page calendar: (image: account settings meetings.png) 2. Click on the drop-down menu next to Default audience for shared notes. Select Internal guests. (image: default audience for shared notes.png) 3. All calendar event conversations will be automatically shared with event guests who share your Workspace domain. This setting affects all calendar events recorded manually or through Otter Notetaker.

Automatically add Otter Notetaker to your meetings

Set up Otter Notetaker to automatically join your calendar event meetings. Notetaker will automatically record, transcribe, provide a summary, and share your conversations. To have your Notetaker automatically join your meetings, you will need to connect your calendar. You have the option to connect to both Google and Microsoft accounts. #### Manage your Notetaker auto-join settings You have up to 5 settings you can manage, depending on your account. - Meetings with a video link: Notetaker will automatically join all calendar events with a valid Zoom, Google Meet, or Microsoft Teams meeting URL. - Meetings where I am the host: Notetaker will only join calendar events where you are the event organizer. - External meetings outside your domain: Notetaker will only join calendar events where at least 1 calendar guest is outside of your Workspace domain. - Internal meetings within your domain: Notetaker will only join calendar events where all calendar guests are within your Workspace domain. - Meetings I manually select: Notetaker will only join calendar events that you have manually toggled auto-join ON.

  1. Navigate to the Notetaker settings page. You can access your Notetaker settings through Account Settings > Meetings page or through the settings gear icon on the homepage calendar. (image: access settings.jpg)
  2. Next to Default-join settings, click on the drop-down and select the default auto-join option you would like to set. (image: available settings 2.jpg)

Connect your calendar

  1. Click on Apps on the left navigation menu. (image: apps.jpg)
  2. Under Connect calendars & contacts, click Add next to the calendar you wish to connect.

Note: The email address associated with your Google or Microsoft account does not need to match the email address of your Otter account.

(image: google microsoft calendar connect add.jpg) 3. If prompted, sign in to your Google or Microsoft account. 4. Click Allow (Google) or Yes (Microsoft) to enable Otter to access your account. (image: calendar permissions.jpg) 5. Repeat steps 2-4 to add multiple calendars. 6. Once connected, your calendar events will be synced to Otter. You will see your calendar events appear on your homepage calendar.

Review the meetings that Notetaker will join

Once connected, review the calendar events that Notetaker will join on your homepage calendar. Notetaker will join meetings that have been toggled on for auto-join. Learn more about managing your calendar meeting events. (image: otterpilot autojoin.jpg) #### FAQs #### Can I create a new meeting in Otter? - No. - Meetings need to be created either in your calendar or Zoom and be synced over for Notetaker to join. - For ad-hoc meetings, you can manually add Notetaker.

Change Otter Notetaker’s display name

Otter Notetaker’s name will show as ‘[Your Name]’s Notetaker (Otter.ai)’ in your meeting. You can update the [Your Name] portion inAccount Settings > General > Profile. Click Edit in the upper corner. (image: edit profile.jpg) Click into the Full name field, and update your the first name portion. Click the check mark to save and Done, once you have finished updating your name. (image: edit first name.jpg) Notetaker will pull from your first name to populate the** [Your Name]** portion. It will show as ‘[Your Name]s Notetaker (Otter.ai)’ during the meeting (e.g., Dani’s AI Notetaker (Otter.ai)). (image: otter’s notetaker.png) #### Can I change the full Notetaker’s name? It is not currently possible to individually change the full Notetaker name on the account level, but a Workspaces on an Enterprise plan can change the suffix part of the name (e.g. AI Notetaker (Otter.ai)). Contact your admin to change the Notetaker’s full name for your entire Enterprise Workspace.

Disable Otter Notetaker chat messages for meetings

During an online Zoom, Google Meet, or Microsoft Teams meeting, Otter Notetaker can send chat messages that include a link to the live summary and transcript, takeaways, action items, and Otter Chat questions and answers. You can turn off and disable these Notetaker chat messages anytime. (image: chat message.png) #### Turn off Notetaker chat messages Find all of your Notetaker settings on theAccount Settings > Meetings page or through the settings gear icon on the Home page calendar: (image: otterpilot settings overview 1.png) Under Notetaker chat messages, you have two settings you can manage. Learn more about Notetaker settings. - Send live transcript and summary - Send Otter Chat Q&A

Toggle both to OFF to disable Notetaker chat messages during online meetings. (image: turn off otterpilot chat messages.png)

How many meetings can Otter Notetaker join simultaneously?

Otter Notetaker can join multiple meetings concurrently on all paid plans. Notetaker supports overlapping scheduled meetings, meetings that start back-to-back, or meetings that run past their scheduled time. Refer to our pricing page for the number of available Notetaker that can run concurrently for your plan. The total number of concurrent Notetakers includes meetings that are joined automatically or meetings where Notetakers have been added manually. #### FAQs #### Why is my Notetaker not joining the meeting? - There may be a variety of reasons why Notetaker is not joining your meeting. - Verify that you are the primary recorder for Notetaker deduplication. Only the primary recorder’s Notetaker will join the meeting. - Review our Troubleshooting Otter Notetaker article.

Manage auto-share settings

Customize share settings for your meeting conversations. You can manage your default audience, permission level, and collaborator share settings. Find all of your Notetaker settings on the Account Settings > Meetings page or through the settings gear icon on the Home page calendar. (image: ways to access sharing settings.png) You have control over the following settings: - Default audience for shared notes - Default permission level - Allow Collaborators to share

These settings only apply to conversations recorded through a calendar event, including Otter Notetaker automatically set to auto-join an event, or by clicking record, and manually selecting the calendar event. (image: Record a calendar event.png) #### Default audience for shared notes Manage the default conversation share settings for your calendar event recordings. (image: default audience for shared notes.png) All event guests - Automatically share the conversation with all guest participants on the calendar event invite.

Same domain guests This setting will only be available for Workspace domains that are not generic (e.g., Gmail.com, Yahoo.com, Outlook.com, iCloud.com, etc.) - Automatically share the conversation with all guest participants on the calendar event invite who share your Workspace domain. - e.g., Workspace domain is otter.ai. The conversation will be shared with all guest participants on the calendar event invite with an email ending in @otter.ai, regardless of whether they have an Otter account or not.

Workspace members - Automatically share the conversation with all guest participants on the calendar event invite who are members of your Workspace. - Review the members in your Workspace in Workspace > Manage Workspace > Members.

Don’t Share - Keep meeting notes private. - Conversations recorded through the calendar event will not be shared with anyone. You will need to share your conversations manually or set the automatic sharing for each calendar event.

Default permission level

Control the permission level for shared calendar event conversations. (image: default permission level.png) Viewer: - Can only view and playback the conversation - Cannot edit, add, or view Takeaways - Export option is only available if toggled on by the conversation owner

Collaborator: - Users can edit, view, playback, and export the conversation - Users can edit, add, and view Takeaways - Users can also share the conversation if toggled on by the conversation owner

Allow Collaborators to share

(image: allow collaborators to share.png) Manage the default setting for allowing Collaborators to share a conversation. Toggling this setting On will allow Collaborators to share the conversation with other users. - Gordan recorded a calendar event - Gordan shares it with Juniper with Collaborator permission - Gordan shares it with Lyla with Viewer permission - Juniper is allowed to share the conversation with other users - Lyla is only allowed to view and playback the conversation, but not share the conversation with other users

Manage individual calendar event share settings

You can manage individual settings for each calendar event. On the Otter Homepage, manage your events through the Meeting or Calendar tab. (image: manage individual event calendar settings.png) #### Otter mobile app Access your auto-share conversation settings on the mobile app by tapping on Account > Account Settings > Meeting settings.** Setting choices will reflect across the mobile app and web browser. (image: mobile settings.png) ** #### FAQs #### Why am I missing share settings? - If you do not see all of the share settings reflected in the screenshots above, please reach out to your admin, as they may have turned off certain share settings options. - Admins can control the Workspace share settings and may disable certain share options.

Why don’t I see the ‘Workspace members’ option on the iOS or Android mobile app?

  • The ‘Workspace members’ option is not yet available on the iOS or Android mobile app.
  • This setting will be an option in future updates.
  • Users can select this option by signing in to Otter on a web browser and managing this setting in Account Settings > Meetings. The setting choice will apply for the account, but will not show on the mobile app.

(image: mobile settings.png) **

(image: default auto-join.jpg) You have up to 5 settings you can manage, depending on your account. - Meetings with a video link: Notetaker will automatically join all calendar events with a valid Zoom, Google Meet, or Microsoft Teams meeting URL. - Meetings where I am the host: Notetaker will only join calendar events where you are the event organizer. - External meetings outside your domain: Notetaker will only join calendar events where at least 1 calendar guest is outside of your Workspace domain. - Internal meetings within your domain: Notetaker will only join calendar events where all calendar guests are within your Workspace domain. - Meetings I manually select: Notetaker will only join calendar events that you have manually toggled auto-join ON.

Toggling this setting On will prompt Otter at the beginning of the meeting to send a chat message that includes a URL to the live transcript, and reminders to add agenda items or notes. Participants of the meeting can view the live transcript through the conversation link. Right before the meeting ends, Notetaker will send another message with a summary and action items.Learn more about chat messages during the meeting. ##### Send Otter Chat Q&A  Send the questions and AI-generated answers from Otter Chat directly to your meeting for all participants to view.Learn more about Otter Chat Q&A. (image: sample chat message.jpg) #### Send pre-recording emails Notify calendar event guests that your Notetaker will be joining and recording the meeting. If toggled On, Notetaker will send an email to all calendar guests 5-10 minutes before the scheduled calendar event, notifying participants that your Notetaker will be joining and recording the meeting. Below is a preview of the email a calendar guest will receive: #### Auto-capture meeting screens Notetaker can automatically capture your meeting slides through our Automated Slide Capture feature. Have the option to review both the transcript and meeting slides.

Manually add Otter Notetaker to a meeting

You can manually add Otter Notetaker to your Zoom, Google Meet, or Microsoft Teams meetings using a valid meeting URL. You can manually add Notetaker for ad-hoc meetings, meetings where Notetaker did not join successfully, or to meetings where the event is not on your Otter calendar. 1. Copy the meeting URL.

Note: Make sure your meeting URL includes the password. Notetaker will not be able to join your meeting without the embedded password. 2. Navigate to the Otter homepage and click into the Paste meeting URL to add Otter text field.  (image: manually pasting meeting URL.png) 3. Copy the meeting URL from Zoom, Google Meet, or Microsoft Teams and paste it into the field. Hit Enter/Return or click the Record button. (image: record meeting.png) 4. Notetaker will join momentarily.

It may take up to a few minutes for Notetaker to join, but once it has, you will see Notetaker in your participant list. - The meeting host may need to admit Notetaker from the waiting room. - The meeting host may need to allow Notetaker to record the meeting.

Manually add Notetaker to a live meeting event

If your calendar event meeting is not set for Notetaker to automatically join, you can still add Notetaker manually during the meeting. On your homepage calendar, the event will show a Notetaker button once the meeting has started. Click Notetaker to add Notetaker to your meeting. It will take a moment for Notetaker to join your meeting.

Notetaker is not joining my Zoom meeting

There may be many reasons why Otter Notetaker cannot join your Zoom meeting. Review this article for steps on troubleshooting Notetaker. For more troubleshooting steps, review our general Troubleshooting Otter Notetaker article. #### Otter timed out waiting for the host to admit it Notetaker will not be able to join if there is a waiting room and the host of the meeting does not admit Notetaker. Please inform the host of the meeting of Notetaker and have them admin Notetaker into the meeting. You can toggle on pre-recording emailsto notify the host before a meeting about Notetaker. (image: otter timed out waiting for the host to admit it.png) #### Zoom compliance errors Since Notetaker records audio in a meeting, per Zoom compliance requirements, a pop-up may appear asking the host to grant permission to allow Otter to record the meeting.  This pop-up only appears if users are allowed to record to their computer files is enabled in Zoom and the host is present in the meeting. If Notetaker is not able to record your Zoom meeting, you will see one of two errors in your Otter conversation: - Host disabled recording for this meeting. Refer to, or provide the host, steps to enable recording on Zoom’s side. (image: host disabled recording.png) - Host denied recording access. Notetaker was able to join the meeting, but the host denied Notetaker from recording. Notetaker is immediately removed from the meeting. Refer to, or provide the host, instructions on how to allow Notetaker to record. (image: host denied recording.png)

How to resolve Zoom compliance errors

Depending on if the host is an Otter user, please have them follow the instructions below. Zoom host has an Otter account (recommended) Have the host of the meeting log into their Otter account and follow the steps listed in our Set up Notetaker to join your Zoom meeting article. Zoom host does not have an Otter account We provide an alternative method for users to set up their Zoom account to allow Notetaker to record. This method will prompt the host to accept recording permissions each time Notetaker, so we do recommend creating an Otter.ai account connecting through the authentication process above instead to avoid the pop-up each time. Connecting through this alternative method will surface a pop-up each time in meetings for the host, while connecting through the authentication method allows you to skip this step.  (image: host allow recording.png) ##### Step 1: Enable Recording in Zoom (Required) Recording to computer files is required for Notetaker to be able to record the Zoom meeting. This setting must be enabled by the host of the meeting so Notetaker to join and record the meeting. Provide these instructions to the meeting host or enable them within your own Zoom account if you are the host of the meeting. ###### Zoom settings (Personal) 1. Sign into your Zoom account. 2. Under Personal on the left navigation menu, click Settings.  3. Navigate to the Recording tab. 

  • Toggle ON Record to computer files.
  • Select the Hosts can give meeting participants permission to record to their computer option. (image: enable zoom settings.png)

Note: If the Record to computer files option is grayed out, please contact your Zoom admin and send them the instructions for enabling the recordings setting for the entire workspace.

Zoom settings (Admin)

If you are the admin of your Zoom account, enable recordings for all users in your organization. 1. Sign into the Zoom account as an admin. 2. Under Admin on the left navigation menu, expand Account Management.  3. Click Account Settings. Navigate to the Recording** tab. **

  • Toggle ON Record to computer files.
  • Select the Hosts can give meeting participants permission to record to their computer option. (image: record to computer files - admin.png)
Step 2: Allow Notetaker to record (host instructions during the meeting)
  1. Notetaker will join the meeting at the start. If there is a waiting room, admit Notetaker into the meeting.
  2. Once Notetaker joins successfully, the host will be notified through a pop-up that Notetaker is requesting to record the meeting. The host will need to click Allow Recording. (image: host allow recording.png)

Note: Checking Apply these permissions to all future requests in this meeting will allow Notetaker to rejoin the meeting without the pop-up again for the current meeting. This setting does not apply to future meetings. The host will need to click Allow Recording for each meeting where Notetaker is joining. 3. Click OK to consent to being recorded. (image: consent to being recorded.png)

Note: Each participant in the meeting will see this pop-up and need to consent as well to being recorded. We recommend keeping your Zoom app up-to-date. If you are running an older version of Zoom, you may see this pop-up instead. (image: meeting is being recorded.png) 4. Notetaker will begin to record and transcribe in real-time.

Otter emails

If Notetaker is not able to join or record your scheduled meeting, you may receive an email with steps on how to allow Notetaker to record. Manage those notifications in Account Settings > Notifications, under Product Tips. (image: product tips.png) Additionally, the host will receive an email 24 hours before the start of the meeting with steps to allow Notetaker to record to ensure your meeting is successfully recorded. (image: email.png)

Otter Chrome extension

The Otter Chrome extension enables meeting hosts and participants to effortlessly transcribe Google Meet and Zoom meetings in real-time. #### Set up the Otter Chrome extension 1. On the Chrome Browser, navigate to the Otter Chrome extension in the web store. 2. Click Add to Chrome. (image: add to chrome.png) 3. Review the permissions and click Add extension. (image: add extension.png) 4. The Otter Chrome extension is installed.  (image: otter chrome extension has been added.png) 5. Click on the Otter Chrome extension to sign in (if not already signed in) or create an account. (image: sign in to Otter.png)

Using the Otter Chrome extension

Navigate to the Google Meet or Zoom meeting URL page in the browser when the meeting starts. Otter Chrome extension works for both scheduled calendar meetings and ad hoc meetings. Click Add AI Notetaker to add Notetaker to your meeting.  (image: zoom and google.jpg) ##### Auto join all meetings You can select Enable AI Notetaker to auto-join all other meetings as an option. This will ensure Otter AI Notetaker automatically joins all of your Zoom and Google Meetings scheduled on your calendar without having to manually add Otter each time. You can manage the auto-join setting in Account Settings > Meetings. (image: enable ai notetaker to auto join all other meetings.jpg) ##### Recording audio options You have the option to record the internal browser audio along with your microphone’s audio. (image: include microphone audio.png) ##### Provide microphone permission access If you are experiencing any issues, check that you have provided microphone access to your browser. (image: provide microphone permission access.png) #### (image: google meet.png) Google Meet 1. Click on the Google Meet link in the calendar or paste a valid meeting link in the browser URL.  (image: google calendar event.jpg) 2. On the Google Meet page, click on the Otter Chrome extension. 3. Click Add AI Notetaker to have your meeting automatically transcribed in real-time. Otter AI Notetaker will join your meeting momentarily. (image: google meet.jpg) 4. Otter AI Notetaker will join as a participant. Review the real-time transcription in the transcript or find the live conversation in My Conversations, through the in-chat links, or through the calendar event. (image: google meet otter chrome extension.gif)

Open the transcript in Otter

Click Open in App to open the full Otter transcription page. You can add more takeaways, interact with Otter Chat, add images, and more. (image: open in app.jpg) ##### Highlight Click Highlight in the lower right corner of the panel to highlight the last sentence. (image: highlight last sentence.jpg) ##### Hide or show transcription Click Hide or Show transcription anytime during the meeting. (image: show hide transcription.jpg) ##### Move the panel Click and drag the panel around using the six dot menu in the upper right corner. (image: vertical move.gif) #### Manually record on your device instead You can record in your browser instead of sending Otter AI Notetaker to your meeting. Click Record on my device instead. This option is available anytime during the Google Meet if Otter AI Notetaker is not already recording. (image: record on my device instead.jpg) Selecting this option will allow you to manually record your meeting through the browser. (image: my conversations record.jpg) You can select My Conversations if you do not want to share the conversation, or you can select a Channel to share to. (image: share with and record.jpg) #### (image: zoom.png) Zoom 1. Click on the Zoom meeting link in the calendar or paste a valid meeting link in the browser URL. (image: zoom calendar meeting.jpg) 2. On the Zoom meeting launch page, click on the Otter Chrome extension. 3. Click Add AI Notetaker to have your meeting automatically transcribed in real-time. Otter AI Notetaker will join your meeting momentarily. (image: zoom meeting.jpg) 4. Otter AI Notetaker will join as a participant. You can find the live transcript of the meeting in My Conversations. (image: otter ai notetaker joining meeting.jpg)

Note: There is no floating transcription panel for Zoom meetings. The floating transcription panel is only for Google Meet. Access your live transcription in My Conversations.

(image: youtube.png) Transcribe a video (YouTube, Vimeo, and more)

You can record and transcribe videos across various platforms. The Otter Chrome extension will capture the internal audio from the browser. 1. On the Chrome browser, open the video link (e.g., a YouTube video). 2. Click on the Otter Chrome Extension.  3. You can toggle ON or OFF the setting to include your microphone’s audio. 4. Click Start recording on the Otter extension. 5. Click play on the video. 6. Otter will begin to record and transcribe the video’s audio.

(image: recording a YouTube video.png) #### FAQs #### Does the Otter Chrome extension work with Microsoft Teams or Webex? - The Otter Chrome extension only supports meeting links associated with Zoom or Google Meet at this time

Do I have to use Chrome?

  • Yes.
  • You will need to use Chrome to utilize the Otter Chrome extension.

Does the Otter Chrome extension support any other languages?

  • Yes.
  • Otter supports transcribing in Spanish, French, or English (US or UK). Learn more about supported languages.
  • Set your language in Account Settings > General before the meeting to transcribe.
  • We hope to add more languages in the future.

What should I do if I am experiencing an error with the Otter Chrome extension?

  • For errors while trying to add Otter AI Notetaker, check out our troubleshooting article.
  • If you are unable to resolve the error or you’re having trouble manually recording through the Otter Chrome extension, contact our Otter Support Team.
  • Please attach a screen recording or screenshots of the error you are experiencing to better assist our Otter Support Team with troubleshooting.

Where is the floating transcription panel for Zoom meeting?

  • The floating transcription panel is only available for Google Meet meetings at this time.
  • Otter AI Notetaker will join your Zoom meetings as a participant.
  • Review and interact with the live transcription in My Conversations during the Zoom meeting.

Where do I manage the “Enable AI Notetaker to auto join all other meetings” setting?

  • You can manage the setting on the pop-up in the extension or you can manage your auto-join settings in Account Settings > Meetings.
  • Learn more about managing Otter AI Notetaker settings.

Otter Notetaker Deduplication

When multiple people in a Workspace have their Otter Notetaker set to join their meetings, there may be many Notetakers that join the meeting. This can create multiple recordings and conversations for the same meeting. Otter Notetaker Deduplication reduces the number of Notetakers joining a meeting from the same workspace by only having one Notetaker join from the Workspace. Otter Notetaker Deduplication applies to all team members’ calendar meetings in the same Workspace. Deduplication will happen only amongst members of the same Workspace. #### Who gets chosen as the primary recorder? Deduplication will happen only amongst members of the same Workspace. If a guest on the calendar event is an Otter user but is not in the same Workspace, their Notetaker will still join the meeting. Otter will look at the calendar event guest list and see how many Workspace team members have their Notetaker set to auto-join the meeting. From that list, Otter will select a primary recorder based on various factors: - If the meeting event organizer has their Notetaker set to auto-join the meeting, they will be given priority and become the primary recorder. - If the meeting organizer’s Notetaker is not sharing with all calendar guests, and there is another Otter member who is sharing with more people, that member’s Notetaker will also be selected to join the meeting.

Example scenarios for chosen primary recorder

Example 1: Meeting event organizer - Abigail is the meeting event organizer and is sharing with all calendar guests. - ✅ Only Abigail’s Notetaker will join from her workspace.

Example 2: Shared priority  - Abigail and Dani are in the same Workspace and are attending the same meeting. - Abigail is the meeting event organizer, but is not sharing with all calendar guests. - Dani has their Notetaker sharing to all calendar guests. - ✅ Both Abigail’s and Dani’s Notetakers will join the meeting.

Example 3: Multiple Workspaces - Abigail and Dani are in Workspace #1 (Otterly) and are attending the same meeting “Cross client sync”. Abigail is the meeting event organizer, but is not sharing with all calendar guests. Dani has their Notetaker sharing to all calendar guests. - Olivia and Gustavo are in a different Workspace #2 (Beaverly) and are attending the same meeting “Cross client sync”. Olivia has their Notetaker sharing to all calendar guests. - ✅ Being the event organizer, Abigail’s Notetaker will join. - ✅ Sharing to the most calendar guests from Workspace #1, Dani’s Notetaker will join. - ✅ Sharing to the most calendar guests from Workspace #2, Olivia’s Notetaker will join.

Otter Notetaker auto-join calendar status

Easily review which meetings your Notetaker will auto-join on the calendar. ##### Primary recorder For meetings that Notetaker is set to join or where you are set as the primary recorder, you will see the Notetaker toggled as on for auto-join. ##### Deduplication For meetings where you are not the primary recorder, you will see the Notetaker toggled off and grayed out. The Notetaker toggle for all other Workspace members of that meeting will be toggled off and grayed out. The primary recorder’s profile will appear on the calendar event meeting. Hover over the profile picture to the left of the toggle to see more info on the primary recorder. #### Otter Notetaker’s display name in the meeting By default, for meetings where your Notetaker is the only one that joins, Otter will show as ‘First_Name’s Notetaker (Otter.ai)’ (e.g. Abigail’s Notetaker (Otter.ai)). You can update your account’s name anytime in Account Settings > General > Profile > Edit. ##### Enterprise users Admins in an Enterprise Workspace have the option to customize the full Notetaker name. As part of deduplication, the primary recorder’s Notetaker will show as your ‘Workspace_Name’s customized Notetaker name’ in meetings (e.g. Otterly Incorporated’s Note Transcriber) when there are multiple users’ Notetakers joining from the same Workspace. To display, follow these steps: 1. The Workspace admin can set a custom name for the Notetaker in Workspace > Manage Workspace > Settings. By default, the display name will be blank.

E.g., You can set the display name to ‘Note Transcriber’ if you want to display it as ‘Workspace_Name’s Note Transcriber’. 2. (Optional) Review your Workspace name and update if needed in Workspace > Manage Workspace.

Current limitations

  • We currently don’t support enabling Notetaker Deduplication on a per-meeting basis. Notetaker Deduplication will apply to all calendar event meetings for team members within the same Otter Workspace.
  • We currently don’t support a way to select a specific team member as the primary recorder for a meeting.

FAQs

Can I choose who the primary recorder is?

  • No.
  • We currently don’t support a way to select a specific team member as the primary recorder for a meeting.
  • Learn more about how a primary recorder is selected.

Does Deduplication apply to Otter Notetakers joining from other Workspaces?

  • No.
  • Deduplication only applies within the Workspace.
  • If multiple Otter users from different Workspaces are attending the same meeting, the primary recorder from each Workspace will be chosen and only that user’s Notetaker will join the meeting.
  • Learn more about how a primary recorder is selected.

Can I turn off Deduplication?

  • Yes.
  • To turn off this feature, please have the Workspace admin contact our Otter Support Team.
  • This request can only be processed by having the admin contact our team.

Can I fully customize the Notetaker’s name?

  • Customizing the Notetaker’s full name is only available on the Enterprise plan.
  • Learn more about customizing the Notetaker’s name.

Otter Notetaker can automatically join your Zoom, Google Meet, or Microsoft Teams meetings and transcribe your meetings in real-time. Connect your calendar, sync your events, and let Notetaker take care of the rest. With Notetaker, you get many features, including Otter Chat Q&A, Otter AI Chat with Voice, Automated Live Summary, Automated Slide Capture, and Meeting Summary. (image: notetaker overview.png) Once you connect your calendar (Google or Microsoft), your meetings will sync to Otter. Click Add next to the calendar platform you want to connect.

Note: The email address associated with your Google or Microsoft account does not need to match the email of your Otter account. (image: apps.png) 2. Sign in to your account if prompted and follow the platform’s steps to connect your account.  Repeat steps 1-2 to add multiple calendars. 3. Once connected, your calendar events will be synced to your Otter Homepage. Learn more about managing your calendar events.
