Microsoft sign-in issue | “Need admin approval”

One of the ways to sign in to Otter is through Continue with Microsoft. If you or a member of your Workspace is experiencing the error message ‘Need admin approval’ when signing in through this method, it means consent for the Otter application has not been granted. Please reach out to your Microsoft Entra administrator to resolve this and follow the suggested resolution below. (image: need admin approval.png) #### Grant users permission to consent The admin of your Microsoft Entra account can enable permissions for users to consent to applications when signing in with their Microsoft account. 1. As an admin, sign in to Microsoft Entra. 2. Under Applications, click on Enterprise applications.  3. Select Consent and permissions under Security.  (image: microsoft entra enterprise application.png) 4. Check Allow user consent for apps and click Save.  (image: user consent.png)

Users can now sign in to Otter using Continue with Microsoft. (image: continue with microsoft.png) #### Admin approval If admin approval is required for your organization, ensure Users can request admin consent to apps they are unable to consent to is toggled to Yes. This will allow users to request admin consent for Otter. (image: admin consent.png) #### Additional assistance If the solutions above do not work or can’t be implemented for your organization, please reach out to Microsoft Support for further assistance. Otter has no effect on Microsoft permissions and will not be able to provide custom consent flows.

Remove a member from a Workspace

Admin(s) can remove any user from the Workspace and revert them back to an individual Otter account. All conversations and account information will be preserved when being reverted back to an individual account. - A prorated credit will be issued to the Workspace billing account, based on the remainder of the subscription period, for the user that was removed. This credit can be applied to additional seat licenses, renewals, or purchases. Learn more about how seats work. - If the removed user previously had an active paid plan that has not yet expired, their subscription will revert to that plan. If not, the user’s subscription will be on the Basic plan.

Follow these steps to remove a user from the Workspace: 1. Sign in to Otter.ai as an admin. 2. Click on your Workspace and click Manage Workspace. (image: manage workspace.png) 3. Under the Members tab, click on the 3 dots next to the team member you want to remove from the Workspace. Select Revoke access to Workspace.(image: revoke access to workspace.png) 4. Select Remove account from Workspace. Carefully review the information on the pop-up. Click Revoke access to proceed with removing the member from your Workspace.   (image: revoke access.png) 5. The member is now removed from the Workspace.

What a removed user will see when logging in

A user recently removed from a Workspace will see a pop-up the next time they log in alerting them of the change. They will have the option to Contact Workspace Admin if they believe it’s a mistake or Upgrade to a paid plan to proceed with creating their own Workspace. (image: removed from workspace message.png) User will retain all of their data when being removed from a Workspace. Applicable limits will apply.

Using Otter: Custom Meeting Templates & Insights

Copy and share your insights

Easily share Otter AI-generated meeting Insights with your team. You can link directly to the Otter conversation or copy and paste Insights into emails, Slack messages, documents, and other collaboration tools, helping teams quickly review meetings, align on next steps, and take action after every conversation. #### Copy your insights To copy the full Insights section, click the three-dot menu and select Copy text. You can then paste the content into other platforms, documents, emails, or messages while preserving formatting and direct links to the related transcript sections. You can also copy individual insight sections. Hover over the section and click the Copy text icon. #### Share a link to your insights Navigate to the conversation containing the Insights you want to share. Click the three-dot menu and select Copy link. You can then share the link to direct users straight to the conversation and its insights. Users must be signed in to an Otter account and have shared access to the conversation as a Viewer or Collaborator to view Insights. If they do not have access, they can request access to the conversation.

Custom Meeting Type Templates

Otter.ai Custom Meeting Type Templates are powerful enhancements that make meetings and conversations more structured, customizable, and insightful. Customize the summary tab by applying an Otter-provided template or creating your own to extract key details, generate structured summaries, and automate follow-up actions and post-meeting workflows. #### Meeting Templates Overview Easily apply a template to automatically generate key details, insights, and structured summaries from your conversations. Meeting templates help streamline repeat workflows, especially for conversations where you consistently follow the same format or process. Templates are available at both the workspace and personal level: - Workspace templates – Available for all members in the workspace to use. These templates can only be created and managed by workspace admins. - Personal templates – Create your own templates for private use. Other workspace members can’t access your personal templates, but they can view the generated content if the template is applied to a shared conversation.

Meeting Template customizations

Overview Concise summary of the meeting

A concise summary of the meeting’s key details. Toggle this section on or off to show or hide it in the Summary tab when the template is applied. #### Action Items Tasks and next steps from the meeting AI-generated action items that capture tasks and responsibilities from the meeting. Toggle on or off to show or hide this section in the Summary tab. Otter automatically assigns action items, and you can add more manually at any time. #### Insights Key details and important highlights A detailed breakdown of key meeting topics and discussion points. Insights summarize meeting conversations into actionable takeaways, organize important information into categories, and highlight key points up front, typically as short topics with supporting bullet points. Toggle this section on or off to control its visibility in the Summary tab when the template is applied. ##### Add new insights to the template 1. Click New Insight at the bottom of the Insights section. 2. Fill out the Insight name and Prompt fields. Click Save when finished.

Tip: Don’t know what prompt to write? Try using Otter AI Chat to test out prompts. Once you have the desired output, you can edit or add the prompt to the template. 3. The insight will be added to the template. 4. Make sure you click Publish or Update at the top of the template when finished to save your changes.

Edit or delete the insight You can edit or delete the insight anytime by clicking the 3 dots next to the insight and selecting your action. - Edit Update the Insight name or Prompt anytime. - To apply to a conversation, select the template again in the conversation.

  • Delete the insight.

Outline Structured breakdown of topics covered

Automatically generated structured summary and a high-level overview of your meeting. Toggle ON or OFF to display or hide this section on the conversation’s Summary tab when applying the template. Click on any part of the Outline to go directly to the associated source in the transcript. #### Custom Prompt Fully customize AI output with your own prompts Full flexibility in defining the content and format you want from the meeting. Generate all types of content for post-meeting processes, such as sending an email of the meeting, translating the transcript to another language, creating the next meeting’s agenda, and much more. The possibilities are endless. Otter supports Markdown, so you can format your prompt and ensure the output reflects exactly what you need. ##### Add a Custom Prompt 1. Toggle on Custom Prompt. **** 2. Fill out the Custom Label and Prompt field. 3. Make sure you click Publish or Update at the top of the template when finished to save your changes.

Template selection rules Set conditions to automatically apply templates

Set rules to automatically apply the template to meetings that match the conditions. Set one or multiple conditions to match various meetings. ##### Template meeting conditions - Condition: Operators: Format: Description: Example - AI Classification: is: Select from dropdown: Matches meetings based on the AI-detected meeting type, such as Sales or Recruiting.: AI Classification + is + Sales - Meeting Title: is, is not, contains, does not contain: Enter text: Matches the calendar event title.: Meeting Title + does not contain + product - Meeting Host Email: is, is not, contains, does not contain: is / is not: Enter a full email address or search for a workspace member. contains / does not contain: Enter a domain, such as @example.com.: Matches the meeting host’s email address.: Meeting Host Email + is not + otter-demo@otter.ai - Meeting Participant Email: is, is not, contains, does not contain: is / is not: Enter a full email address or search for a workspace member. contains / does not contain: Enter a domain, such as @example.com.: Matches participant email addresses.: Meeting Participant Email + does not contain + @acme.com - Meeting Host Department: is, is not: Select a predefined or custom department: Matches the meeting host’s department.: Meeting Host Department + is + Operations

Actions Run actions like sharing and communications

Run actions like sharing to a channel or sending an email whenever a meeting is matched to a template. #### Create a new template You can create a meeting template for the workspace (admin only) or a personal meeting template to use for conversations. Meeting templates for the workspace are accessible to all members to use in conversations. #### 🏢 Create a new meeting template for the workspace (admin only) You must be an admin to create a template for the workspace. By default, all workspaces will include preset templates from Otter. 1. Click your profile avatar (top-left), select Manage Workspace, open the Templates tab, and click New Template. 2. Choose a pre-built template or select Blank to create your own, then click Use Template.

Tip: Don’t know where to start? Select one of the available templates to customize to your liking. 3. Customize your template, then click Publish in the upper-right corner when you’re done. 4. Your template is now available to all workspace members. In a conversation, select it from the template dropdown.

The Created by column shows who created the template, and Last edited by shows who last updated it. #### 👤 Create a personal meeting template You can create templates for your own personal use at the account level. Personal templates are private—they aren’t shared with your workspace and can’t be used by other members. If you apply a personal template to a shared conversation, all participants in that conversation will be able to view the template and any content it generates. 1. Click your profile avatar (top-left), select Account Settings, open the My Templates tab, and click New Template. **** 2. Choose a pre-built template or select Blank to create your own, then click Use Template. **** 3. Customize your template, then click Publish in the upper-right corner when you’re done. 4. The template is now available to use for your conversations or shared conversations. ****

Apply your template to a conversation

Apply your template on desktop

In a conversation, click the Template dropdown and select a workspace or personal template. The conversation will refresh each time you select a template, and the content will be regenerated automatically. If you update a template, it will be marked as Outdated. To apply the latest version, reselect it from the Template dropdown. The Summary tab will regenerate with updated content. #### Apply your template on the mobile app In a conversation, click the Template dropdown and select a workspace or personal template. The conversation will refresh each time you select a template, and the content will be regenerated automatically. #### Managing templates #### Access templates from the conversation page Access or create workspace and personal templates anytime from the Template dropdown in a conversation. Hover over a template type and select an option to manage or create templates. #### Update your template Update your template and sections at any time, then click Update at the top of the template to save your changes. #### Turn template items on and off Toggle template items on or off at any time. Only items that are turned on will be applied in the conversation. #### Reorder Click and drag the template items to reorder. #### Duplicate or Delete a template - Duplicate a template to customize or make changes without affecting the original. - Delete a template at any time.  Conversations that previously used the template will still display the generated content and template name. - Once deleted, the template is no longer available. If you apply a different template, the previously generated content will be replaced.

Click the three dots (⋯) next to the template, then select Duplicate or Delete. #### FAQs #### Can I share my personal templates? No. Personal templates cannot be shared at this time. However, if you apply a personal template to a shared conversation, all participants will be able to view the template name and the content it generates. #### Can I apply my personal template to shared conversations? Yes. You can apply a personal template to a shared conversation. All participants in the conversation will be able to view the template name and the content it generates. If another participant changes the template, you’ll need to reapply your personal template for it to be visible again. #### Can I edit the default templates provided by Otter? Yes. You can edit the default templates. If you delete a default template, it will be restored to Otter’s original preset. #### If I update my template, does it automatically regenerate the content in the conversation? No. Updating a template does not automatically regenerate content. To apply updates, reapply the template in the conversation. The content will then regenerate based on the latest version. #### If I apply a new template, what happens to previously generated content? When you apply a new template, the Summary tab regenerates with new content based on that template. Previously generated content is not saved and will be replaced. Even if you reapply the same template, the content will regenerate each time. We recommend saving any important content before switching templates. #### Can I add more than one Custom Prompt? No. At this time, only one Custom Prompt is available to be added to the template. For any other insights, details, or workflows you would like to generate, use Otter AI Chat. #### Can I add, edit, or update meeting templates on the mobile app? No. You can only apply meeting templates on the mobile app. Creating, updating, and managing meeting templates is not currently available on the mobile app. To create, edit, or manage templates, use a desktop browser. #### Can I set a default template for all conversations? No. Setting a default template for all conversations is not supported. However, you can use Rules to automatically apply templates based on specific conditions. #### Can workspace admins manage personal templates created by members? No. Workspace admins cannot view or manage personal templates created by individual users. Personal templates are private and can only be managed by the user in Account Settings → My Templates. If a personal template is applied to a shared conversation, all participants can view the template name and the content it generates. Admins can manage and view all workspace templates created by other admins in Workspace → Templates. #### Why is the Custom Meeting Type Templates feature missing from my account? The Custom Meeting Type Templates feature is available on all plans, but usage limits and capabilities may vary. See the pricing page for details based on your plan. If you don’t see this feature in your account, contact Otter Support for assistance.

Manage and edit your insights

Manage your insights using Custom Meeting Templates. Templates can be managed at the individual account level or through workspace templates available to all members. #### Manage templates 1. Manage workspace templates in Manage Workspace → Templates, or manage personal templates in Account Settings → Templates. Then select the template you want to edit. 2. Click New Insight under the Insights section. 3. Fill in your details and prompt, and click** Save. 4. Click Publish** in the upper-right corner of the template to save your changes. 5. Click the refresh icon to apply the template and regenerate the new insights. 6. Your insights will be generated when applicable.

Regenerate your insights

Insights generated from your Custom Meeting Templates can be regenerated at any time after adding new meeting data or reapplying the template. In your conversation, open the Template drop-down and select the meeting template associated with your insights. Each time you apply a new template, Otter regenerates the meeting insights and details based on that template’s structure and prompts. #### Outdated template Templates are marked as outdated when changes are made to the template. To regenerate the insights using the latest version of the template, click the refresh icon.

Using Otter: Import & export conversations

Bulk export conversations, audio, or Takeaways

Bulk export multiple conversations, conversation audio, or Takeaways with a few clicks. - Web browser export steps - Mobile export steps - FAQs

Web browser export steps

  1. Navigate to My Conversations, the Channels, the Direct Message, or the Folder where the transcripts are located, that you want to export.

Note: Bulk exporting audio is not available in Channels or Direct Messages at this time. We hope to bring this feature in future updates. You can still export the audio through the conversation page. 2. Select all or select the individual conversations you want to export by using the checkboxes.  (image: select infographic.png)

Note: The ‘Select all’ checkbox option will not show until you select at least one conversation. 3. Click the Export icon at the top. (image: select and export.png) 4. Select the format(s) and additional settings. Click Export.  (image: export.png) 5. The bulk export process will begin. You will receive an email with a link to download the ZIP file containing all of your transcripts once it’s ready. (image: bulk export email.png)

Mobile export steps

  1. Click on the icons next to the conversations to select them. (image: mobile export selection 1.png)
  2. Click the 3 dots and select Bulk export. 
  3. Select the format and continue with additional settings.
  4. Click Continue.
  5. The files will be exportable to your device.

(image: bulk_export_mobile.gif) Once you have exported, you can save the file to your local storage or export to any of the available apps in the menu. The menu options will be based on your device. Please refer to your iOS or Android device. (image: save to files.png) #### FAQs #### Can I bulk export audio in Channels or Direct Messages? - No. - Bulk exporting audio is not available in Channels or Direct Messages at this time. The export pop-up will not have the audio option in Channels or Direct Messages.  (image: audio not available.png) - We hope to add exporting audio to Channels and Direct Messages in future updates. - You can still export the audio through the conversation page or bulk export through the All Conversations page, which includes recordings that you own and ones that have been shared with you.

I clicked ‘Select all’, but it did not bulk export all conversations in my account. How do I bulk export all conversations in my account?

  • ‘Select all’ will only select the conversations loaded on the current page.  (image: select all.png)
  • To bulk export all conversations in your account, scroll down and load all conversations on the page. Once loaded, click ‘Select all’.

Create captions & subtitles for your video

Otter conversations can be easily turned into captions or subtitles by exporting your conversation as an SRT file. Captioning or subtitles are great tools for improving accessibility and providing a better viewing experience. While SRT is a widely supported format, please check that your program is compatible with SRT files. #### Create an SRT file in Otter Once your recorded or imported conversation has finished processing, you can edit the textwithin Otter before exporting. If you need to make larger edits, such as deleting or adding additional text, we recommend exporting the conversation first as an SRT file. 1. Navigate to the conversation you want to export. 2. Click the 3 dots in the upper corner and select Export. (image: export.png) 3. Make sure Transcript is toggled on and select srt as the ‘File format’. Adjust any other options you need, such as speaker names or line breaks. Select Export when ready.  (image: export_settings.png)

Your SRT file is now ready to be uploaded to your video editing program or video platform as captions or subtitles. You can preview the SRT by opening the file in a text editor. (image: preview.png) #### Upload your Otter SRT file to your video program or platform You have the option to upload your SRT file to many programs and video platforms. While SRT is a widely supported format, please check that your program is compatible with SRT files. Upload SRT file to YouTube ##### Upload SRT file to YouTube Follow YouTube’s instructions on adding captions and subtitles. 1. Import your video file to YouTube and follow their instructions for video uploads. 2. On the Video details tab, click Add in the Add subtitles section. (image: youtube_add_subtitles.png) 3. Select the first option, Upload file.  (image: youtube_import_srt_file.png) 4. Select the time option that best suits your SRT file and select CONTINUE. (image: youtube_subtitle_timing.png) 5. Locate the SRT file and upload to YouTube. Once uploaded, you have the option to edit the captions text and timestamps further. (image: youtube_edit_subtitles.png) 6. When finished, click blue Done button. (image: youtube_done.png) 7. Follow YouTube’s instructions to complete the video upload process.

Import the SRT file into Adobe Premiere ##### Import the SRT file into Adobe Premiere Follow Adobe’s instructions for importing and editing captions/subtitles in Adobe Premiere.

Export conversation audio

Export an audio file of your conversation. 1. Click the 3 dots in the top right. (image: export_.png) 2. Toggle on Audio.  (image: audio_toggle.png) 3. Click Export.  (image: export_button.png) 4. The exported file will be downloaded in an MP3 format.

Export conversations

You can export conversations, including ones shared with you, in multiple formats (audio and text). This makes it easy to reuse them in other services, such as generating closed captions or sharing via email. You have the following options you can export, in addition to your conversation transcript: - Export Highlights & Comments - Export conversation audio - Export your conversation as an SRT file for closed captioning and subtitles

Export to Text

  1. Open the conversation you want to export, click the three dots in the top corner, then select Export.
  2. Choose the file format for the text file.

Clipboard copies the text directly to your computer’s clipboard (only available when “Highlights and Comments” and “Audio” are toggled off). 3. TXT exports a plain text file with no formatting. 4. DOCX creates a Microsoft Word document. 5. PDF generates a portable document (PDF) for easy sharing and viewing. 6. SRT exports text with timestamps in SubRip Subtitle format, which can be used for video captions and is commonly supported by platforms like YouTube. 7. Choose how to name the exported file and any additional export options. A preview of the exported text is shown below the options. Show speaker names 8. Show timestamps 9. Show highlights 10. Show images 11. Combine paragraphs of the same speaker 12. Combine all paragraphs 13. Remove Otter branding (available for TXT and CLIPBOARD formats) 14. Click Export to save the conversation to your selected download location.

Export to Audio

  1. Open the conversation you want to export, click the three dots in the top corner, then select Export.
  2. Turn on Audio, then click Export to save the file to your selected download location. Audio exports are downloaded in MP3 format.

Mobile

  1. Open the conversation you want to export. Select the Transcript tab and click the three dots in the top right corner, then select Export.

Note: The export option will only show on the Transcript tab. 2. Select Export audio to download an MP3 file, or Export text to download a text-based format. 3. Tap Continue to save the file to your selected download location. ****

FAQs

Can I bulk export multiple conversations?

Yes, you can bulk export multiple conversations on our Business and higher plans. Learn more about bulk exporting conversations. #### Where do my exports download? After clicking Export on desktop or Continue on mobile, you’ll be prompted to choose where to save the file. #### I can’t find my downloaded file. What should I do? Check your browser or device settings to see where downloads are saved. You can also review your recent download history. #### Can I export to other platforms? Yes. Enterprise plans include integrations that allow exports to supported third-party platforms. #### Can I export the Summary? Yes. You can export the full conversation summary. Learn more about exporting the Summary. #### Can I export the Outline? The Outline can’t be exported as a file, but you can copy the full summary and pull out the individual Outline items. Learn more about copying the Summary. #### Can I export Action Items? Action Items can’t be exported directly, but you can copy them manually. Learn more about copying the Summary. #### Why do I only see TXT as an export option? TXT is the only export format available on the Basic plan. Upgrade to a paid plan to access additional formats. #### Why can’t I export a conversation? If the conversation was shared with you, make sure you have the correct permissions. Only Collaborators, or Viewers with export enabled, can export conversations.

Click Export to save the file to your device. (image: export.png)

The file will be exported to your browser’s default download location. (image: highlights and comments exported.png) #### Export setting not available If the conversation has no highlights or comments, the export setting will be grayed out. There will be no option to toggle on this setting. (image: highlights and comments-2.png) #### FAQs #### Can I export highlights and comments in formats other than TXT? - No. - We currently only support exporting highlights and comments in TXT format at this time. Export Summary

You can export the conversation Summary (Overview, Action Items, Insights, Outline, and Custom Prompt) at any time or easily copy each section individually. This allows you to share or paste the Summary into other tools, documents, or workflows as needed. #### Export Summary Export the full conversation summary to use in other documents, platforms, or workflows. 1. Note: The available summary options (Overview, Action Items, Insights, Outlines, Custom Prompt) depend on the applied template. Customize your template to show specific summary export options. 3. The file will be downloaded to your device.

Bulk export summaries Learn how to bulk export multiple conversation summaries

You can bulk export conversation summaries. Sharing permissions apply if exporting a shared conversation. Select the conversations, then click Export. #### Copy Summary Copy the full summary Open the conversation and click Copy summary. This copies the Overview, Action Items, Insights, Outline, and Custom Prompt, which you can then paste or share. ##### Copy and paste summary example #### Copy Action Items Copy only the action items from the summary Copy all action items by clicking the three dots and selecting Copy all action items. To copy an individual one, hover over an action item and click Copy action item.  #### Copy Insights Copy only the insights from the summary Copy all insights by clicking the 3-dots and selecting Copy text. #### Mobile Export summary is not yet available on mobile. Use the web browser or the desktop app to export the summary. The Outline doesn’t have a dedicated copy button. Use copy summary, paste it into another document, then select and copy just the Outline section. Alternatively, you can highlight any copy text. #### Can I copy only the Overview? The Overview doesn’t have a dedicated copy button, but you can click into the field to highlight and copy the text. #### Why am I missing some export summary options? The summary options available in the Export dialog are determined by the template applied to the conversation. Only summary sections that are enabled in the selected template—such as Overview, Action Items, Insights, Outline, or Custom Prompt—will appear as export options. If an expected summary option is missing, check the template settings to confirm that the corresponding section is enabled. Learn more about managing your custom meeting templates. - Overview - Action Items - Insights - Outlines - Custom Prompt

Fix problems importing files to Otter

If importing an audio or video file fails or shows an error message, you’re likely encountering a browser issue. (image: mceclip0.png) To resolve issues with your browser, try one or more troubleshooting steps. #### 1. Check your internet connection If your files are slow to import, check your internet connection. A slow connection is a common cause of errors. - Check your upload speed and ensure it matches what you subscribed to with your internet service provider (ISP). We recommend upload speeds of at least 3 Mbps. (image: IMG_4233.PNG) - Try connecting to a different network such as your mobile phone hotspot or disconnect your Wi-Fi (on mobile) to see if your files upload correctly. If you’re able to import the file when switching to a different network, it may be caused by your router settings or ISP.

2. Check your computer’s sleep setting

You can set your computer to go to sleep or hibernate after a specified duration of inactivity. However, if you are importing a large file and your computer goes to sleep or into hibernation during the import, it may disrupt the import process and cause the import to fail. When importing a large file (> 200MB), try temporarily changing or disabling your computer’s sleep or hibernate settings. ##### On a Mac Note: The steps below are from macOS Monterey. Some options may be different depending on your Mac and version of macOS. Learn about Battery preferences on a Mac. 1. Choose Apple menu (image: Apple_logo_black.svg.png) > System Preferences, and click Battery. 2. Click Power Adapter on the left.

Turn on Prevent your Mac from automatically sleeping when the display is off. 3. Turn off Enable Power Nap while plugged into a power adapter.

On Windows

Note: The steps below are from Windows 11. Some options may be different depending on your version of Windows. Learn how to adjust sleep settings in Windows. 1. Select Start (image: microsoft_windows_icon.svg) menu > Settings > System > Power & battery > Screen and sleep. 2. Under Sleep, select Never under both dropdowns.

3. Use a different web browser

Try using a different web browser (like Google Chrome or Mozilla Firebox). If you’re able to import the file when switching to a different browser, it may be caused by a browser setting. Try resetting the site settings for httpe://otter.ai or restoring your browser to its default settings. Learn about resetting site settings for Chrome, Safari, and Firefox. #### 4. Ensure your browser is compatible with Otter Check the system requirements and supported browsers to make sure you’re using a supported browser and operating system. We recommend using Chrome, but you can use another browser, such as Mozilla Firefox or Apple Safari. Try using a different browser to see if you experience the same results. #### 5. Use Otter in a private browsing window When you browse privately, your browser won’t use your cookies, site data, or browser extensions which can sometimes cause issues. Learn about private browsing for Chrome, Safari, and Firefox. If the import finishes without issues when using a private browsing window, there may be an issue with your cookie, site data, or a browser extension. - Learn how to clear cookies and site data in Chrome, Safari, and Firefox. - Try disabling some of your browser extensions/plugins, such as an ad blocker. Learn how to turn off or uninstall extensions in Chrome, Safari, and Firefox.

6. Clear your browser cache

Clear your browser cache and cookies and then try to import your files again. Learn how to clear cookies and site data in Chrome, Safari, and Firefox. #### 7. Restart your computer or device Sometimes your computer or device may act problematic, and restarting may fix it. #### 8. Reload Otter or close the mobile app If you’re experiencing internet connection issues, Otter may not have properly loaded. Reload the web page and then try to import files again. If you’re using the Otter mobile app, force close and reopen the app. Learn about closing apps on iOS and Android. #### 9. Reduce the size of your file You can import large files in Otter, but if they are too large, they might not import properly. You can import files as long as they’re less than 5GB in size and you don’t exceed your monthly limit. If you are importing a video file, export it as audio-only to reduce the file size. You can export to audio using QuickTime Player on Mac or VLC on Windows. #### 10. Update to the latest version of the Otter mobile app If you’re using the Otter mobile app, make sure it’s the latest version. Alternatively, use Otter on a desktop browser. #### 11. Check your virus scanning or security software Virus scanning software and adblockers on your local system can sometimes interfere with certain websites or actions. Make sure that this software is not blocking Otter. #### 12. Check if “Data Saver” is enabled in the Otter mobile app In the Otter mobile app, enabling Data Saver helps reduce cellular data usage by restricting imports/uploads to Otter when your device is connected to a Wi-Fi connection and the Otter mobile app is in the foreground (open and in view). When importing a large file (> 200MB), try temporarily disabling Data Saver or make sure your device is connected to a Wi-Fi connection and the Otter mobile app is in the foreground. #### Advanced troubleshooting ##### Check your firewall and proxy settings Customized firewall and proxy settings can sometimes block access to Otter. Check with your network administrator that your firewall and proxy settings are configured to allow a connection to Otter. #### Need additional help? If these didn’t fix your issue, contact support for further assistance.

Import an audio or video file

Otter lets you import audio and video files from your computer or mobile device for automatic transcription. Once uploaded, Otter processes the recording to generate a transcript, AI-powered summaries, action items, outlines, and other meeting notes. You can also apply meeting templates to imported conversations to generate additional AI content. Otter supports the following file formats: - Audio formats: Video formats - AAC (.m4p): AVI - MP3: MOV (QuickTime) - M4A (including Apple Voice Memos): MPEG - WAV: MP4 - WMA: WMV - OGG: MPG, MKV, M4P, 3GP

Import files

  1. On the homepage, click Import in the upper right and select Browse.

Plan: On the Business or higher plans, you can import directly from Dropbox.  2. Browse or drag the file you would like to import. 3. The file will begin uploading. 4. Once uploading is complete, Otter will begin transcribing the file. You can close the pop-up and continue using Otter as usual. Track the progress in the lower left corner. Learn more about processing times. 5. The transcript will be ready to view once the conversion has finished processing.

Import on mobile

You can import through the iOS or Android Otter mobile app. 1. Tap the plus (+) button in the top right and then select Import audio or video file. 2. Select your file and open it. 3. You can track the upload progress in the tracker at the top. 4. Once the file uploads, it will begin processing. Learn more about processing times. 5. The transcript will be ready to view once it’s finished processing.

Import a file from another application You can import from other apps such as Apple Voice Memo, Samsung Voice Recorder, voice app, and more

You can import audio and video files from a variety of applications and devices. The steps below use Apple Voice Memos as an example, but the process is similar for most apps. 1. Open the application you want to share from. 2. Share the file and tap the Otter app. 3. Optionally change the name, select where to share it (by default, it’s saved to My Conversations), then click Import. 4. The file will be successfully imported to Otter and will begin processing. After processing, the transcript can be viewed. Learn more about processing times.

Email notifications

You can optionally enable notifications for imported files in Account Settings → Emails** or on the mobile app. You’ll receive an email notification once the file has finished processing and is ready to view. #### FAQs #### What is the max file size I can import into Otter? You can import files up to 5 GB** in size. Your ability to import files is also subject to your plan’s monthly transcription allowance and recording limits. #### How long does it take for an imported file to process? Learn more about processing times. #### Why won’t my file import? Import issues are commonly caused by: - The file exceeds the 5 GB limit. - Your monthly transcription or recording limit has been reached. - The upload was interrupted by a poor internet connection or the device going to sleep. - The file format is not supported.

Can I close Otter while my file is processing?

Yes. Once the upload has completed, you can safely close the Otter app or browser. Processing continues on Otter’s servers until the transcript is ready. #### Why is my file taking a long time to upload? Large files may take longer to upload depending on your internet connection. During the upload, keep the Otter app or browser open and prevent your computer or mobile device from going to sleep. Interrupting the upload may cause it to fail. #### Can I import recordings from Apple Voice Memos, Samsung Voice Recorder, or other voice recorder apps? Yes. As long as the recording is saved in a supported audio or video format, you can share or upload it to Otter from most recording applications. The exact sharing steps vary by device and application. #### Can I import multiple files at once? You can drag and drop multiple supported files into Otter from your computer to import them. Each file uploads and processes independently. #### Why didn’t Otter transcribe my file accurately? Transcript quality depends on the audio quality of the recording. Background noise, multiple speakers talking at once, poor microphone quality, or low recording volume can reduce transcription accuracy. Learn more about accuracy. #### Will Otter generate a summary, action items, and an outline for imported files? Yes. Once your file has finished processing, Otter automatically generates AI-powered meeting notes, including a summary, action items, and an outline (when available). You can also apply a meeting template to the imported conversation.

Using Otter: Organize your conversations

Convert a direct message to a Channel

DMs are created when a conversation is shared with others. If you plan to share conversations long-term or you need to include more people, you can convert a DM to a Channel. #### What to expect - When you convert a DM to a Channel, conversations from the DM will be visible to any new members you add to the Channel. - Members of the DM will not be notified about the change.

Convert a DM to a Channel

  1. Click into Direct Messages and click the DM you want to convert. (image: dm_channel_name.png)
  2. Click on the DM name or the pencil icon to rename. (image: rename_dm.png)
  3. Type a new name and press Enter.
  4. The DM is converted to a Channel and will now appear under Channels. (image: renamed_to_channels.png)

Direct Messages (DMs)

Direct Messages (DMs) let you collaborate privately with one or more people in your workspace. Like Channels, DMs support conversations, recordings, and other collaboration features, but they’re only visible to the participants in the direct message. #### Direct Messages Overview You can find Direct Messages in the left navigation menu. Each direct message is listed under the name of the person you’re messaging, making it easy to switch between private conversations and channels. Conversations are listed by when they were last shared, not by date. ##### Create a new direct message Click on the plus sign next to DIRECT MESSAGES on the left menu. Select who you want to create a DM with or type their email. The name will reflect the person you created the DM with. By default, Direct Messages are private. If you convert a Direct Message into a public Channel, it becomes visible to other workspace members under Suggested Channels, where they can choose to join. Learn more about Suggested Channels. #### Convert a Direct Message to a Channel If you want to make a Direct Message (DM) visible to more people and organize it as a shared workspace, you can convert it into a Channel by renaming the direct message. #### FAQs #### Why is one of my direct messages missing? Direct Messages may not show if you converted them to a Channel. You can find converted DMs under Channels. Additionally, if a direct message channel has more than 2 members, it will no longer show in the left panel menu, but can be accessed on the direct message page. #### Can I sort conversations in Direct Messages? No. Conversations, by default, are sorted by last shared date rather than creation date. #### What is the difference between sharing with a person and sharing to a Direct Message? When sharing a conversation, you can share it directly with an individual or to a Direct Message (DM). - Share with an individual: The conversation is shared only with that person and doesn’t appear in your shared Direct Message. - Share to a Direct Message: The conversation is posted to the shared Direct Message, where all participants in the DM can view it.

To share a conversation to a Direct Message, select the DM instead of the individual user’s name.

Favorite a Channel

Add Channels to your favorites for easy access. 1. Click into the Channel you want to favorite. 2. In the upper right corner, click the star icon to favorite the Channel. 3. The Channel will show under Favorites now for easy access.

Organize conversations by Company

Automatically organized conversations by Company give your team a complete, centralized view of every meeting, call, and note tied to each customer account. Instead of searching across channels, folders, or individual meetings, Companies lets you: - View all conversations associated with a company in one place - Quickly review account history before calls, quarterly business reviews (QBRs), and renewals - Provide sales, customer success, and leadership with a shared source of truth for past discussions

Organized conversations by a company go beyond mentions in transcripts or summaries to provide direct access and deeper context. #### Access your Companies You can access your companies in three places: - Favorited Companies - The top of a conversation - The search function

How are conversations associated with a Company?

Otter looks at email domains (e.g., @acmecorp.com) in a calendar event to automatically apply the correct Company: ##### Permissions still apply The company view respects existing conversation permissions: - Users can only see the conversations they already have access to. - Company views do not override access controls; they simply organize the content that a user can already view.

AI Chat powered queries

You can also use companies—alongside channels, conversations, and folders—to filter your AI Chat queries. Select one or multiple contexts at a time. #### Bulk select conversations Select one or more conversations to share, export, or delete Bulk select conversations in the company view to share, export, or delete. 1. Go to the company view, click the company name, then select Bulk Select. 2. Select one or more conversations to bulk share, export, or delete.

FAQs

Can I manually add a Company to a Conversation?

No. Conversations cannot be manually labeled at this time, but we plan to implement this feature in future updates. Stay tuned. #### Will workspace members be able to see all workspace conversations for a Company? No. Members can see only conversations associated with a Company to which they already have access. Existing conversation sharing and workspace permissions still apply. #### Are Company views available on mobile? No. Company views are only available on desktop and web at this time.

Organize your conversations in folders

You can organize your conversations by creating folders within your Otter account to help you find them more easily. #### Folder overview Click on the Folder section on the left side navigation menu to get an overview of all your folders. You will be able to view how many conversations you have in each folder: (image: Folder_overview.png) #### Create a folder 1. On the left side navigation menu, click the plus button (+) next to Folders. (image: create_folder.png) 2. Enter a name for the folder. 3. Click Create. (image: folder_creation.gif) 4. You can begin recording, importing, or moving conversations into the folder.

Move a conversation to a folder

  1. Navigate to My Conversations.
  2. Click on the 3 dots next to the conversation and select Move.  (image: move_conversation_from_my_conversation.png)
  3. Choose a folder to move the conversation to.  (image: move_conversation.gif)
  4. (Alternate Method) You can move the conversation to a folder within the conversation.  (image: move_conversation.png)

Rename a folder

  1. Click the folder on the left side navigation menu under the Folder section.  (image: rename_folders.png)
  2. Hovering over the title, click on the title box to enter into edit mode.  (image: rename_folder.png)
  3. Enter a new folder name and click Enter/Return to save.(image: rename_folder.gif)

Delete a folder

  1. Navigate to the folder you are wanting to delete
  2. Click the 3 dots in the top right corner and click Delete Folder (image: delete_folder.png)

Using Otter: Otter AI Chat

Advanced Otter AI Chat Modes

Otter AI Chat offers two response modes — Express and Advanced — designed for different levels of reasoning, integration, and speed. Whether you’re looking for quick, high-level answers or in-depth, step-by-step reasoning, you can now switch between modes based on your needs. #### Express Mode - Optimized for speed and concise responses. - Focuses on real-time summarization and quick insights from the active meeting. - Ideal for situations where users need fast answers without deep reasoning or cross-meeting context.

Advanced Mode

  • Search – Find information across transcripts, screenshots, and past meetings.
  • Email & Calendar – Draft emails and schedule meetings.
  • Action Items – Create and assign follow-up tasks to meeting participants.
  • Recording Controls – Pause, resume, or stop the Otter AI Notetaker with a simple chat command.
  • Help – Retrieve relevant support articles from the Otter Help Center.

Change AI Chat mode

AI Chat mode can be changed anytime in collapsed or expanded mode. When querying in Advanced mode, you can click the chain-of-thought drop-down to view the thought process in real-time or to see the steps AI Chat took to generate your answer. ##### Generated example #### 👉 Try it out Ready to try out AI Chat? Below are some example queries you can ask in AI Chat. Try toggling between Express and Advanced mode and asking the same question to compare results: What are the top 5 customer requests from the last 30 calls?  Summarize 3 key market areas we need to improve in based on meetings in Q3. Generate a roadmap of features discussed in product meetings in the last month. #### FAQs #### Can I change the AI Chat mode on the mobile app? - No. - Changing the AI Chat mode is not yet available on the mobile app. - Log in to Otter on a web browser to change the chat mode.

What’s the difference between Express Mode and Advanced Mode in AI Chat?

AI Chat offers two response modes — Express and Advanced — designed for different levels of reasoning, integration, and speed. Express Mode - Optimized for speed and concise responses. - Focuses on real-time summarization and quick insights from the active meeting. - Ideal for situations where users need fast answers without deep reasoning or cross-meeting context.

  • Delivers deeper, more contextual reasoning by drawing on a broader range of data.
  • Slightly slower due to its expanded access and analysis capabilities.
  • Unlocks enhanced tools and integrations, including:

Search Tools – Search across transcripts, visual content (captured screenshots), and past meetings. - Email & Calendar Tools – Draft emails or create Google Calendar invites directly from AI Chat. - Action Items – Automatically create and assign follow-up items to meeting participants. - Recording Controls – Pause, resume, or stop Otter meeting recordings via chat command. - Otter Help Access – Retrieve relevant support articles from the Otter Help Center.

Can AI Chat query my Salesforce data?

  • Yes.
  • On the Enterprise plan, you can integrate Salesforce with Otter. Learn more about using AI Chat Salesforce.

Can I connect Otter with other AI applications?

  • Yes.
  • On the Enterprise plan, you can connect to other AI applications such as Claude, ChatGPT, Cursor, and more through Otter MCP. Learn more about Otter MCP.

Asking Otter AI Chat questions

You can ask Otter AI Chat a question or prompt anytime during the live meeting or post-meeting, as well as for any imported files. #### AI Chat prompt examples 👉 Not sure what to ask? Try selecting one of the prompts Otter provides to get started. Try out some of the prompts below or create your own prompt to ask AI Chat. We recommend being detailed and specific when writing your prompt. If the response does not quite answer your question, you can ask AI Chat to rephrase or provide more details. AI Chat will use the previous questions you asked in that conversation to build upon a new response. ##### Extract data - Summarize the last 5 meetings into key topics, add a timestamp for each topic, and summarize each topic into 3-5 bullet points. - What were the key concerns raised, by who, and what was the resolution for each if discussed in last week’s meeting? - List the speakers for the Design <> Product meeting last Wednesday with a short paragraph of each of their decisions and action items in separate bullet points. - Please summarize the key points and decisions made in November’s meetings. Include information about any action items or next steps that were discussed for the end of the year.

Generate content
  • Draft an email summary of the last 2 meetings regarding the mobile feature, listing specific action items and who they are assigned to.
  • Based on the last 3 interview meetings, list and summarize each interviewee’s strengths and weaknesses.

Generate content with Otter AI Chat

Using Otter AI Chat, you can generate a multitude of content for different audiences, platforms, and services. Save time by having AI Chat generate the content for you. Check out some of the ways you can use AI Chat in your workflow. #### AI Chat for Sales Check out how our sales rep uses AI Chat! #### Email Want to send an email to someone with information from the conversation? Ask AI Chat to generate that for you. For example, I have an interview conversation I want to share with a colleague. I can ask Otter to generate a summary and provide the main topics. Prompt: Draft an email summarizing this conversation and list out the 5 bullet points of the main topics of this interview. - Describe the action - Add in context - Set the tone of voice - Describe the format you want

Social media content

Have a conversation you want to share or promote on social media? AI Chat can easily generate multiple posts for you. Prompt: Write 3 different Twitter posts to promote this interview. #### Generate a Blog Post Meetings can contain a lot of great info you may want to share. You can have OtterChat create a blog post for you based on the conversation. Prompt: Write a half-page blog post on this conversation, with the content being split into 3 sections. #### Generate the Meeting Minutes Never worry again about taking minutes, as AI Chat is there to summarize and produce a report that can be shared with all participants. Instead of manually taking minutes, have AI Chat generate one post-meeting. Prompt: Write structured meeting minutes for this call. #### Tailor AI Chat to your needs Not quite getting the answer you need from AI Chat? Ask follow-up questions! AI Chat is able to take feedback and generate new responses. It’ll take your existing questions and chat history to generate new responses based on the feedback you provide. Check out some of the examples below. ##### Ask follow-up questions You can ask follow-up questions to tailor the reply to your needs. Some examples: - Make that longer/shorter - Add more detail about X - Add timestamps - Make the email more casual/formal

Image Enriched Conversation AI Chat

Extract details and info from your conversation images, slides, and screenshots. Summarize, pull key insights, and get quick overviews of the images from your meetings using Otter AI Chat. #### Image enriched conversation chat overview Otter AI Chat extracts key details from images added to the conversation during the live meeting or added post-meeting. Immediately query the automatic live screen captures or the images you add to the conversation. Image enriched conversation chat supports the following image formats: - PNG - JPG - GIF

Using chat to query on images

  1. Click into a conversation.
  2. On the right side panel, click on the Otter AI Chat tab.
  3. Click on the Chat option and select Advanced.
  4. Begin typing your query regarding the images in your conversations.

Tip: Use keywords related to images to make sure AI Chat extracts from your images. e.g., images, slides, photos, diagrams, screenshots, etc.

Best practices and examples

Make sure to explicitly mention keywords related to images in your query in order for it to work: - Based on the slides, what are the main focus areas for market growth? - According to the diagram shared on the screen, how does the update affect mobile users? - Describe the images uploaded to the meeting. - Summarize the topics in the screenshots regarding the sales plan. - What speakers are mentioned in the photos?

FAQs

Can I use this feature to query across all my conversations at once?

  • No.
  • Image enriched chat is not yet available to query across multiple conversations on images.
  • You will need to click into a conversation or select a conversation as context to query on screenshots within that meeting.

Is image enriched conversation chat available for the Otter mobile app?

  • No.
  • This feature is only available on the web browser at this time, as Advanced mode is not yet available for the iOS or Android mobile Otter app.
  • Make sure to use Advanced mode while on a web browser to extract info from your images.

Is there a file size limit?

  • Yes.
  • Image file sizes must be 5 MB or less.
  • We do not support image file sizes larger than 5 MB at this time.

Is there an image resolution size limit?

  • Yes.
  • Image resolution size must be less than 8000x8000 pixels.

Can I query images on older conversations?

  • No.
  • At this time, querying on images is only available for conversations recorded after the feature was enabled for your account. Any conversations containing images before the feature was enabled for your account are not eligible.

To query an older conversation, we recommend the following: 1. Save the images from the conversation. 2. Download the audio. 3. Import the file back into Otter. 4. Upload the saved images back to the conversation.

Opt out of Otter AI Chat

Otter AI Chat and Topics are part of Otter’s AI features. These features can help make your conversations more valuable by using AI. Learn more about AI Chat & Topics. Opting out of AI Chat will remove the AI Chat feature from your workspace. Opting out of AI Chat only affects the conversations owned by your workspace’s members. If an Otter user outside of your workspace shares a conversation with you and they have AI Chat enabled in their account, you will still see the AI Chat tab in that specific conversation. #### Opt out of Otter AI Chat 👉 You can opt out of AI Chat by submitting a request here.  For details on our data policy, please see our Privacy Policy and Terms of Services.

Otter AI Chat FAQs

Otter AI Chat is a powerful AI tool that provides an easy way for meeting participants to ask questions and collaborate in real-time with the transcription, all without interrupting the meeting flow. Read through the frequently asked questions (FAQs) below for AI Chat. #### Disclaimers Accuracy and reliability: While AI Chat strives to provide accurate and up-to-date information, it may occasionally generate incorrect or outdated responses. Therefore, it is important to verify any critical information obtained from this AI Chat using reliable sources. Hallucinations: It’s important to acknowledge that no vendor can guarantee no hallucinations, as they are an inherent risk with current large language models (LLM) technology stemming from the generative nature of these models. However, we have taken every precaution to mitigate this risk and ensure trustworthy outputs. Learn more about Understanding hallucinations across Otter AI Chat. No legal or professional advice: The responses generated by AI Chat should not be considered a substitute for professional or legal advice. It is always recommended to consult with qualified professionals or experts in specific fields for personalized guidance or information. Data privacy and security: AI Chat is designed to respect user privacy, and no personal information or data is stored or shared. However, please refrain from sharing any sensitive or confidential information during your interactions. User responsibility: Users are responsible for their interactions with AI Chat and any actions they may take based on the provided information. The developers and owners of this AI Chat shall not be liable for any direct or indirect damages or consequences arising from the use of AI Chat. Use at your own risk: The use of this AI Chat is at your own risk. The developers and owners of AI Chat do not guarantee the availability, reliability, or uninterrupted operation of AI Chat. #### FAQs #### Can other users see my AI Chat responses? Your AI Chat conversations are private and visible only to you. Use them to explore ideas, draft content, and review insights. If you’d like to share a response, you can manually copy and paste it. #### Do you use 3rd party services for AI Chat? AI Chat uses a combination of our internal large language models and those from different service providers. #### Does AI Chat send my data to 3rd parties? Otter only sends your prompts and transcript to 3rd parties if you interact with AI Chat by sending a question. No data is sent until a user asks AI Chat a question. #### Is my data stored with 3rd-party external providers? No. Data is not stored by any of our 3rd-party external providers. The transcript and chat history are sent to external providers when a user asks a question, but the providers do not store them. #### Is my data stored on Otter? As with Otter transcripts, Otter holds your data in secure cloud storage that achieves the security standards required for our hosted platform and our customers’ needs. Otter does not access your audio recordings unless given explicit consent for troubleshooting specific product support issues and/or the user opts in to contribute data for system improvement. Read more about our data policies on our Security & Privacy page. #### Is my data used for training with 3rd-party external providers? No. Our 3rd-party AI service providers do not train on user data or use it for training. #### How can I improve the AI model’s responses? Encouraging the model to respond can be effective. For instance, asking the model to “try your best” has yielded positive results for some users. #### Are there any other techniques to enhance AI model interactions? Aside from encouraging the model and managing conversation history, trying different phrasings or making your queries more specific can also yield better responses. #### The answer provided has content unrelated to the transcript. How does Otter handle hallucinations? It’s important to acknowledge that no vendor can guarantee no hallucinations, as they are an inherent risk with current large language model (LLM) technology stemming from the generative nature of these models. However, we have taken every precaution to mitigate this risk and ensure trustworthy outputs. #### What precautions are taken to mitigate risk and ensure trustworthy outputs? - Carefully crafting prompts that provide clear guidance to the LLM to base its responses solely on the applicable content within Otter. - Providing users with a feedback mechanism to report any incorrect responses, serving as feedback loops to continuously improve the models based on real-world data. - Leveraging LLMs’ reasoning capabilities to generate valid inferences that go beyond merely restating their training data. - Ultimately, we have robust processes to ground Otter’s LLM outputs in applicable content and enable continual enhancement through user feedback. Maintaining accuracy and trustworthiness remains our top priority.

Can the conversation history impact the AI’s responses?

Yes, the chat history plays a role in shaping the model’s responses. Deleting previous questions and responses and then rephrasing your question can sometimes lead to better outcomes. #### Do AI Chat responses contain data or information outside of my conversations? AI Chat answers questions based solely on the conversations in your account and those shared with you in channels. The context for answers is limited to the conversations, context (e.g., conversations, channels, and folders), and integrations you add to AI Chat. #### Can I opt out of AI Chat? Yes. Learn more about opting out of AI Chat. #### Can I disable the “Otter Help” feature that provides help articles instead of directly answering my questions? No. There is currently no way to disable the “Otter Help” feature in AI Chat. However, you can try these strategies to get more relevant responses: - Use specific key phrases like “based on past meetings” to guide AI Chat to use the actual conversations. - Downvote any unhelpful responses that don’t directly answer your question. This helps the AI learn which types of responses are most useful.

Otter AI Chat is not showing

Otter AI Chat may not be enabled for your account or workspace. Read through our AI Chat FAQs. If you would like to enable AI Chat for your account or workspace, please have your admin submit a request here. Once we have approval from the admin, we can enable AI Chat for the account or Workspace.

Otter AI Chat Overview

Otter AI Chat is a powerful tool that helps you ask questions, generate summaries, and get insights from your meetings and conversations. You can use AI Chat to ask about meeting content, request more details about what was said, or create summaries for emails, reports, social media posts, and more. AI Chat works across all of your conversations in Otter, including meetings shared with you. It can answer questions about past meetings, provide context from channels, and help you quickly find important information without manually searching transcripts. AI Chat is available during live meetings and after meetings end, so you can continue asking questions and generating insights at any time. ✅ Private by default Your AI Chat conversations are visible only to you unless you choose to share them. This allows you to explore ideas, draft content, and review insights privately before collaborating with others. ✅ The same chat experience everywhere AI Chat is accessed the same way across Otter, whether you’re on the Homepage, inside a Conversation, or in a Channel. ✅ Contextual answers AI Chat automatically understands the content you’re viewing and uses that context to generate more relevant responses. Whether you’re in a live meeting or reviewing a past conversation, AI Chat provides answers tailored to you. #### AI Chat sessions All AI Chat sessions are private to you across your conversations. You can start a new chat session at any time or return to a previous session to continue the conversation. AI Chat maintains context within each session, making it easy to manage conversation history and seamlessly switch between past and new chats. To view past sessions, click the menu in the upper-left corner of the AI Chat page or panel. From there, you can reopen any previous session and continue asking questions or generating insights. For any questions regarding accessing older conversations, refer to our FAQ. #### Context for AI Chat Interact with AI Chat seamlessly across all your conversations on the Homepage, in Conversation, or in Channel. Start in one conversation and end up in another anywhere within Otter. Ask questions and pull additional details anytime. Mention a conversation, channel, or folder to add context to your chat. You can use search to quickly filter and find what you’re looking for. Select the item you want to add, and it will appear next to the @** symbol in your message. For every response, you can see the sources AI Chat used to generate its answer. Click on any of the added context to view the source directly. Otter lets you add or remove conversations, channels, or folders within the same chat session, so you can refine context as you ask questions and uncover insights. Click Show thinking** to see how AI Chat generated the answer. Conversations used to generate the response appear as blue hyperlinks with the conversation title. Click the link to open the original conversation referenced in the answer. #### AI Chat modes AI Chat offers two response modes — Express and Advanced — designed for different levels of reasoning, integration, and speed. Switch between modes as needed. Learn more about AI Chat modes. #### AI Chat connectors AI Chat connectors are integrations that allow Otter to securely access external tools, files, and data sources, such as cloud storage, messaging platforms, or CRM systems. By using connectors, users can search for or create documents, pull in real-time information, and automate workflows without leaving AI Chat, making operations more powerful, context-aware, and useful for everyday work. ##### Add a connector The steps below will be similar for all connectors. You can manage and add connectors directly from the AI Chat menu or on the Integrations page. 1. Click Add next to the connector. 2. Sign in to your account and review the information. Click Allow to connect. 3. AI Chat can now pull information from connected sources. Toggle connectors on or off to control what’s included in your search scope.

Available connectors
  • Connector: Key Capabilities: What You Can Do in AI Chat
  • Google Drive: View, download, create, edit, and delete files: Search documents, summarize files, create or update content
  • Gmail: Read emails, manage settings, draft and send emails: Search inbox, summarize threads, draft or send emails
  • Jira: View and update issues (jira-work): Search tickets, review status, update or manage issues
  • Notion: Access pages/databases, respect permissions, search, take actions: Search workspace content, summarize pages, update content based on access level
Jira connector
  1. Connect by clicking Add in the connectors menu, or by clicking Connect through the Integrations** page for Jira. **
  2. Sign in to your Jira account, review the permissions, and click Accept to finish connecting your account.
  3. Jira is now connected. Make sure it is toggled on in the connectors menu before querying.

If you see this error, contact your Atlassian admin and ask them to try the following: - Allow Atlassian MCP to connect to your Atlassian site. Learn more about authorizing apps in Atlassian. - Add https://otter.ai/** to your domain allowlist. 

Learn more about adding trusted domains in Atlassian. - Review Atlassian’s supported domain patterns requirements.

Visit our pricing page for more details, or learn more about using AI Chat with Salesforce. #### AI Chat navigation ##### Panel view When you’re in a conversation or on the homepage, you can access AI Chat anytime in the right panel. Click on the Chat icon while in collapsed view to open the right panel if you do not see it. ##### Expanded view You can open the expanded view by clicking AI Chat in the left navigation menu or selecting Expand from the panel view. Click Collapse to return to the conversation you were viewing. #### Manage AI Chat settings for meetings Go to Integrations > Meetings to manage all of your AI Chat settings for meetings. Control which meetings AI Chat can access. #### FAQs #### Why can’t I see my previous chat history? Not all chat and conversation history from AI Chat 1.0 was migrated to AI Chat 2.0, and migrated content from AI Chat 1.0 will not appear in the session dropdown. #### What is the 2.0 experience of AI Chat? AI Chat 2.0 is the latest version of Otter’s AI Chat experience, designed with improved performance and new capabilities. - Otter Web & Desktop: Available now ✅ - Otter Mobile (iOS & Android): Coming soon 🟠

In the mobile app, AI Chat will continue to use the previous 1.0 experience until the update is released. #### Why does AI Chat look different on desktop versus mobile versions of Otter? AI Chat may look different depending on the platform you’re using, as different versions are currently available: - Otter Web & Desktop: AI Chat 2.0 - Otter Mobile (iOS & Android): AI Chat 1.0

Some features, such as session history and a unified chat experience across conversations, channels, and folders, are only available in AI Chat 2.0 and are not yet supported on mobile. #### Why is AI Chat missing in my account? All accounts are opted in to AI Chat by default. If you do not see the option to use AI Chat in your account or workspace, please have your admin contact our Otter Support Team. #### Why are AI Chat connectors and integrations grayed out? AI Chat connectors and integrations are available only in Advanced mode. Switch from Express to Advanced mode to connect and toggle on connectors. Learn more about AI Chat modes. #### Can I opt out of AI Chat? Yes. Learn more about opting out of AI Chat. #### Why is the AI chat response unrelated to my question? Sometimes the AI chat may generate a response that doesn’t fully match your question. This can happen due to a phenomenon called AI hallucination. Read our Understanding hallucinations across Otter AI Chat article for more info.

Otter AI Chat voice commands and actions

Use voice commands with Otter AI Chat during your Zoom meetings. Learn more about setting up Otter AI Chat with voice. - Create an action item - Schedule a meeting - Stop or Pause/Resume the recording - Send an email - Learn more about how to use Otter

Create an action item

Create an action item directly in the Otter conversation. 🗣️ Voice Examples: - “Hey Otter, add an action item for [task description] for [person]” - “Hey Otter, create a task: [task details]” - “Hey Otter, note down [task details] as an action item”

The action item will appear shortly in the Otter conversation under the Summary tab. #### Schedule a meeting Create a Google Calendar link with pre-filled details. 🗣️ Voice Examples: - “Hey Otter, schedule a meeting next Wednesday at 10am in the morning with Gordan” - “Hey Otter, schedule a follow up for us to chat next week Tuesday at 9am”

Otter AI Chat will generate a calendar event. Click the link in the Otter conversation or Zoom chat window to pre-fill. Review the event and click Save to add it to your calendar. (image: calendar event.png) #### Stop or Pause/Resume the recording Stop, pause, and resume the meeting using voice chat. ##### Stop the recording 🗣️ Voice Examples: - “Hey Otter, stop recording” - “Hey Otter, end the recording now” - “Hey Otter, turn off the recording”

Pause and Resume the recording

🗣️ Voice Invocation Examples: - “Hey Otter, pause the recording” Recording paused - Type ‘resume’ in AI Chat to resume the recording

“Hey Otter, pause the recording for 10 minutes” - Recording paused - Recording will resume in 10 minutes - Type ‘resume’ in AI Chat to resume the recording anytime

Send an email

Use Otter AI Chat to send an email of the conversation. You can send a variety of information in the email: Summary, action items, generated insights, and more. - For users in the Workspace or known contacts, AI Chat automatically finds and populates the email. - If an email is not found for the user, you can provide the email directly to AI Chat through voice.

🗣️ Voice Examples: - “Hey Otter, draft an email for Reese Lou to send containing the action items from this meeting.” - “Hey Otter, send Lyla Fern a summary of this meeting via email.” - “Hey Otter, email juniper.patel@beaverly.ai a summary of this meeting via email.”

Otter will generate an email. Click the link in the Otter conversation or Zoom chat window to pre-fill an email draft. Review the email draft and click Send to send the email. (image: email.png) #### Learn more about how to use Otter You can ask AI Chat directly about how to use Otter, its functionality, and information about the product.

Otter AI Chat with voice

Otter AI Chat with Voice is your real-time AI Agent for Zoom calls, helping you ask questions, summarize and share discussion topics, create action items, schedule meetings, and more. Interact with AI Chat using text or voice chat to complete a variety of functions and capabilities. Integrated into your Zoom meetings, AI Chat is always available to assist. #### Set up voice for AI Chat AI Chat with voice requires Otter Notetaker to join your Zoom meetings. Manage your Notetaker settings. 4. Manage your voice chat settings in Account Settings > Meetings.

Settings you can control for voice chat

Voice Chat - This will allow meeting participants to use verbal commands and questions to prompt AI Chat during a meeting. Learn more about available commands and actions for AI Voice Chat.

Past meetings within recurring series - When using Voice Chat in a meeting that is part of a recurring series, AI Chat will be able to pull from past meetings in that series. Learn more about recurring meetings.

Meetings shared to public channels in your Workspace - Meetings that have been shared to a public Channel in your Workspace will be available for AI Chat to pull from.

Otter Help - Ask questions about using Otter, such as functionality or info about the product.

Admin controls for the Workspace

Admins can control the setting to enable or disable voice chat for members in the Workspace. 1. As an admin, navigate to Workspace > Manage Workspace > Settings. 2. Under Voice Chat, toggle the setting on or off. 3. When toggling the setting, the admin can apply it to all members or only members who have not changed the setting previously.

Note: There is no way to lock the voice chat setting for members. Members of a Workspace can still adjust their individual settings for their account.

Apply to all - will apply to all members and override individual account settings. 4. Apply to all but keep member settings - will apply only to member accounts that have not previously changed their individual voice chat setting in Account Settings > Meetings. 5. Click Save to confirm the setting.
