Channels Overview

Otter channels help teams stay organized by providing a central place to access shared meetings and transcripts. Whether a channel is private or public, it makes it easier for team members to find and review important meeting content in one location. With Otter AI Chat, you can privately ask questions, summarize discussions, and pull key insights from meetings to quickly find the information you need. #### Channel overview Creating a channel provides many benefits for you and your team: - Utilize AI Chat to ask questions across all of the conversations in the channel - Centralized location of shared recordings - Organize your projects, teams, and ideas - View live transcripts in real-time

Channel permissions

There are two types of channels you can create: - Public - All members in the workspace can access and join. Public channels will show as a suggested channel for workspace members to join. - Private - Only people invited can join the channel.

When creating a channel, you can set permissions. You can update permissions at any time after creating a channel. #### Creating a channel Create a channel to share conversations with your team members. 1. Click the plus button (+) or + New channel. 2. Name the channel, invite members, and set visibility. Click Create Channel when ready.

Note: All of these settings and members can be managed at any time. 3. The channel is created. You can begin sharing conversations and inviting more members to your channel. Learn more about sharing conversations with your channel.

Suggested channels

All public channels can be viewed by workspace members. Under the Suggested on the left side, click through the suggested channels to preview them. To join a channel, click Join Channel.  #### Direct Messages Direct Messages (DMs) are a form of Channels where they only reflect sharing between individual users. Direct Messages can automatically be created if you are sharing with one other individual and can be found on the left nav menu. DMs act the same as Channels, so you can update private/public status, invite more members to your DMs, favorite DMs, and everything else Channels can do. ➡️ Learn more about Direct Messages Note: If your DM contains more than one member, it will not show under the left nav menu. You will need to click into the Direct Messages page to find them. #### Managing your channel #### Favorite a channel Favorite a channel for easy access You can favorite any channel for easy access. 1. Click the star icon to favorite a channel. 2. The channel will show under Favorites now for easy access.

Rename a channel Rename your channel anytime

  1. Click on the member icons to open channel settings. 
  2. Under the Settings tab, click edit next to Channel Name. ****
  3. Type the new channel name and click Save Changes to confirm.

Invite more users to join Invite members in or outside your workspace to join a channel

  1. Click on Invite in the upper right corner of the channel. Enter one or more email addresses, separated by a comma or space, and then press Return or Enter. Click Invite. 

Note: When you invite members to a channel, they will gain access to all conversations within the channel and inherit the permissions granted to the channel for each conversation.

External members

Users who are not part of your workspace will appear as external users, regardless of whether they share the same email domain. #### Leave a channel Leave a channel anytime 1. Click on the profiles to open channel settings. **** 2. Under the Settings tab, click Leave Channel. You will be immediately removed from the channel.  ****

To rejoin the channel, have a member invite you. If it’s a public channel, you can rejoin anytime. #### Remove someone from a channel Channel owner and admins can remove members from a channel 1. Click on the profiles to open channel settings. **** 2. Under the Members tab, click the 3 dots next to the member you want to remove. Select Remove member. The member will be immediately removed.  ****

Delete a channel Channel owners or admins can delete a channel

Conversations are not deleted when a channel is deleted. They remain available in the conversation owner’s account, but must be reshared if other members need access. 1. Click on the profiles to open channel settings. **** 2. Under the Settings tab, select Delete channel for everyone. 3. Click Delete to confirm. The channel has been deleted, and any conversations shared with the channel will no longer be visible or accessible to members.

Warning: Deleting a channel is permanent and irreversible. No conversations are deleted.

Bulk select conversations Bulk manage conversations through sharing, exporting, or deleting

You can bulk select conversations to share, export, or delete in the channel. 1. Click on the profiles to open channel settings. **** 2. Under the Settings tab, select Bulk Select. 3. You can then bulk select conversations to share, export, or delete.

If you are unable to select a conversation, you may not have the permissions to share, export, or delete. Learn more about conversation permissions. #### Using AI Chat in channels You can use AI Chat to ask questions and prompts, or to generate content across conversations. All chat sessions are private. Members in the channel cannot see your chats. Learn more about using AI Chat. Click the Chat icon to open the right-side chat panel. You can immediately start querying, add more context, or open other chat sessions. #### FAQs #### Can I make an external user the channel owner? No. An external user cannot be made into a channel owner. To update the owner, make a member in your workspace the channel owner. #### Can I sort conversations in a channel? Channels cannot be sorted at this time. Conversations will be sorted by last shared. The most recent shared conversations will appear first, regardless of date recorded. #### Can I add more context while using AI Chat in a channel? Yes. You can add more context, such as additional channels, conversations, or folders, to your queries. Click @ or type # to add more context. Learn more about Otter AI Chat. #### Can there be more than one owner of a channel? No. A channel can only have one owner at this time. However, workspace admins who are members of the channel can manage certain settings, such as deleting the channel or removing members. #### As a workspace admin, can I manage or remove channel members? As a workspace admin, you can remove channel members. Any member can invite others to public channels or to channels they are already a member of. #### As a workspace admin, what if I deactivate a user who is the owner or member of a channel? What happens to the channel? Any conversations previously shared in the channel will still be accessible even after the user has been deactivated. Archiving of conversations still applies, depending on your plan. If there are conversations beyond the most recent 25 that are shared, they will still appear in the channel but will not be viewable or accessible. #### As a workspace admin, what if I remove a user who is the owner or member of a channel? What happens to the channel? The owner or member will still be part of the channel, just not the Workspace. They will be removed from the # General channel. Any conversations previously shared in the channel will still be accessible even after the user has been deactivated. Archiving of conversations still applies, depending on your plan. If there are conversations beyond the most recent 25 that are shared, they will still appear in the channel but will not be viewable or accessible. #### What happens if the channel owner deletes their account or has their account deleted by a workspace admin? The channel will still exist. The user’s conversations will be deleted and removed from the channel. All other existing conversations will continue. #### If I delete a channel, will it also delete the conversations? No. Deleting a channel will not delete the conversations. The conversations will continue to be in the user’s account. Deleting a channel will only remove the channel and access to its conversations. #### Can I delete the # General channel? You cannot delete the # General channel. This channel is generated automatically by Otter, and workspace members are added automatically. #### Can I change the # General channel to private? The # General channel cannot be set to private. All members joining the workspace are automatically added to the # General channel. You can remove members from the channel if needed.

Share a conversation

Sharing Otter conversations allows others to view the transcript, playback the conversation, and collaborate or interact with the conversation: edit, share, or export. You have full control over access to your conversations. You can revoke access to a conversation anytime. #### Permission sharing overview ##### Conversation permissions settings When you share a conversation with someone or a Channel, you can choose the permission level they have. - Collaborator:

Edit, view, playback, and export the transcript - Interact and ask Otter Chat questions - Edit, add, and view Takeaways - Share the conversation (if toggled on by the conversation owner)

Tip: Learn more aboutallowing Collaborators to share a conversation.

  • Can only view and playback conversation
  • Cannot edit, add, or view Takeaways
  • Export option is only available if toggled on by the transcript owner

Note: This export setting option also applies to public links as well as privately shared speeches.

When you create a link for your conversation, you can select who has access to the conversation. - Anyone in your workspace:

Users in your workspace will be able to access the conversation. - You can edit the permission to Collaborator or Viewer. - To review the users in your workspace, navigate to Workspace > Members.

  • Permission is defaulted to Viewer.

  • Anyone with the link can view and playback the transcript without signing in.

  • Anyone with the link can also see any highlights or inline images, but not see any Takeaways, nor add any new Takeaways.

  • Users who are signed in will be able to view the transcript, Takeaways, and Otter Chat messages, but not interact with the conversation.

  • Only users or Channels the conversation is shared with will be able to view the conversation through the generated link.

Set the default settings for the Collaborators and Viewers that you have shared the conversation with. - Collaborators can share the conversation and change sharing permissions

Allows collaborators of the conversation to share with other users - Change any of the sharing settings in the pop-up

  • Allow viewers to export the conversation including text formats, audio, and Takeaways

Otter can automatically share conversations through your calendar event recordings to ensure everyone has access to the conversation and enhance collaboration. Manage your calendar recording share settings. Learn more about managing your calendar share settings. Navigate to Account Settings > Meetings or click the settings gear icon on the Homepage Calendar to easily access your conversation-sharing settings. #### Share a conversation (with a user or Channel) 1. Navigate to the conversation you want to share. 2. Click the Share button in the upper right corner. 3. Click in the Add names, emails, or channels field. You can invite through one of these methods or a combination of them: 

Type the email of the user and click Enter/Return 4. Browser through the All, People, Channels, and Direct Message tabs 5. Search by the name 6. Add an entire Channel

Note: Sharing to a Channel means all members in the Channel, or members added later to the Channel, will have access to conversation.

  1. You can manage the permissions level before sharing.

Tip: You can set different permission levels for each user and Channel. Repeat the steps above for each individual user or Channel, or update the permission level for each one after you share in the pop-up. 2. Optionally, you can leave a message that will show in the email the user will receive.

Note: Avoid adding external URL links in your message. 3. Click Share when you’re finished. Users will receive a notification and an email that a conversation was shared with them. You can manage the permission and revoke access anytime.

  1. Navigate to My Conversations, the Folder, or Channel where you want to bulk share conversations.
  2. Click the checkbox next to the conversations you want to share to select them.
  3. Click Share at the top menu. Add the user(s) or Channel(s) you want to share with. Optionally, adjust the permission level or add a message, and click Share to confirm.

You can create a private or public link to allow other users to view your conversation. 1. Click the Restricted dropdown and select the permission level you want for your conversation.

Note: You can change this anytime and existing URLs will update with the permission. 2. Click the gear icon on the right to manage additional settings for sharing and export. 3. Once you have reviewed those settings and are ready, click the Copy link to generate the URL.

You can share a snippet of a specific portion within a conversation instead of the entire conversation. 1. Navigate to the conversation. 2. Click on the Transcript tab and find the sentence, paragraph, or section you want to create a snippet for.

Note: You cannot create a snippet from multiple sections of the conversation. The selected portion must be one highlighted text. 3. Highlight the text in the transcript. In the toolbar pop-up, click Share and select the Snippet icon to generate the link.

By default, only that portion of the text will show when navigating to that URL. - The conversation will always show ‘Note’ as the title regardless of what you have titled your conversation. - The portion will show the speaker of that portion. - Users can playback the audio of that portion.

Stop or change conversation sharing permissions

You have full control over sharing permissions for your conversations. Whether a conversation was shared manually, through auto-share, or a conversation link, you can change, update, or stop the sharing permissions of a conversation anytime. You can update the following settings: - Stop sharing a conversation with users or Channels - Change sharing permission for a user or Channel - Update Collaborator and Viewer permissions - Revoke or update conversation public link settings

Miscellaneous

Data security and privacy policies

Security at Otter is extremely important to us. You trust Otter to keep your data secure, and responsible custodianship of your data is our top priority. Learn about Otter’s Privacy & Securitypractices and policies and our FAQs for more information. - See our Terms of Service to review our contract with you and our security practices. - See our Privacy Policy for more information on the information we collect and how it may be used. - See our California Resident Privacy Notice for information on individuals residing in California and compliance with the California Consumer Privacy Act (CCPA). - See a list of subprocessors with access to certain customer data. - Otter uses AWS services for its data storage in the AWS region West, United States. Otter uses AWS S3 storage and enables AWS SSE (Server Side Encryption) on data (S3 buckets). It encrypts the key itself with a root key that it regularly rotates. Amazon S3 server-side encryption uses a 256-bit Advanced Encryption Standard (AES-256).

Workspace Admin Logs

Workspace Admin Logs help Business and Enterprise admins monitor activity across their workspace. Review events performed by both admins and members, including member management, workspace settings, security changes, recording policies, and other workspace-related actions. You can export the log as a CSV file for auditing, compliance, troubleshooting, and reporting. #### View and export workspace admin logs Go to Manage Workspace, then click Download Admin Logs in the upper-left corner to export your workspace admin logs as a CSV file. You can open the file in a spreadsheet application, such as Microsoft Excel or Google Sheets, to review activity, create reports, analyze trends, or maintain audit records. #### What activity is logged? Workspace Admin Logs record workspace-related activity performed by both admins and members. This includes actions that affect your workspace, such as member management, workspace settings, security policies, and other shared resources. Individual account activity, such as changes to a user’s personal account settings, is not included. ##### Workspace activities Activities performed within your workspace, including: - Adding, removing, deactivating, and reactivating members - Changing member roles, including assigning or transferring the Owner role - Accepting or declining workspace join requests - Updating workspace settings and security preferences - Updating the workspace subscription - Canceling the workspace subscription - Deleting member accounts

Security & authentication
  • Turn Two-Factor Authentication (2FA) on or off
Workspace settings
  • Turn Social Sharing on or off
  • Enable or disable pre-recording email notifications
  • Enable or disable the Prevent guests and viewers from selecting and copying text setting

Enterprise-specific logged activities ✨

Enterprise workspaces may include additional admin log events depending on the Enterprise features and admin controls enabled for your organization. For example, actions related to recording policies, Notetaker management, SSO settings, and other Enterprise-specific controls may appear in your logs. #### FAQs #### Why is the Download Admin Logs option missing? Only workspace admins can download workspace admin logs. If you’re an admin and the Download Admin Logs option is still unavailable, verify that your workspace is on the Business or Enterprise plan, as the feature isn’t available on lower-tier plans. If you continue to run into issues, contact our Otter support team. #### Can members view or download workspace admin logs? No. Only workspace admins with the appropriate permissions can view and download workspace admin logs. #### What information is included in a workspace admin log? Each log entry records the action performed, who performed it, and when it occurred. Depending on the event, additional details may be included, such as the affected member, workspace setting, or policy that changed. #### Do workspace admin logs include member activity? Yes. Workspace admin logs record workspace-related actions performed by both admins and members. For example, if a member invites another member (when permitted), that activity is recorded in the log. #### Are personal account changes included in workspace admin logs? No. Workspace Admin Logs only record actions that affect the workspace. Changes made to an individual’s personal account or profile are not included. #### Why don’t I see a specific action in my workspace admin logs? Not every action is recorded. The events available in workspace admin logs depend on your subscription plan, enabled features, workspace configuration, and the type of activity performed. If you have a specific question about an action or event, please contact our Otter support team. #### Do Enterprise workspaces record additional events? Yes. Enterprise workspaces may record additional events based on the Enterprise features and admin controls enabled for the workspace. This can include actions related to security policies, recording controls, directory synchronization, Notetaker management, and other Enterprise-specific features. #### What other workspace activity can I track in Otter? In addition to workspace admin logs, admins can use Workspace Analytics to monitor workspace usage and member activity. Analytics provide insights into metrics such as active members, conversations, AI Chats, recording activity, and other workspace usage trends.
